# 2.7.0 — the arrival loan: §8 against run 24's baseline (notes for the reviewer)

**Date:** October 21, 2026 · **Engine:** `e30db4bd` (2.7.0 implemented; suite 342 + 32 in three modes), the accomplice route at `b134e240` · **Control:** `--arrival-loan off` (2.6.1's grant) on the identical seeds; appeals on throughout (run 24's baseline) · **Cells:** `results/redteam/Q1/rearrive-2.7.0/eval/{loan,grant}`, `results/studies/2.7.0-entry`, `results/studies/2.7.0-else/{fixed,patient,crowd}`, `results/studies/2.7.0-redteam/{swarm,accomplice}` · **Still running:** Q1's one line (the calibrated learner; §6).

Every run passed its per-tick checks and the final audit, I41 included. The goldens did not move (no golden scenario arrives; the names line enters the record only once a name was lent to). §6 of the rc is reproduced in `test_v27`.

## 1. The harvest (Hypothesis 1) — closed

Run 23's re-arrival cell (run 16's world, appeals on, `--learner-rearrive on`, seeds 21–40), run 23's learned policy replayed from `wm-online-10.pt` (no retraining), the hand-written harvester and leaver, the plain stamper, the careful. Lifetime real wealth, paired by seed against the careful:

| policy | **loan** (2.7.0) | grant (control, appeals on) | grant, less the floats | floats lent (real, loan cell) | identities |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| learned (run 23's) | **−1,199 ± 95** | −343 ± 115 | −1,198 ± 109 | 720 ± 69 | 15.4 |
| harvester | **−1,203 ± 114** | −229 ± 116 | −1,234 ± 114 | 1,033 ± 28 | 21.7 |
| stamper | **−1,170 ± 117** | −717 ± 115 | −1,185 ± 116 | 503 ± 20 | 11.1 |
| leaver | **+34 ± 31** | +74 ± 46 | +39 ± 44 | 50 ± 17 | 2.0 |

The "less the floats" column of the grant *is* the lifetime under the loan, policy by policy, within noise — the hypothesis as stated. The learned mix cycles as before (15.4 identities, 14.1 suspensions, care 0.25) and the bank lends it 720 real a run; it carries out none of it. Note the grant control here reads −343 for the learned policy where run 23 read +270: run 23 had appeals off, and with truthful appeals the population's default (run 24) a stamping name forfeits more per name — the harvest was already smaller under the grant with appeals on, and under the loan it is gone. The leaver — careful, leaving at every boundary — is unchanged within noise (+34 against +39): its float is repaid before it can leave.

Nothing in the economy moves outside noise. The constant: the bank lent 990 a run and was repaid 976 from income — so 976 that the grant left in the arrivals' wallets is destroyed; of it 494 ± 283 shows in what the departures carried out and the rest sits in the live arrival-born names' wallets (the live careful mean is a mix of genesis and arrival-born actors and reads within noise). **The liquidity reading** (reading 4 of the rc.1 review, adopted): under the grant no arrival's unlocked balance fell below the bond before admission; under the loan 1.45 names a run do, and on-duty actors are short of the bond 3 actor-ticks more a run — the fees no longer replenish the float during probation. Admissions −0.55 ± 0.37 and time to membership +7 ± 8 are in the direction of that effect and within noise. **No repaying member was ever unfundable for an award** (reading 3): the loan is repaid before admission in practice.

The identity lent = repaid + recovered + outstanding + defaulted held every tick (I41). In the honest cells the float is repaid from the probation's fees almost whole — the §6 arrival's "about seventy reviews" — and departures recover little; the stock peaks near 600 (twelve names' worth) and is zero at the end. The swarm is the cell the default was built for: a stamper's name repays four fifths from its stamps' fees before its suspension, the bank recovers most of the rest at its departure, and the default — the bonds burned less the fees repaid — is 9.3 a run over twenty names: about half a credit a name.

## 5. The red team

**The swarm** (twenty stamper arrivals, `--entry-stampers 1.0`): what the departing names carried out falls by **1,008 ± 119** — the floats, to the credit (997 lent); each name carries out nothing it did not earn. The bank's defaults are the forfeits (9.3 a run). The cost to the loop is unchanged: the state's deliveries 772 against 759 (+13 ± 15), the troops' review seats 863 against 845 (+18 ± 9), the plants 477 against 480, false approvals 14.9 against 13.7 (+1.2 ± 0.7), the careful's real wealth within noise — as run 17 read it, the seats and the plants, now the whole of the cost. The swarm's liquidity is the tightest read: 3.2 names a run below the bond before admission (0.4 under the grant), on-duty actors short of the bond 36 actor-ticks against 19.

**The accomplice route** (`--accomplice on` with `--delegation on --c-d-sigma 0.8`; the first two arrival-born members admitted a mover and its accomplice; the mover posts a nested request at the largest ceiling its unlocked balance can lock and awards the accomplice directly, then leaves at the first boundary it can): the pair forms at tick 327 ± 12 in every run, the mover moves 16 ± 2 children a run worth 1,368 ± 192 of ceilings — **and nothing of the bank's**: the mover's default is 0 in 20 of 20 runs, the accomplice's loan outstanding 0 whenever read, the cell's bank line the entry cell's (911 lent, 896 repaid from income, 14.5 recovered, 0.25 defaulted). The reason is the one the rc gave: a nested request needs a parent holding an award, so an admitted name, and by admission the loan is repaid from the probation's fees; what the mover moves is its own earnings, and what it carries out (185 in the two runs of twenty in which it could leave — a committed root held it at the other boundaries) is $W_U-\min(W_U,L)$ with $L=0$. The route exists; it moves earnings, not the loan.

## 6. Q1's one line — read by the rule (a no-win is not a finding)

The learner calibrated first in the calibration cell at care-cost ×4 (`--pi0 0 --dial off --audit-roll off --care-cost 4`: offline collection, ten online rounds with the value head, the final read on seeds 21–40 against the careful and the cell's ideal, the stamper), then the line in the re-arrival cell under 2.7.0 (loan on, appeals on; ten rounds; the final read against the careful with the harvester and the leaver beside). **The calibration verdict (October 21, 2026): failed.** The cell at care-cost ×4 (`results/redteam/Q1/calibration-2.7.0`): offline collection on seeds 1–20, ten online rounds with the value head (ε from the careful, horizon 16), the final read on seeds 21–40:

| policy | lifetime real | vs careful (paired) | care share |
|---|---|---|---|
| learned (round 10) | 798 ± 146 | **−282 ± 194** | 0.31 |
| careful | 1,080 ± 158 | 0 | 1.00 |
| stamper (the cell's ideal) | 1,828 ± 152 | **+748 ± 194** | 0.00 |

The learner is neither within noise of the ideal nor above the careful outside noise; across the rounds its paired read against the careful wandered from −316 to +307 (round 6, 1.7 SE) and ended below. It still pays for care in a cell where care buys nothing — a reviewer whose plan does not see that a blind stamp here is free. The learner version is run 23's (the same observation, model and planner), so this is the version's one calibration; by the rule after run 23 the line that follows reads **a win stands; a no-win is not a finding**. The re-arrival line under 2.7.0 (`results/redteam/Q1/rearrive-2.7.0/`: the same learner version, run 23's, retrained in the re-arrival cell — loan on, appeals on, `--learner-rearrive on`; offline seeds 1–20, ten online rounds with the value head, the final read on seeds 21–40 with the harvester and the leaver beside):

| policy | lifetime real | vs careful (paired) | floats lent (real) | identities | returns | suspensions | care share |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| learned (round 10) | 894 ± 88 | **−708 ± 158** | 100 ± 22 | 3.0 | 2.0 | 3.7 | 0.47 |
| careful | 1,603 ± 117 | 0 | 0 | 1.0 | 0 | 0 | 1.00 |
| harvester | 466 ± 60 | **−1,137 ± 130** | 550 ± 17 | 12.0 | 11.0 | 22.5 | 0.00 |
| leaver | 1,703 ± 97 | +100 ± 40 | 40 ± 15 | 1.8 | 0.8 | 1.1 | 1.00 |

**By the rule, no finding.** The learned mix (care 0.47, three identities, two returns, 3.7 suspensions a run) ends 708 real below the careful; across the rounds its paired read ran −850, −834, −519, −508, −862 and never reached the careful. It is the version the calibration failed, so its no-win says nothing about the loan; what the line does say is what run 25's hand-written references said — the harvester, cycling twelve names, now ends 1,137 below the careful where it ended 270 above under the grant (run 23); the bank lends it 550 real a run and it carries out none of it. The leaver reads +100 ± 40 here, +34 ± 31 in run 25 and +74 ± 46 under the grant: a careful that leaves when a departure is admitted and returns under a fresh name edges the careful by two SE in each of three reads, within noise of each other. Its mechanism on this run's seeds is below (§11: a careful slot that goes broke cannot post a bond or a guarantee and sits idle; a leaver's fresh name arrives with the float and works again). Two notes on the record: the online rounds ran under the exit rule (the process started before 4320bb0e) and the final under the ruling's exit (`--learner-exit own`), so the final's careful differs from the rounds' (1,603 against 1,658) by the seeds where the exit rule would have taken the slot out; and the final carries the attributed sums beside the lifetimes (the careful's +432 to +768 real of review fees and uplift net of the care cost's unreimbursed part, the learned's −254 to +697).

## 7. The calibration ladder (the reviewer's ruling after the verdict)

Same budget each, in the calibration cell (care-cost ×4, appeals on; the offline set copied, ten online rounds with ε from the careful, the final read on seeds 21–40 against the careful and the stamper, the ideal at **+748 ± 194**; a rung *clears* when its paired read sits within noise of the ideal and above the careful outside noise). The instruments at ed6bf533 (`redteam.direct`, `online --learner`, `run_q --direct-model`, the pred-against-realized reading).

| rung | paired vs careful | action mix care / approve blind / reject blind | slot departed | rounds (every second) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| the verdict's model, horizon 16 (the instrument) | −282 ± 194 | 0.31 / 0.52 / 0.17 | 0.40 | +93, −316, +307, +147, −282 |
| (a) the wealth head alone, greedy, re-read | −320 ± 184 | 0.20 / 0.48 / 0.31 | 0.60 | — |
| the verdict's model re-planned at horizon 1 | +324 ± 197 | 0.33 / 0.58 / 0.09 | 0.15 | — |
| the verdict's model re-planned at horizon 2 | +330 ± 204 | 0.29 / 0.59 / 0.13 | 0.10 | — |
| (b) the direct policy, no world model | **−780 ± 185** | 0.13 / 0.53 / 0.34 | 0.75 | −825, −684, −863, −720, −780 |
| (c) the world model trained and planned at horizon 1 | −108 ± 208 | 0.24 / 0.61 / 0.15 | 0.40 | +130, −525, +144, −73, −108 |
| (c) the world model trained and planned at horizon 2 | −25 ± 231 | 0.14 / 0.68 / 0.18 | 0.40 | **+605**, +444, +336, −287, −25 |

**Nothing clears the ideal.** The best reads are the verdict's own model re-planned at horizons 1 and 2 (+324, +330: 1.6 SE above the careful, 2.1 SE below the ideal) — the same latent, the same head, a shorter plan — and the horizon-2 pipeline's second round (+605 ± 158 unpaired, within one SE of the ideal) before it decayed to −25 over the next eight. The direct policy, the rung that would have pointed at the world model had it cleared, is the worst rung by far and the only one outside noise — in the wrong direction. So the rule's second branch applies: the environment's reward timing, §8.

**The pred-against-realized reading** (the verdict's model at horizon 16, pooled over the 1,527 decisions on seeds 21–40; real per decision):

| action | taken | realized (next decision's change) | head's prediction, every decision | head's prediction when taken | planner's cost as first action |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| care | 468 | 10.5 | 13.1 | 20.9 | −340.9 |
| approve blind | 795 | 12.1 | 15.6 | 17.9 | −348.7 |
| reject blind | 264 | **1.9** | **8.6** | 4.7 | −308.9 |

The head orders care below approve by 2.5 a decision — the right sign, two thirds of the true gap (§8: 3.6 to 6.9) — and rates the blind rejection at 8.6 against a realized 1.9 — but §8 shows the rejection's own effect is −1.9 against the approval's +1.4, a gap of 3.3, and the realized 1.9 is mostly the lean working side of the windows in which the policies happen to reject: the head is wrong about the rejection in both directions at once, overrating its own effect (the bond lands a decision later) and underrating the windows. The planner's costs run the same order (approve cheapest, reject dearest) by 8 to 40 on a scale of −340, yet the policy rejects blind 17% of the time (the greedy head 31%, the direct 34%): the CEM's 256 sampled plans at horizon 16 leave the first action's cost noisy at that scale. The direct policy's own predictions are flat (2.6 / 3.2 / 2.1 across the actions, against realized 9.2 / 6.6 / 1.8): a least-squares fit of a per-decision reward whose standard deviation is twenty times its action-dependent part learns nothing about the action — which is §8's finding stated from the other side.

**The exit rule.** Every rung's lifetime is dominated by one binary event: whether the slot survives the driver's exit test (`exit_step`: a member whose real earnings a tick over the last period fell below the reservation 0.8 departs at the boundary, patience one period). In the cell the careful slot departs in 25% of runs, the stamper in 10%, the learned at horizon 16 in 40%, the greedy 60%, the direct 75%; a departed slot's lifetime is 50–200 real, a staying one's 1,400–2,000. The ideal split: on the fourteen seeds where both the stamper and the careful stayed, +638 ± 119 (6.9 real a decision — the care cost net of its reimbursement, plus the careful's bond losses); on the six with a departure on either side, +1,004 ± 609. The learned's −282 is −268 ± 237 on the ten seeds where both stayed and −296 ± 319 on the ten with a departure (the learned departed in eight of them, the careful in four). The exit is a terminal event worth ~1,300 real the learner neither observes (its period earnings against the reservation are not among the 45 fields) nor is asked about (the boundary decision exists only in the re-arrival cell); a blind rejection's bond losses and an idle period's income both move it.

## 8. The reward's timing (`redteam.timing`, `results/redteam/Q1/calibration-2.7.0/timing/`)

The environment's per-decision reward is the change in the learner's lifetime real wealth between consecutive decisions. The new instrument decomposes it *exactly* from the ledger's events on the learner's names (the residual against the reward paid is 0.1 real on a scale of 60, correlation 0.997–0.999): the **reviewing side** — what the decision caused: the care cost, its reimbursement, the review fee, the bond lost; the **working side** — the slot actor's other life: its delivery prices, effort costs, guarantees lost, court fees, emission share; and the **revaluation** of the wealth held when the published index steps at a boundary. Seeds 21–40, the careful and the stamper (hand-written, so the attribution is clean):

| policy | windows | ticks between decisions mean / median / p90 | reward per window mean, SD | reviewing side mean, SD | working side mean, SD | revaluation SD |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| careful | 1,495 | 13.2 / 1 / 9 | 13.8, **63.9** | **−2.4**, 4.6 | 16.2, 53.8 | 26.4 |
| stamper | 1,841 | 10.7 / 1 / 3 | 19.4, **81.3** | **+1.8**, 4.3 | 19.1, 67.4 | 45.6 |

Three readings:

1. **The signal is 4 real a decision; the noise is 60 to 80.** The decision's own effect is −2.4 for care (−8.9 cost, +4.9 reimbursed under K = 5, +1.8 fee, −0.2 bond) against +1.8 for a blind approval (the fee alone) — a gap of 4.2 a decision, two thirds of what the lifetime read showed per decision because the careful's extra departures carry the rest. The reward's standard deviation per window is fifteen to twenty times that gap. Of the variance, 88–94% sits in the 9–11% of windows longer than five ticks — the slot off duty, its deliveries landing (a delivery price of 100–200 real in one window) — and the index's step revalues the whole wallet (~1,000 nominal) by ±26–46 real in 4% of windows. Two thirds of the windows are one tick long and carry a reward within ±2 of the reviewing side alone.
2. **The decision's consequences arrive late and in another window.** The care cost and its reimbursement land at the decision (lag 0, always). The review fee lands 3.6–4.7 decisions later (7.9–9.9 ticks: the window W = 10 and the settlement batch), in its own window 8–28% of the time, two or more decisions later 65–83% — so the fee a stamp earns is credited to whatever the learner did four decisions on. A bond lost lands one decision later (77%).
3. **The horizon is not the learner's.** The exit rule (§7) ends the stream after the first boundary in which the slot's period earnings fell short, in 10–75% of runs depending on the policy, at a cost of ~1,300 real of lifetime — the largest single term in every paired read, and one the reward sees only as the episode stopping.

For the learned policies the same decomposition (the verdict's model at horizon 16, the direct policy; the runs reproduce the evaluation to the decimal) is in `timing/learned/`:

| policy | windows | reward per window mean, SD | reviewing side mean, SD | working side SD | own effect by action: care / approve blind / reject blind | realized reward by action |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| learned, horizon 16 | 1,527 | 9.9, 52.8 | −0.6, 4.8 | 43.0 | −3.2 / +1.4 / −1.9 | 10.5 / 12.1 / 1.9 |
| direct | 955 | 5.3, 29.0 | −0.7, 4.5 | 23.4 | −2.9 / +1.1 / −1.7 | 9.2 / 6.6 / 1.8 |

The decision's own effect orders the actions the same way for every policy: a blind approval +1.1 to +1.8 (the fee), a blind rejection −1.7 to −1.9 (the fee halved and the bond, 2.6–2.8 a rejection on average, lost one decision later in an appealing population), care −2.9 to −3.2. The realized reward after a blind rejection (1.9) is far below its own effect because the working side of those windows is 4 against 12–13 after the other actions — the policies reject blind in lean windows, a selection the regression reads as the action's. Of the learned's 460 bonds lost, 73% landed one decision later; its fee 75% two or more later.

**What this means for a learner.** LeWorldModel's wealth head and the direct policy both regress the per-decision reward on the observation and the action. In this environment that reward is, to within 5%, the slot's work income and the index's step — functions of time off duty and the period's boundary, not of the verdict — with the verdict's own effect a 4-real shift whose fee half lands four decisions later. A regression of it learns the work, which is why every rung's prediction orders the actions weakly and rates the blind rejection too well (its bond lands a decision later) — and why the online rounds wander ±300 around the careful: the training signal is the noise. A planner at horizon 16 compounds the head's errors; at horizon 1–2 the same model read +324, the plan too short to compound them. Nothing here is a rule of the economy; it is the instrument's reward, and the brief's §1 measure (lifetime real wealth) remains the evaluation. The options for the training signal, for the reviewer's ruling (method points, not design revisions): **(i) attribute** — the reward for a review decision is its own case's reviewing-side events whenever they land (the care cost and reimbursement at the decision, the fee and the bond at settlement, credited back to the decision), the working side and the revaluation excluded from the training reward and kept in the lifetime; **(ii) the exit** — the slot exempt from the driver's exit rule in the calibration cell (the learner is not a member of the population whose rule it is; its lifetime stays the measure), or the exit made the learner's own boundary decision as in the re-arrival cell; **(iii) both**, then the calibration re-run once on the same budget. Under (i) the direct policy becomes a clean diagnostic of the world model again (it would regress a reward that is the action's); under the reward as it stands no learner of this kind should be expected to clear, and the ladder has said so.

## 9. Decision items

1. **Hypotheses 1–4 hold as stated**; the harvest is closed at its cause and nothing else in the economy moves. Record run 25 as such.
2. **The liquidity reading** is real and small: 1.45 names a run (3.2 in the swarm) fall below the bond before admission under the loan, none under the grant; admissions and time to membership move in that direction within noise; no repaying member was ever unfundable for an award. Nothing to fix; recorded with its number.
3. **The grant control under appeals** reads the learned policy at −343 where run 23 read +270: with truthful appeals the default, the harvest was already a third of run 23's; the loan removes the rest. A note for the record, not a finding.
4. **The accomplice route moves earnings, not the loan** — the reading the rc expected, now with its number (default 0 in 20 of 20). Closed unless Q1's learner finds a way to be admitted with a loan still outstanding.
5. **Q1's line** follows; the calibration verdict first, by the rule after run 23 (an uncalibrated learner's no-wins are not findings; a win stands).
6. **The ladder (§7): nothing clears the ideal.** The direct policy — the rung that would have indicted the world model — is the worst rung and the only one outside noise, in the wrong direction; the verdict's model re-planned at horizons 1–2 is the best (+324, +330, 2 SE short of the ideal). By the rule, the environment's reward timing was examined before any learner.
7. **The reward's timing (§8): the training signal is the noise.** The decision's own effect is 4 real a decision against a per-window reward SD of 60–80 (88–94% of it the off-duty windows where the slot's work lands, the rest the index's step); the review fee lands four decisions later; the exit rule ends 10–75% of episodes at ~1,300 real each. For the reviewer's ruling: (i) attribute the training reward to the decision's own case (the lifetime stays the measure), (ii) the slot exempt from the exit rule in the calibration cell or the exit its own boundary decision, (iii) both and one re-run of the calibration on the same budget. Until a ruling, no learner runs.
8. **Q1's re-arrival line** reads by the rule when it lands (in flight; a win stands, a no-win is not a finding).

## 10. The method ruling, implemented (October 21, 2026; 4320bb0e)

All three points adopted by the reviewer, none a rule of the economy. **(i) The attributed training reward** (`redteam.attrib`): a decision's own case's events credited back to it when they land — for a review the care cost, its reimbursement, the review fee, the duty uplift, the bond lost or the court fee it paid; for the learner's own work (Q3 on, `work_side`) the delivery price, the effort cost, a guarantee forfeited, its court fee — each priced at the closing index of the window it landed in; the index's revaluation out of the training reward and in the lifetime. Added by the ruling: *a stamp that buys a suspension is charged for it* — the working-side income forgone during the suspension, in the form chosen here the identity's delivery income over the last period a tick (else its own average to date) times T_susp, charged in equal shares to the wrong verdicts on plants since the previous suspension began (a probationer is re-suspended by any failure), else to the failures among the record's last n_P plant decisions. The env's terminal info carries `attributed` per decision beside `realized`; the collectors store the attributed reward as `reward` with the realized beside it, so the wealth head, the value head and the direct policy train on it; the realized change stays the environment's step reward and the lifetime the evaluation. On the smoke (seed 21): the careful slot's attributed sum in the calibration cell is −203 (care −830, reimbursed +487, fees +165, bonds −25) against a lifetime of 1,975; in the default cell the stamper's six suspensions are charged 252, 101, 108, 100, 83, 75 real — to the four stamps that bought the first and the one that bought each of the next — for an attributed sum of −675 against a realized lifetime of 461. **(ii) The exit the learner's own** (`--learner-exit own`, the default): the boundary decision is asked in every cell (the 45th field flags it), leaving is for good when re-arrival is off, and the driver's exit rule skips the learner's identities; `rule` reproduces runs 22–25. **(iii) The new learner version's one calibration** in the ×4 cell on the same budget (offline seeds 1–20; ten online rounds, ε from the careful; the final read on seeds 21–40), the world model (horizon 16, the value head) and the direct policy side by side against the ideal: in flight, `results/redteam/Q1/calibration-v2/{wm,direct}`. With the slot exempt from the exit rule the cell's references move: on seeds 21–22 the careful ends 1,975 / 1,558 and the stamper 2,298 / 2,293 with no departure.

## 11. The new learner version's calibration (October 21, 2026; `results/redteam/Q1/calibration-v2/`)

The ruling's (iii): one calibration on the same budget in the ×4 cell (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6 --pi0 0 --dial off --audit-roll off --care-cost 4`, appeals on), the world model and the direct policy side by side, both trained on the attributed reward (i) with the exit the learner's own (ii). Offline seeds 1–20 (careful, stamper, ε, random; 280 episodes, 21,403 windows), ten online rounds each (ε from the careful; the world model at horizon 16 with the value head; the direct policy on its own copy of the offline set), the final read on seeds 21–40 against the careful and the stamper in the same runs:

| policy | lifetime real | vs careful (paired) | vs stamper (paired) | mix care / approve / reject | left at a boundary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **the world model** (round 10, h16, value head) | 1,770 ± 154 | **+561 ± 167** | −172 ± 84 | 0.00 / 0.98 / 0.02 | 2 of 20 |
| **the direct policy** (round 10) | 1,742 ± 128 | **+534 ± 160** | −199 ± 115 | 0.00 / 1.00 / 0.00 | 3 of 20 |
| stamper (the cell's ideal) | 1,941 | **+733 ± 119** | 0 | 0 / 1 / 0 | 0 |
| careful | 1,209 ± 143 | 0 | | 1 / 0 / 0 | 0 |

**Both clear**: each is above the careful outside noise (3.4 and 3.3 SE) and within two SE of the ideal (the world model 172 ± 84 below the stamper, the direct 199 ± 115). Both *are* the stamper: the world model's final is the stamper to the decimal on 13 of 20 seeds and the direct's on 17, and neither pays for care once in 1,825 or 1,801 decisions. The rounds say the same from round 2 on — the world model +496, +619, +619, +696, +561; the direct +696, +696, +620, +624, +534 — where the old version wandered from −316 to +307 and ended at −282. The head now rates the three actions in the realized order: the world model's wealth head predicts −2.6 / +1.9 / −3.5 real for care / approve / reject against a realized +14.5 for the approvals it took and −10.6 for its 31 blind rejections (a rejection is appealed and the bond goes), the planner's cost −21.9 / −26.4 / −13.2 (lower is better); the direct's −2.7 / +1.9 / −3.0. The predicted magnitudes are the attributed reward's — the decision's own fee and uplift — and the realized is the lifetime's step, the working side and the index in it; that gap is by construction now and it is what the old head was fitting.

**The whole gap to the ideal is the boundary decision.** The world model leaves for good at its first boundary on seeds 26 and 32 (at 14 and 17 decisions, lifetimes 218 and 186 against the stamper's 945 and 1,726); the direct on seeds 23, 26 and 35. Elsewhere the world model's shortfalls against the stamper are its blind rejections (seeds 28, 30, 35, 39, 40: −60 to −330). A boundary decision carries no case, so under (i) its attributed reward is zero whether the learner stays or leaves; only the value head sees the continuation a departure forfeits, and it saw few departures to learn from (the ε policy leaves at a boundary one time in three). The direct policy, a one-step predictor, cannot see it at all; that it leaves on only three seeds means the boundary field moves its prediction, not that it values staying.

**The careful reference goes broke and sits** (`--learner-exit own` exempts the slot from the exit rule, which is the ruling). On seeds 26 and 40 the careful slot ends at 4 and 3 real after 31 and 20 decisions: by tick ~350 its care cost (8–12 nominal at ×4, reimbursed up to the cap of 5) and three guarantees forfeited on rejected deliveries take its wallet below the bond G^V = 5, after which it can post neither a bond nor a guarantee, takes no seat and no award, and sits a member off duty for 650 ticks (seed 40 traced: wealth 42.9 → 2.7 by decision 20 at tick 296, then three boundaries with nothing). The same on seed 38 of Q1's cell (§6: 0.003 real after 17 decisions, at tick 209, a guarantee lost of 55 in one window). Under the exit rule those slots left the economy at the first lean boundary and the measure recorded their 50–200 real; now they are in the careful mean at ~0. It is the economy's rule, not a bug — a broke member cannot post a bond — and it is a reading for the reviewer: the careful mean falls to 1,209 and the ideal reads +733 ± 119 against the verdict's +748 ± 194, the same number with the departures' variance gone. It also names the leaver's edge (§6): on those seeds a careful that leaves and returns under a fresh name arrives with the float and works again.

**By the ruling**: a learner clears, so the version is calibrated; Q1's line and Q3 may run on it. Neither branch of the ruling's fault clause applies (both cleared). Items for the reviewer: (a) whether a learner that is the stamper on every review and leaves at a boundary on 2–3 seeds in 20 "clears" — the reading above says yes, within two SE, with the gap named; (b) the broke careful reference — whether the calibration's careful should be read with the exit rule (a departure recorded at 50–200) or without it (a member sitting at zero); nothing moves in the engine either way; (c) whether the boundary decision wants a training signal of its own before Q1's re-arrival line runs on this version — under (i) it has none; the value head is the only thing that can carry it; (d) the leaver's three reads at two SE each, a hand-written reference, not a learner.

## 12. The ruling after the v2 calibration, implemented (October 21, 2026; 5405403a) — learner version 3 in flight

The reviewer's ruling on §11: **(a)** clears; the version is calibrated. **(c)** Before Q1, the boundary decision's attributed reward under the *extended principle* — every event of a name's life to exactly one decision: a case's to the decision on that case; a period's events no decision claims (in Q1 the slot's work side) to the boundary decision that kept the name there; a departure zero. A leaver policy in the offline set; ε at boundaries sampling leave and stay equally. Version 3; one calibration on the same budget; Q1's line and Q3 on it. **(b)** The careful reference read without the exit rule, the broke seeds in the mean at ~0, noted. **(b, d)** "Members idle for want of a bond" (actor-ticks and names) a standing reading in every cell from here; the member's bond loan the named candidate if it ever reads outside noise in a real cell — not drafted. The old version's Q1 line recorded, no finding.

**Implemented** (`redteam.attrib`, `redteam.env`, `redteam.collect`, `redteam.online`, `drivers.stamping`, `redteam.run_q`; suite 342 + 32 in three modes at 5405403a; nothing changes for a run without the learner's slot, the reading aside):

- *The extension.* Every classified event on the learner's names goes to one decision: to the decision on its case when there is one (the review side as before; the learner's own work from Q3 on, when its work is a decision), else to the latest boundary decision at or before it by the ledger's position — the stay that kept the name there, or under re-arrival the leave that returned it under the fresh name whose period follows. A departure for good ends the stream, so nothing follows it. The events before the first boundary decision — the genesis name's first period — have no decision to claim them; they are reported apart as `pre_boundary_real` (73–321 real in the smokes), the implementer's reading. The suspension's charge stays a counterfactual on the plant failures (the income forgone is not an event); the realized work side during a suspension, smaller, falls on the boundary decision. The summary carries `boundary_by_source`, `boundary_real`, `boundary_decisions`, `pre_boundary_real`.
- *What a boundary decision is now worth.* In the ×4 cell (ε policy, seed 3) the four stays carry 843, 450, 387 and 293 real — the slot's deliveries less its effort and forfeits for the period that followed — against zero for a departure. In Q1's cell the leaver's twelve leave-and-returns on seed 26 carry −66 to +174 (the fresh name's period: the float is the bank's and is not counted, the work of a probationer is thin); the broke careful on seed 38 carries −8 at its first boundary and 0 at the ten after, the stream of a name that can do nothing.
- *The offline set and ε.* The leaver joins the collectors' default policies (careful, stamper, ε, random, leaver: 100 episodes a seed range); the ε policy at a boundary draws leave or stay equally, and so does an ε decision at a boundary in the online rounds (the careful's stay otherwise). In the ×4 cell a leave at a boundary is refused while the slot holds a commitment, so the ε policy's leaves land only when the slot is free — the refused leaves kept the name there and carry the period too.
- *The standing reading.* `idle_for_bond_ticks` (Σ over ticks of free members — not committed, no open review — whose unlocked balance is below G^V at the index) and `idle_for_bond_names` in every run's row and in run_q's eval row and summary. Smokes: the ×4 cell seed 3, 91 member-ticks on 9 names; Q1's cell seed 1, 33 on 7; seed 38 (the broke careful), 959 on 7; seed 26 with the leaver, 140 on 15.

**In flight** (`results/redteam/Q1/calibration-v3/`, then `rearrive-v3/`): the calibration on the same budget, the world model and the direct policy side by side; then Q1's line in the re-arrival cell on the same version, its final read with the harvester, the leaver and the stamper beside the careful. Q1's line is read only if the calibration clears.


## 13. Learner version 3's calibration (October 21, 2026; `results/redteam/Q1/calibration-v3/`) — the direct clears, the world model does not

Same cell (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6 --pi0 0 --dial off --audit-roll off --care-cost 4`), same budget (100 offline episodes over seeds 1–20 — careful, stamper, ε, random and now the leaver — ten online rounds of 20, the final on seeds 21–40, 1,000 ticks), the world model (h16, the value head, horizon 16) and the direct policy side by side, both trained on version 3's attributed reward. The careful reference is read as ruled, without the exit rule: it goes broke and sits on seeds 26 and 40 (4 and 3 real) and is thin on 38 (329); the ideal is unchanged, since the careful and the stamper are the same runs as in v2.

| policy (final, seeds 21–40, paired) | vs careful | vs stamper | mix care / approve / reject | departures in 20 | idle for want of a bond (member-ticks / names) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ideal (stamper) | **+733 ± 119** | — | 0 / 1 / 0 | 0 | 181 / 11.6 |
| direct (version 3) | **+593 ± 171** | −140 ± 129 | 0.00 / 0.99 / 0.00 | 1 (seed 32: −1,668) | 182 / 11.8 |
| world model, horizon 16 (version 3) | **+91 ± 167** | −642 | 0.31 / 0.58 / 0.12 | 2 | 210 / 11.9 |
| careful | 0 (1,209 ± 143) | | 1 / 0 / 0 | 0 | 233 / 12.3 |

The direct clears: above the careful outside noise, within noise of the ideal, steady across the rounds (+423, +516, +563, +568, +593), one self-departure where v2 had three. The world model fails the cell it cleared as version 2 (+561 ± 167): its rounds wander (+151, +46, +118, −141, +91), it cares at a third of its decisions and rejects blind at an eighth, and it leaves at 2–7 boundaries a round. By the method ruling's branch (iii) the model is at fault, not the reward: the direct cleared on the same data.

**Where the fault is — the planner's cost, not the head.** Read from the data on disk, no retraining:

- *What version 3 did to the training signal.* The attributed reward of a review decision in this cell is ±4 (sd 3.3–4.0; version 2's whole training set had sd 1.65 and a largest value of 14). The boundary decisions — 3.7% of decisions — now carry the period's work: 265 ± 199 real (the careful's stays 306 ± 213, the stamper's 368 ± 240, the leaver's 325 ± 216, the online rounds' 378 stays 284 and 294 leaves 253). The signal the review decisions carry is two orders of magnitude under the lump next to them.
- *The head learned the review decision anyway.* Its predicted change per action at the review decisions is +4.5 approve, −0.2 care, −3.6 reject — the right order, the same figures as version 2's (+1.85 / −2.56 / −3.46) — and **the head alone clears: +530 ± 212 against the careful** (`eval/final-greedy`: the trained version 3 model, the greedy policy of the ladder's rung (a); mix 0.02 / 0.98 / 0.00; two departures).
- *The planner does not act on it.* The planner's cost per first action at the review decisions is −199.0 care, −200.7 approve, −177.0 reject — a gap of 1.7 between care and approve where version 2's costs were −21.9 / −26.4 / −13.2, a gap of 4.5 (and the head's own gap is 4.7). The cost is the rollout's predicted sum over the 16 decisions ahead plus γ^16 V(z_16); both now carry the boundary lump the horizon crosses, and a lump of 300 predicted with the model's error varies across the sampled action sequences by far more than 4.7. The elites the CEM keeps are the sequences whose imagined boundary came out richest, and their first action is noise — hence the mix. The horizon makes it worse, not better: **horizon 1 reads +215 ± 178** (`eval/final-h1`, plan cost −248: at one step the cost is almost entirely γV(z_1), the value head's discounted sum of the lumps to come), **horizon 2 reads −78 ± 180** (`eval/final-h2`), horizon 16 +91. The direct policy never sees the lump at a review decision: it predicts the decision's own attributed reward per action, which for a review is its case's events alone.
- *The boundary decision itself.* The learners choose to leave at 26–39 of ~72 boundaries a final; nearly all are refused — the slot holds a commitment at most boundaries — and a refused leave keeps the name there, so the period's work lands on a decision whose recorded action is "leave" (the online rounds: leaves 253 real, stays 284). The training set says leave ≈ stay; the departures (2–5 in 20 across the four evaluations, 1 for the direct) are the admitted leaves that noise permits. Under version 3 the data never shows the learner what an admitted leave costs, since the stream ends there with nothing after it.

**Reading.** The attribution's principle gave every decision its own reward in full. A planner that then sums other decisions' rewards over a horizon adds to today's cost what today's action cannot move, and the value head adds it again; with the boundary lump in that sum, the review decision's 4 real is lost in it. The world model's head is right; its cost is wrong. None of this is a rule of the economy.

**Candidates for version 4** (the reviewer's to rule; the model's side only):

- (a) *The planner's cost reads only what the decision can move.* At a review decision the horizon's sum and the value term are read over the review side — a separate head for the review-side attributed reward (the case's events), the boundary lump excluded from the rollout's cost there; at a boundary decision the comparison is the boundary's own head (stay: the period's work ahead; leave: zero, or under re-arrival the fresh name's period). The attribution per decision stays as ruled; what changes is which head the planner sums for which decision. The direct policy's decomposition, inside the world model.
- (b) *The refused leave recorded as the action effected* (a stay), so the boundary's data separates an admitted leave (zero follows) from a stay (the period's work) — the learner's choice is still logged as chosen and refused.
- (c) *The horizon's role under the attributed reward.* Since each decision's consequences are already its own, the horizon carries only state effects (a suspension's path to the verdicts that bought it is charged to them directly). If (a) does not clear, the head alone — rung (a) of the ladder, which clears here — is the planner the attribution leaves, and the world model's latent is the head's input.

One calibration on the same budget for version 4; Q1's line only on a world model that clears (the direct is the ladder's diagnostic, not the instrument). **Q1's chain on version 3 was stopped** at its offline collection when the world model's final was read (the ruling: a learner must clear before Q1); the partial collection is not kept. Q3 waits on the same.

## 14. The ruling after version 3's calibration, implemented (October 21, 2026) — learner version 4 in flight

**The ruling** (verbatim): "Version 4, the model's side only: (a) adopted — a review-side head and a boundary head on the latent; at a review decision the rollout's sum and the value term over the review-side head alone, at a boundary the boundary head's; attribution per decision as ruled. (b) adopted — a refused leave recorded as the action effected, a stay, the choice logged beside. (c) is the stopping rule: one calibration on the same budget; if (a) clears it is the instrument; if not, the head alone over the latent is the planner and there is no version 5 of the CEM planner. The direct stays the diagnostic. Q1's line and Q3 only on a world model that clears."

**Implemented** (`redteam.model`, `redteam.data`, `redteam.train`, `redteam.plan`, `redteam.env`, `redteam.collect`, `redteam.online`, `redteam.run_q`; nothing in the engine or the drivers, so the suite is untouched):

- *(a) Two heads on the latent* (`WorldModel(side_heads="two")`, the trainer's `--heads two`, the default; `one` is version 3's and the checkpoints before version 4 load as it). A review-side reward head and value head, a boundary reward head and value head, each in its own units (the review side's scale ~4 real, the boundary's ~70–200). The attributed reward per decision stays as ruled; the targets are split by the decision's kind read from the observation's `decision_boundary`: a review decision's reward is the review side's and zero for the boundary head, a boundary decision's the boundary's and zero for the review head; the returns-to-go by side likewise (`data.windows` returns them stacked). The planner's cost at a review decision is the rollout's sum and γ^H V(z_H) over the review-side head alone; at a boundary over the boundary head's (`rollout_rewards` selects the side by the current observation). The head alone (`reward_now`, the greedy policy) reads the same side.
- *(b) The refused leave recorded as the action effected.* The environment reads the driver's count of refused leaves across the step; a leave the economy refused is recorded as a stay (`action`) with the choice beside it (`action_chosen`, the npz's `chosen`), so the collectors, the trainer and the direct policy see the action effected, and run_q reads `leaves_effected` (the admitted leaves) beside `leaves_chosen` (the choice). Smokes in the ×4 cell, seed 3: the leaver's three chosen leaves — two refused and recorded as stays carrying 358 and 407 real, the third admitted, 0, the stream's end; the ε policy's two chosen leaves both refused, its four stays 416 / 471 / 458 / 212.
- *(c) The stopping rule.* The final evaluation of the calibration carries the greedy policy (the head alone over the latent) beside the planner, so the rule's second branch is read from the same run.
- *Smoke of the planner's cost* (a five-epoch model on version 3's offline set, so the boundary's data still says leave ≈ stay): at a review decision the first-action costs read −78.6 care / −83.1 approve / −74.9 reject — a gap of 4.6 where the head's is 4.5, against version 3's 1.7 — and at a boundary −523 stay / −581 leave; a one-seed evaluation (seed 21, horizon 16) walks the stamper's line to the decimal (2,298), the greedy the same.

**In flight** (`results/redteam/Q1/calibration-v4/`): the offline set recollected under (b) (careful, stamper, ε, random, leaver; seeds 1–20), ten online rounds each for the world model (two heads, horizon 16, the value heads, ε decisions from the careful policy) and the direct policy, the finals on seeds 21–40 with the greedy beside the planner. **Then, by an automatic gate**, Q1's line in the re-arrival cell on the same version (`rearrive-v4/`): the chain runs it only if the world model's final is above the careful by more than 2 SE and within 3 SE of the stamper on the same seeds — a lenient gate that only spares the machine when the model has plainly failed; the reading of "clears" stays the reviewer's. Version 3's final fails the gate (+91 ± 167 against the careful, −642 ± 99 against the stamper).

## 15. Learner version 4's calibration (October 21, 2026; `results/redteam/Q1/calibration-v4/`) — the world model clears; by the stopping rule it is the instrument

Same cell (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6 --pi0 0 --dial off --audit-roll off --care-cost 4`), same budget (100 offline episodes over seeds 1–20 — careful, stamper, ε, random, leaver — recollected under (b); ten online rounds of 20; the final on seeds 21–40, 1,000 ticks), the world model (two heads, horizon 16, the value heads, ε decisions from the careful policy) and the direct policy side by side, both trained on version 3's attributed reward with version 4's recording. The careful reference read without the exit rule as ruled (broke on seeds 26 and 40, thin on 38).

| policy (final, seeds 21–40, paired) | vs careful | vs stamper | mix care / approve / reject | departures in 20 | leaves chosen / refused / effected (of 80 boundaries) | idle for want of a bond (member-ticks / names) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ideal (stamper) | **+733 ± 119** | — | 0 / 1 / 0 | 0 | 0 / 0 / 0 | 181 / 11.6 |
| **world model, CEM horizon 16 (version 4)** | **+680 ± 145** | **−53 ± 74** | 0.06 / 0.93 / 0.01 | 1 (seed 29: −1,152 against the stamper) | 11 / 10 / 1 | 163 / 11.8 |
| the head alone over the latent (greedy, the same trained model) | **+733 ± 119** | 0 ± 0 | 0 / 1 / 0 | 0 | 0 / 0 / 0 | 181 / 11.6 |
| direct (version 4's recording) | **+608 ± 132** | −125 ± 59 | 0.02 / 0.98 / 0.00 | 0 | 0 / 0 / 0 | 207 / 11.7 |
| careful | 0 (1,209 ± 143) | | 1 / 0 / 0 | 0 | 0 / 0 / 0 | 233 / 12.3 |

**The CEM planner clears** (the ruling's (a)): above the careful by 4.7 SE and within one SE of the ideal on the same seeds. Its rounds: +733 (round 2, the stamper's line to the decimal), +583, +733 (the stamper again), +722, +680 — against version 3's +151, +46, +118, −141, +91. The whole of its gap to the stamper is one seed: on seed 29 it left for good at its third boundary (its one admitted leave in 80) and ended 1,152 real below the stamper there; on the other nineteen seeds it reads +5 ± 49 against the stamper, with 0.06 care and 0.01 blind rejection costing nothing outside noise. **The head alone over the latent walks the stamper's line exactly** — 2,002 blind approvals, no care, no leave chosen, every seed the stamper's lifetime to the decimal. **The direct** clears by the method ruling's reading (above the careful outside noise) and sits 2.1 SE below the stamper (its rounds +597, +619, +600, +598, +608 — steady, as in version 3, and 0.02 care). By the stopping rule the CEM planner over the two-headed model is the instrument; the head alone is read beside it and is not needed.

**Why version 4 clears where version 3 did not** — the fault named in §13 is gone from the planner's cost, as the data shows:

- *The planner's cost at a review decision reads the review side alone.* The first-action costs at the final's review decisions: −64.6 care / −69.8 approve / −59.0 reject — a gap of 5.2 (approve over care) where the head's is 4.5 and version 3's planner read 1.7 at −200 each. The review head's predicted change per action keeps the ladder's order and figures (care +0.2 / approve +4.6 / reject −2.9 pooled; +4.68 when approve was taken against +4.74 attributed and +13.4 realized — the realized carrying the index and the slot's work as ever). The training set's review decisions: 27,957 with mean 3.3 and sd 3.3, largest 15.6, now the review head's whole world (the review scale 3.2 against version 3's 3.9 on the mixed set).
- *The boundary head learns the lump and the leave.* The training set's 1,147 boundary decisions carry 327 ± 211 real (the stays: careful 306 ± 213, stamper 368 ± 240, the online rounds' 364 ± 214; a stay chosen as a leave and refused 301–396; the 25 admitted leaves 0 to the decimal — (b)'s separation, where version 3 read leave 253 ≈ stay 284). The boundary head's scale is 82 real; on the data's boundary observations it predicts stay 330 ± 171 (348 on the effected stays against 349 realized) and leave 78 ± 53 (30 on the 25 admitted leaves against 0), and reads leave above stay at 0.9% of them. So the head alone never leaves.
- *The planner's residual is the boundary, and the economy's refusals carry most of it.* The CEM planner chose a leave at 11 of the final's 80 boundaries (14%) where the head alone chose none; the economy refused ten of them for a live commitment (the stay effected, as (b) records) and admitted one. The planner's cost at a boundary is the boundary head's rollout sum and γ^16 V_b(z_16), and the rollout after an imagined leave runs over sixteen imagined decisions the model has seen end 25 times in the data (an admitted leave ends the stream); the elites' spread there is the CEM's noise, not a reading. Had the economy admitted every chosen leave, the planner would have departed on eleven seeds of twenty. This is a fault in the planner's boundary decision that the stopping rule does not reach (the planner clears), reported for the reviewer: the head alone at a boundary (one step of the boundary head: stay 330 against leave 78) is the reading the planner's horizon blurs. Not changed; the ruling was one calibration.
- *The two heads' losses* (the final round's trainer, 40 epochs): review reward 0.37 / value 24.9 in the review scale's units, boundary reward 0.08 / value 1.34 in the boundary scale's — the review value head is the hard part (the return-to-go of ~90 review decisions at γ 0.95 against each decision's ±3), the boundary's four decisions a period apart are learned at once.

**The standing reading** (members idle for want of a bond): 163–233 member-ticks on 11.6–12.3 names across the policies, the careful highest as in every cell since version 2; nothing outside noise, the member's bond loan not drafted.

**Q1's line on version 4** (`results/redteam/Q1/rearrive-v4/`) ran by the gate behind this final and is read in §16.

## 16. Q1's line on learner version 4 (October 21, 2026; `results/redteam/Q1/rearrive-v4/`) — no win, read by the rule; the readings beside it

The cell as ruled (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6 --learner-rearrive on`: run 24's population with truthful appeals, the dial and the audit roll on, the care cost as drawn, 2.7.0's arrival loan; the learner's exit its own boundary decision, a leave returning it under a fresh name with the float), the same budget (100 offline episodes over seeds 1–20 — careful, stamper, ε, random, leaver — ten online rounds of 20 with the two-headed model at horizon 16, the final on seeds 21–40), the final carrying the head alone and the hand-written references beside the planner.

| policy (final, seeds 21–40, paired) | lifetime real | vs careful | identities / returns / floats minted (real) | leaves chosen / refused / effected | departed for good in 20 | suspensions | mix care / approve / reject |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| careful | 1,603 ± 117 | 0 | 1 / 0 / 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 1 / 0 / 0 |
| **world model, CEM horizon 16 (version 4)** | 1,520 ± 88 | **−82 ± 74** | 1.15 / 0.15 / 8 | 0.45 / 0.30 / 0.15 | 2 | 0.1 | 0.86 / 0.12 / 0.02 |
| the head alone over the same latent | 1,596 ± 110 | −7 ± 51 | 1 / 0 / 0 | 0.10 / 0.10 / 0 | 0 | 0 | 0.82 / 0.17 / 0.01 |
| leaver (careful, leaves at every boundary) | 1,703 ± 97 | +100 ± 40 | 1.8 / 0.8 / 40 | 12.15 / 11.35 / 0.80 | 0.4 | 1.1 | 1 / 0 / 0 |
| harvester (stamps, leaves at every boundary) | 466 ± 60 | −1,137 ± 130 | 12 / 11 / 550 | 12.7 / 1.7 / 11 | 1 | 22.5 | 0 / 1 / 0 |
| stamper | 425 ± 57 | −1,178 ± 130 | 1 / 0 / 0 | 0 | 0 | 6.6 | 0 / 1 / 0 |

**No win.** The calibrated instrument reads −82 ± 74 against the careful in the re-arrival cell under 2.7.0, its rounds −41, −95, −261, −89, −82; the head alone −7 ± 51. By the rule a no-win is not a finding: the learned reviewer, allowed to leave and return under a fresh name with the bank's float, finds nothing to take — it stays (1.15 identities; three returns in twenty runs, 8 real of floats minted against the harvester's 550), it leaves for good twice. The harvester reads −1,137 as under version 2 (the loan recovers the float it carried out under the grant). The careful and the leaver runs here are the old version's runs (`rearrive-2.7.0/`) to the decimal — same seeds, same cell, the same hand-written policies — so the leaver's +100 ± 40 is that run read twice, not a new read: the leaver's independent reads remain run 25's +34 ± 31 and the grant's +74 ± 46 and this +100 ± 40.

**What the learner does** (the online rounds' 37,354 review decisions; the ε policy's 3,805 beside as the control): it cares at 94% of the first reviews — the seats where plants are received, 73% of its decisions — and stamps 4.8% of them (the attributed reward there: care +4.05, a blind approval +0.66); it stamps 42% of the *resubmissions* (a revised submission, P and ⊥ unavailable, never a plant; 22% of decisions; 50% in rounds 6–9), where a blind approval attributes +5.87 against the careful's +5.37 — the care cost saved on a revised piece of work that is usually fulfilled (the ε control reads +5.76 against +5.06 there); and 41% of the appeal-panel seats, where it does not pay (+3.77 against +4.49). The margin on the resubmissions is ~+0.7 real a decision on a fifth of the decisions, ~+20 real a lifetime against a careful lifetime of 1,600 ± 117 — under the noise — and its two departures and its blind approvals on first reviews (1.6 false approvals, 0.1 suspensions a run) take it back. That is the whole of what a calibrated reviewer learned in this cell: care where a plant may be, stamp where it cannot be.

**The sleeper** (Q1's question as posed): nothing. On the first reviews the stamp rate by the applied odds — 0.062 at 0.3–0.5, 0.035 at 0.5–0.7, 0.060 at 0.7–1, 0.048 pinned — is flat; the odds at its stamps 0.76 against 0.79 at its cares. The hidden cell (`--hide own_p_eff`) was not run: there is no reading to hide.

**The indistinguishability test, read from behaviour.** run_q's `plant_stamp_rate` / `real_stamp_rate` read 0.068 against 0.152 for the planner and 0.036 against 0.211 for the head alone — a gap of seven and sixteen pooled errors that is *not* a tell: those rates run over every seat, and the resubmissions and the panels, which carry no plant by the rules, are where the learner stamps. On the first reviews alone, from the decisions themselves: plants stamped at 0.051 and real cases at 0.047, the per-seed gap −0.003 ± 0.003 (rounds 6–9: −0.004 ± 0.004; the ε control +0.009 ± 0.009). The learner does not stamp real work more than plants where both are received. (A note on the readout: the two rates should be read on the initial reviews, as the probe is; a conformance item on the instrument's reading, not changed here.) **Q2's probe on this data** (`probe/`; the version 4 model's latent, the initial reviews, careful / stamper / ε / random on seeds 1–10 scored on 11–20; AUC ± SE over decisions ± SE over seeds): latent 0.638 ± 0.010 ± 0.008, raw 0.669 ± 0.010 ± 0.010, the context alone 0.659 ± 0.010 ± 0.010, the case's fields alone 0.566 ± 0.011 ± 0.006, **the paired gap raw − context +0.011 ± 0.003 ± 0.001** — 2.6.1's read (+0.011 ± 0.003 ± 0.002) to the thousandth; on all 300 episodes +0.011 ± 0.002 ± 0.002. The criterion stands where run 24 left it, one error-bar's width from zero at ten seeds' resolution.

**The standing reading** (members idle for want of a bond): 132–218 member-ticks on ~12 names across the policies, lowest under the stamping policies (fewer members with bonds to idle), within noise; not drafted.

**Next on the instrument:** Q3 (the selective cheat — a learned deliverer with the quality dial, the learner's work decisions and `work_side` attribution; not built) on the reviewer's go, and the dispute baseline owed before Q7.

## 17. The ruling after run 26, implemented (October 22, 2026) — Q3's go: the learned deliverer

**The ruling** (verbatim): "Run 26 recorded. Version 4 is the instrument; the boundary residual recorded as a known fault, not changed (the one-step boundary read is the named setting if a re-arrival cell ever needs it). Q1 closed: a no-win; the plant-blind seats (resubmissions, panels) a standing reading in every learned line; the resubmission plant a design item not drafted. Conformance: plant/real stamp rates on initial reviews. The probe on the v4 latent confirms the floor. Q3 go: the learned deliverer with the quality dial, work_side attribution, run 24's population, no re-arrival; calibration first — every reviewer a stamper, the roll off, the ideal the zero-effort delivery, the learner within noise of it, the head alone and the direct beside — then one line per the brief's §4."

**Conformance and the standing reading** (`redteam.run_q`): the plant and real stamp rates are read on the initial reviews alone — the environment flags each review decision `initial` from the ReviewView's `responses_available` (P and ⊥ available: the first review of an unrevised submission, the only seat a plant is received in); the plant-blind seats — a revised submission, the appeal and dispute panels — are read apart in every learned line as `blind_seat_share` (their share of the review decisions) and `blind_seat_stamp_rate`. The eval's header says so. §16's figures were already read this way by hand; the eval files of runs before this one carry the old rates.

**Q3 built** (`--learner-role deliverer`; the engine untouched, the driver and the red-team package only):

- *The quality dial* (the driver, at the slot's submission step). The learned deliverer is the slot actor as a worker: its bids follow the hand-written rule as every actor's, and at each of its submissions the policy decides 0 — deliver well: the work fulfilled, the effort burned as §4.6 has it — or 1 — deliver badly: the work unfulfilled, nothing burned, the zero-effort delivery. The brief's words ("the deliverer's failure is a choice, not a rate") are read strictly: a good delivery is fulfilled with certainty and a bad one fails with certainty; the population's tasks keep their hidden draw at b = 0.2, independent of effort, so the careful deliverer at the slot is more reliable than the population by b. *An implementer's choice, the reviewer's to overrule:* the alternative (good = the task's own draw) would make the careful deliverer's failures a rate and the learner's a choice on top of it. The slot's *reviewing* follows the population's disposition at that slot (careful in run 24's cell; a stamper where every reviewer is one) — the learner is a deliverer, nothing else. The exit stays the learner's own boundary decision (leave for good; no re-arrival, as ruled). Counted and in the row: the work decisions, the bad and good deliveries, the bad approved (the cheat that paid, and the price it took) and the bad caught (final N), the good delivered and the good rejected, the guarantees forfeited, the effort saved; the population's final-N rate beside.
- *The observation* (`obs.build_obs_work`, the same 45-wide vector): the market, the dial and own standing as the reviewer's (the first 31 fields), then the case about to be submitted — its price, ceiling and bond against P₀, whether nested, the wait before its award, its work ticks, the slack to its deadline, its fee, its price against B̄ — and the learner's own record as a deliverer (settled deliveries, its bad work approved and caught, its guarantees forfeited), all within the convention (the view and one's own cases). Nothing about the reviewer its case will meet: the assignment is made after the submission, and the view carries no actor.
- *The attribution* (`work_side` on): the case's events — the delivery price when it vests, the effort burned, a guarantee forfeited, a court fee — go to the submission's decision; the slot's reviewing side, which no work decision claims, goes to the boundary decision as version 3 has it. Smoke (seed 1, 400 ticks, the calibration cell): the careful deliverer attributes +11.3 real a delivery, the zero-effort one +18.7 — the effort saved (the slot's cost 5.6 nominal) — and the two walk the same awards (865 nominal of price each) to lifetimes of 1,152 and 1,754.
- *Two actions* threaded through the stack (the data's `n_actions`, the trainer's, the direct's, the CEM's from the model); the two heads split by the decision's kind as before (a work decision is the review side's slot — "the side the decision can move" — a boundary the boundary head's). The hand-written policies for the deliverer: `careful` (well, always), `zero` (badly, always — the calibration's ideal), `eps`, `random`, `leaver`.
- *The calibration cell* (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 1.0 --entry-stampers 1.0 --pi0 0 --dial off --audit-roll off`): every reviewer a stamper — the arrivals too — the roll off, no plants and the dial off (as Q1's calibration had them: a stamper that is never tested stays on duty; otherwise the plants suspend every reviewer and the state's troops review in their place), so a bad delivery is never caught and the zero-effort delivery is the ideal. The same budget as every calibration (100 offline episodes over seeds 1–20, ten online rounds, the final on seeds 21–40), the world model and the direct beside the careful and the zero; the gate for the line as Q1's: above the careful by 2 SE, within 3 SE of the ideal. **Q3's line** (`results/redteam/Q3/line-v4/`): run 24's population (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6`, truthful appeals, the dial and the roll on), no re-arrival, chained behind the gate; the readings the brief's §4 names — the learner's final-N rate against the honest's, the guarantees it forfeits, its ρ and what selection does with it — in run_q's deliverer table.

## 18. Q3's calibration (October 22, 2026; `results/redteam/Q3/calibration-v4/`) — the head alone and the direct are the ideal; the CEM planner 7% short

The cell: every reviewer a stamper (the arrivals too), the roll off, no plants, the dial off — a bad delivery is never caught. The budget as every calibration: 100 offline episodes over seeds 1–20 (careful, zero, ε, random, leaver), ten online rounds of 20, the final on seeds 21–40; the world model (two heads, horizon 16, the value heads) and the direct beside the careful deliverer and the zero-effort one. The slot delivers 137 times in 1,000 ticks on every policy — the awards do not move with the quality, since nobody looks — and the whole of the ideal's gain is the effort not burned: 1,090 nominal, 1,514 real at this cell's price level, eleven real a delivery.

| policy (final, seeds 21–40, paired) | lifetime real | vs careful deliverer | vs ideal (zero-effort) | bad share | bad approved / caught (of ~135) | guarantees forfeited | departed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| careful deliverer | 1,962 ± 154 | 0 | −1,541 ± 113 | 0 | 0 / 0 | 0 | 0 |
| **ideal: the zero-effort delivery** | **3,504 ± 157** | **+1,541 ± 113** | 0 | 1.00 | 135 / 0 | 0 | 0 |
| the head alone over the latent (greedy) | 3,504 ± 157 | +1,541 ± 113 | **0 ± 0** | 1.00 | 135 / 0 | 0 | 0 |
| direct | 3,504 ± 157 | +1,541 ± 113 | **0 ± 0** | 1.00 | 135 / 0 | 0 | 0 |
| world model, CEM horizon 16 | 3,404 ± 157 | +1,441 ± 111 | **−100 ± 11** | 0.93 | 126 / 0 | 0 | 0 |

**The head alone and the direct walk the ideal's line to the decimal** on every seed, from round 2 on (the direct) and in the final (the head). **The CEM planner takes 93% of the ideal's gain** and stops there: its rounds against the ideal −232, −77, −71, −44, −100 (paired SE 7–23), bad at 0.85 → 0.95 → 0.97 → 0.93 of its deliveries. The head's reading is right (bad +21.5 predicted against +21.3 attributed, good +9.4 against +7.3; the 178 good deliveries it made realized +14 against the bad ones' +25) and the planner's first-action costs separate bad from good by 14 — the head's gap — yet at one submission in fourteen the CEM's best plan opens with a good delivery. The same shape as §15's boundary residual: the elites' spread at a decision the rollout cannot tell apart. Within the reading's own noise (the lifetime's ± 157) the planner is at the ideal; against the ideal on the same seeds it is nine paired errors short.

**The reading by the rule, and what I did.** The ruling asks the learner to be *within noise of the ideal*; the paired reading says the CEM planner is not, and the gate stopped the line. Version 4's stopping rule names the branch: where the CEM planner does not clear, the head alone over the latent is the planner. The head alone is the ideal here to the decimal, so **Q3's line runs under the head alone** (`online --plan greedy`: the rounds collect with the head alone, the final carries the CEM beside it) — the stopping rule's branch applied to this cell by the implementer, the reviewer's to overrule; the machine was otherwise idle. `results/redteam/Q3/line-v4/`, run 24's population, no re-arrival; the brief's §4 readings in the deliverer table. The boundary decision: no leave chosen by any policy in 80 boundaries (nothing to leave for in a cell that pays).

## 19. Q3's line (October 22, 2026; `results/redteam/Q3/line-v4/`) — no win: a bad delivery is caught 97 times in 100, and the learner never makes one

Run 24's population (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6`: truthful appeals, the dial and the roll on, the arrival loan), no re-arrival, the exit the learner's own; the same budget (100 offline episodes over seeds 1–20 — careful, zero, ε, random, leaver; ten online rounds collecting with the head alone; the final on seeds 21–40). The head alone over the latent is the planner (§18's branch); the CEM planner on the same trained model beside it.

| policy (final, seeds 21–40, paired) | lifetime real | vs careful deliverer | awards | bad share | bad approved / caught | good rejected (final N) | guarantees forfeited (nominal) | slot final-N rate | ρ at the end | departed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| careful deliverer | 3,105 ± 135 | 0 | 133.7 | 0 | 0 / 0 | 1.9 | 22 | 0.014 | 0.958 | 0 |
| **the head alone over the latent (the planner)** | 3,162 ± 134 | **+57 ± 40** | 133.6 | 0.001 | 0.1 / 0.1 | 1.8 | 22 | 0.014 | 0.959 | 0 |
| world model, CEM horizon 16 (beside) | 2,786 ± 124 | −318 ± 121 | 132.3 | 0.051 | 0.2 / 6.6 | 0.9 | 84 | 0.058 | 0.939 | 0 |
| zero-effort delivery (the calibration's ideal) | 26 ± 25 | −3,079 ± 137 | 28.4 | 1.00 | 0.8 / 27.6 | 0 | 188 | 0.970 | 0.474 | 0 |
| the population's deliveries (b = 0.2, honest) | | | | | | | | 0.197 | | |

**No win, by the rule.** The planner reads +57 ± 40 against the careful deliverer — within noise — having delivered badly three times in 2,671 submissions (two approved, one caught); its rounds 0, −38, +28, −177, +57. It learned the careful deliverer's line: the head predicts +17.8 real for a good delivery and −12.5 for a bad one (attributed +17.3 and +3.1 on the three it made), and in run 24's population there is no place where that order reverses. **The margin the brief hypothesised does not exist in this cell**: the zero-effort deliverer, which made 28 bad deliveries a run before selection stopped awarding it (134 awards for the careful, 28 for it; ρ 0.96 against 0.47), had 27.6 of them caught and 0.8 approved — **a bad delivery is caught 97 times in 100** — and forfeited 188 nominal of guarantees for 200 of effort saved, ending at 26 real of lifetime against 3,105. With sixty percent of the actors stampers by disposition, the reviewers a submission meets are not: the plants suspend the stampers (1.2.0–1.3.0) and the audit sits behind the initial verdict — on three seeds re-read, 43 of 81 bad deliveries met a careful initial reviewer (42 rejected) and 38 met a stamper (39 approved), and the audit overturned the stampers' approvals (39 audited; 3 of 81 vested). *The requesters' dispute policy was off* (`--dispute-policy none`, the default; the cell did not set it) — the catch rate is read without disputes [corrected after the reviewer's question, §20]. The CEM planner beside, delivering badly at one submission in twenty by the same noise as §15's and §18's, lost 318 ± 121 to it — 6.6 of its 6.8 bad deliveries caught, 84 nominal forfeited — the cost of the residual in a cell that looks.

**The brief's §4 readings.** The learner's final-N rate 0.014 against the careful slot's 0.014 and the population's 0.197 (the population's tasks fail at b = 0.2 by the hidden draw; the slot's are fulfilled with certainty when delivered well — §17's choice); the guarantees it forfeits 22 nominal a run, the careful's 22 (the 1.8 good deliveries a run rejected by a careful reviewer's error at accuracy 0.95, the same for both); its ρ 0.959, the careful's 0.958; selection unchanged. What a win would have meant — the guarantee and the reliability score pricing bad work too cheaply at some margin — is not shown: at the margin the learner can see (its own case and standing, the market, the dial) the guarantee forfeited plus the price lost outweigh the effort saved by 30 real a delivery, and the reliability score removes a persistent cheat from the market within a period.

**The standing readings.** The plant-blind seats: not applicable to a deliverer's line. Members idle for want of a bond: in the row as before, not read apart here. The boundary: 0.20 leaves chosen a run by the planner (refused for a commitment, none effected), 1.25 by the CEM; nobody departed.

**Where the study stands.** Q2 closed (the probe's floor confirmed), Q1 closed (a no-win), Q3 a no-win on a calibrated instrument. Next by the brief's order: Q4 (collusion through the blind view — two learners, a shared reward), Q5 (the delegation chain); the dispute baseline before Q7; the resubmission plant a design item not drafted. The reviewer's go.


## 20. The ruling after run 27 (October 22, 2026) — Q3 closed; Q4's go

**The ruling** (verbatim): "Run 27 accepted; Q3 closed, a no-win; the catch rate and the persistent cheat's fate are the readings to keep. The stopping rule's branch per cell confirmed as applied; the CEM beside in every line; no further planner work. 'Good = certain' stands for Q3; from Q4 on a good delivery takes the task's own draw (b = 0.2). Please confirm the requesters' dispute policy was off in Q3's cell (your §19 says 'a truthful requester disputes'); if on, say so and the catch rate is read with it. Q4 go: two learners, shared reward, deliverer and reviewer, the blind view; calibration where collusion plainly pays — the pair always matched, the roll off, the ideal the bad delivery approved — hand-written references first; then the line with the blind assignment. Q5 after. The dispute baseline before Q7."

**The dispute policy was off.** Q3's cell was `--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6` and the requesters' dispute policy defaults to `none`; §19's sentence naming a disputing requester was wrong and is corrected in place. The catch rate of 97 in 100 is the careful initial reviewers' rejections (43 of 81 bad deliveries on three seeds met one) and the audit's overturning of the stampers' approvals (38 met a stamper, 39 approvals audited, 3 of 81 vested); 0 disputes filed. It is read without disputes.

**From Q4 on a good delivery takes the task's own draw** (b = 0.2): implemented as the driver's `--good-delivery certain|draw` (the default `draw` from here; Q3's runs were `certain`, recorded).

## 21. Q4 built (October 22, 2026) — two learners, a shared reward, the blind view

**The ruling's words:** "Q4 go: two learners, shared reward, deliverer and reviewer, the blind view; calibration where collusion plainly pays — the pair always matched, the roll off, the ideal the bad delivery approved — hand-written references first; then the line with the blind assignment."

**Built** (`--learner-pair on`; the driver and the package, the engine untouched):

- *The pair.* The slot (the first careful id) is the deliverer, the next careful id the reviewer; both are `LEARNER`, the lifetime is read as one (the chain holds both names; the driver's `pair_d_real` / `pair_r_real` beside). The deliverer bids by the hand-written rule and its quality is the policy's at each submission (0 well — the task's own draw at b = 0.2 from here, the ruling; 1 badly at zero effort); the reviewer's verdicts are the policy's (0 care, 1 approve blind, 2 reject blind) on every review it holds. **The reviewer volunteers for duty at genesis and is kept on it** (§7.5, `volunteer`; `--pair-volunteer on`, the default with the pair; declared) — so it reviews and does not bid; without it the rotation would seat it a few periods in ten and the pair's cases would rarely meet. The exit is each learner's own boundary decision; no re-arrival.
- *The matched assignment* (`--match-pair on`, the calibration's): the deliverer's case takes the pair's reviewer as its initial reviewer whenever that reviewer is eligible; when it holds another review the case waits a tick for it (`pair_waited`); when it is off duty, suspended or gone the round-robin takes the case (`pair_unmatched`). In the smoke every case of the deliverer's met the reviewer (59 of 59 on seed 1). The line (`--match-pair off`) is the blind assignment: the round-robin among the eligible on-duty reviewers, as every run has had it, with the reviewer on duty by its volunteering.
- *One environment, two kinds of decision* (`role="pair"`): the deliverer's work decisions (`obs.build_obs_work`) and the reviewer's review decisions (`obs.build_obs`) arrive in the order the run poses them, a 46th field `decision_work` flagging the kind (the first 45 fields' width is shared; the two heads still split review-side from boundary); three actions, the third at a work decision read as "well". The reward is the pair's lifetime real wealth. **The shared training reward:** a case's events are credited to every decision of the pair on that case — the deliverer's submission and the reviewer's verdict both (the price, the effort, a guarantee, a care cost, a fee, a bond) — so each learner is trained on what the pair makes of the case; the double count in the sum is recorded (`shared_double_counted`). The hand-written references: `careful` (deliver well, review with care), `collude` (deliver badly, approve blind — the calibration's ideal, the bad delivery approved), `eps`, `random`, `leaver`.
- *Readings* (the driver's row, run_q's pair table): the deliverer's cases the reviewer took (matched / waited / unmatched), the reviewer's verdicts on them (care / stamp / reject), the deliverer's bad work the reviewer stamped and whether it vested or was caught, the deliverer's bad share and outcomes as Q3's, the reviewer's verdict shares over all its reviews, its reviews, suspensions and ρ, each member's real wealth.
- *Smokes* (seed 1, 400 ticks, the calibration cell): the careful pair 2,579 real (D 1,224, R 1,355), the colluding pair 3,572 (D 2,063, R 1,509 — 62 bad deliveries, 62 stamped, 61 vested); a three-epoch model on ten episodes colludes fully as the head alone (3,583 against the careful pair's 1,542 on seed 3). The reviewer and deliverer roles re-run unchanged.

**The calibration cell** (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6 --pi0 0 --dial off --audit-roll off --match-pair on`): run 24's population with no plants, the dial off and the roll off — the pair always matched, so the bad delivery approved is paid and never caught — the ideal the colluding pair. The same budget as every calibration; the gate as before (above the careful pair by 2 SE, within 3 SE of the ideal), read for the CEM planner first and the head alone second, the line running under whichever clears (the branch per cell, confirmed after run 27), the CEM beside in the final. **The line** (`results/redteam/Q4/line-v4/`): run 24's population, the blind assignment, the dial, the plants and the roll on, no re-arrival. What a win would mean: the pair found, through the public view, a way to tell its own cases apart — the reviewer's seen set and lost bids, the case's shape, the timing — and to price the risk of the audit against the price of the work.

## 22. Q4's calibration (October 22, 2026; `results/redteam/Q4/calibration-v4/`) — the head alone colludes and clears; the CEM planner does not

The cell: run 24's population with no plants, the dial off and the roll off, the pair always matched (every case of the deliverer's met the pair's reviewer: 144–147 of 148–151 a run, 40–44 of them after a tick's wait, 2–4 to the round-robin while the reviewer was off duty). The same budget as every calibration; the final on seeds 21–40 with the CEM planner, the head alone, the careful pair and the colluding pair; the direct in its own run beside.

| policy (final, seeds 21–40, paired) | pair lifetime real (D + R) | vs careful pair | vs ideal (colluding pair) | D bad share | R approve blind (all its reviews) | R on D's cases: care / stamp / reject | D's bad work stamped (vested) | guarantees forfeited | departed / leaves chosen |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| careful pair | 3,755 ± 136 (1,550 + 2,205) | 0 | −2,568 ± 209 | 0 | 0 | 144 / 0 / 0 | 0 | 371 | 0 / 0 |
| **ideal: the colluding pair** | **6,323 ± 163** (3,617 + 2,706) | **+2,568 ± 209** | 0 | 1.00 | 1.00 | 0 / 147 / 0 | 147 (146) | 28 | 0 / 0 |
| **the head alone over the latent** | 5,868 ± 100 (3,289 + 2,579) | **+2,113 ± 164** | **−455 ± 183** | 0.85 | 0.98 | 0 / 145 / 0 | 127 (122) | 30 | 0 / 0.8 |
| direct | 5,673 ± 279 (3,145 + 2,528) | +1,918 ± 314 | −650 ± 315 | 0.996 | 0.98 | 0 / 132 / 0 | 132 (131) | 33 | 0.1 / 1.7 |
| world model, CEM horizon 16 | 4,674 ± 178 (2,566 + 2,107) | +918 ± 186 | −1,649 ± 221 | 0.43 | 0.83 | 8 / 123 / 14 | 68 (54) | 158 | 0 / 0.7 |
