The members read +9,300 to +10,900 nominal against all-careful (40–47%) and +2,600 to +4,200 against the hand (9–14%) — **and no richer in real terms**: +277 ± 967, −131 ± 914, −1,701 ± 774, −299 ± 746 against the hand, −2,300 to −4,300 against all-careful. The nominal gain is the price: a sixth fewer hands (16–17 alive against 19.4 and 22.7; the suspensions and the departures they bring) sell 160–360 fewer tasks at +1.5 to +3.6 on P_t over the hand's, +7.7 to +9.8 over all-careful's 14.4. The care saved is paid back in suspensions, lost awards and departures. The customers pay the rest: tasks per 1,000 nominal credits −6 to −13% against the hand and −20 to −26% against all-careful, every seed of 20 (the false approvals are paid-for unfulfilled work; the price is higher; the awards fewer) — against the all-careful population's 37.8 tasks a thousand, where 22.7 members compete at P_t 14.4 and a requester holds 962 real. All-careful is the honest reference, and it is also the cheapest and the busiest market of the four.

**A note on the measure.** The ruling fixed the nominal surplus for population cells. For the reviewers' population the nominal and the real disagree in sign against all-careful (+10,590 nominal, −2,333 real) and in size against the hand (+3,911 nominal, +277 real), because this equilibrium changes the number of hands and so the price; §47's bidders disagreed the other way (−8,648 nominal, +4,620 real, the deflator). I report both and leave the measure where the ruling put it; the reviewer may want the real beside in the population tables from here (it is in the first table of every eval).

**The plant/real gap on the initial reviews — a reading, and a question for the instrument.** The population stamps plants 0.26–0.31 and real cases 0.38–0.42 on the initial reviews, a gap of 0.10–0.13 where Q2's single slot read +0.011 (§17). Split on the round-10 collections (57,000 initial reviews a chain): the gap holds within the pinned (+0.10) and within the members (+0.10) — not the seats' composition; it is **absent early** (t < 250: +0.03 and −0.02; the dial's π below 0.25: +0.006 and −0.046) and **present once the dial has risen** (π ≥ 0.35: +0.10 and +0.09), when plants are 30% of the initial reviews against 10%. The fields that separate plant from real among the unseen initial reviews are the dial's and the slot's own record (dial_pi, dial_m, probationers, own_awards — d' 0.5–0.6), all of them the context the dial publishes by design, none of them the case's. Q2's probe on the same collections, with the per-field ablation: Q2's probe on the same collections (each basin's round-10 online files of the world model's chain, trained on seeds 1–10 and read on 11–20, the initial reviews only, ~28,000 decisions each side; the latent, the raw observation, the context alone, the case's fields alone, with the per-field ablation; `probe-online10` under each basin): the raw observation 0.744 / 0.745, the context alone 0.712 / 0.709, the case's fields alone 0.669 / 0.663 — and **the gap raw − context +0.032 ± 0.003 and +0.036 ± 0.003** (the episode-grouped bootstrap), three times 2.6.1's +0.011 ± 0.002 (§17). The ablation puts the gap on the seen set: `seen_ticks_since` adds +0.017 / +0.020 to the context, `seen_before` +0.008 / +0.010, `seen_beta_rel` +0.003 / +0.005, `rv_gap` +0.002 (its 2.6.1 figure); the case's own fields — the ceiling, the bond, the questions — add nothing. **The same probe on this cell's hand-written ε set** (`probe-eps`: the same forty slots keeping the same seen sets, an all-careful population with ε, no learning) **reads the gap at +0.005 ± 0.001 with the seen set adding +0.0000**: the forty-slot construction does not separate plants; this population's dynamics do. In the learned population's data a real root's reviewer had seen it available 13.8% of the time and a plant's 10.4%, and the initial review came 4.6 ticks after the sighting for a real root and 2.9 for a plant; under the ε population 6.7% and 6.3%, 2.9 and 2.7 ticks. The policy uses it: it stamps the cases it saw at 0.62 against 0.40 unseen. Most of the behavioural gap is the dial's own signal read back (the context carries 0.71 of the raw 0.74: the policy cares more where π is high, and plants are many there); the residual is the seen set's, and that is the instrument's question — something in a plant's life (the wait in the market, the submission's draw, the seat) runs differently from a real root's once a sixth of the members are suspended and the dial sits at π 0.35 and m 3.4, and the seen set reads it. I do not have the mechanism; the hand's population shows none of it, and each of Q2's three corrections came after a ruling. **Proposed, not built:** the plant's life by stage beside a real root's — the ticks posted → awarded, awarded → submitted, submitted → seated, the raises, the floor awards, the bids lost — by case kind in the learned population's runs (readings in the driver's row and run_q's table; a conformance diagnostic on the instrument under 2.6.x's principle that a plant takes every step in the pass a real root would). The §17 standing reading keeps its place.

**Read together.** The reviewers in self-play have one basin, and it is a captured one: half the reviews stamped — the plant-blind seats nearly all, the initial reviews two in five, less while pinned — ten times the hand's false approvals standing, the audit layer made of the same population confirming them (its overturn rate below the hand's), the plants alone holding the market alive at 6.6 false approvals a suspension; the members no richer in real terms than the hand's 60/40, a sixth fewer of them, the price up a tenth, the customers a tenth poorer in tasks per credit than under the hand and a fifth poorer than under all-careful. An evaluation under fixed rules (§19): the rules did not fail — the plant record did what it was designed to do and the audit layer did what a captured layer does — and nothing here asks for a rule from me. Two cells and a diagnostic suggest themselves and I launch none unasked: (a) the same self-play on the dispute baseline (`--dispute-policy truthful`), where the challenger stands outside the reviewers' population — the one reading that would say whether 2.x's loop has a challenger the captured layer cannot absorb; (b) the half (`--learner-pop half`): the learners among the hand's 60/40, where the audit layer is not entirely theirs; (c) the plant's life by stage under this population, above.

**(iii) declared in full, for the ruling before launch.** Two populations, one world, one chain; nothing of it built until ruled.

- **The world.** Run 24's world with truthful disputes (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6 --dispute-policy truthful --undercut 0.1`): the dispute baseline, where Q7's requester was read (§42, §44) and the only cell whose customers' measure (tasks received) is read with the challenger the rules give them. The bidders' hand references are re-read in it by the chain itself (the hand's policies run on the same seeds), so §47's figures on run 24's world without disputes are not reused.
- **The bidders' population:** §46's construction unchanged — every careful member at genesis and every careful arrival a slot of Q6's three actions (the ratchet's bid / the 10% undercut floored at cost / withhold), each on its own observation (Q6's fields), the won root's events to its own decision, each slot's unclaimed events to its own lineage, the exit the population's rule. The training reward the attributed real change, as §46.
- **The requesters' population** (`--learner-req-pop all`, new): every standing requester (six; no exit, no standing, no boundary decision) a slot of Q7's three actions at each posting the population's rule admits (the market rule's ceiling / patient, raised to the state's price after `--patient-ticks` / the floor, never raised), each on its own observation (Q7's fields: its wallet, its requests, the three ceilings' affordability, its record), the posted case's events to its own decision (Q7's attribution). The training reward Q7's attributed surplus v·F − S at `--task-value 31` (the value the ruling fixed for Q7's line: the careful requester's own rate on the dispute baseline); the population's measure the customers' tasks per 1,000 nominal credits, as ruled — the training signal and the measure are not the same thing, and I say so here rather than let the measure train the policy.
- **Two policies, two models, one episode.** The driver asks both populations in the same world; the env emits each decision with its kind (`bid` / `post`), each kind its own observation vector, so the collectors write two files an episode (`<policy>-seedN.npz`, `<policy>-seedN-req.npz`) and each round fits two models — the bidders' and the requesters' — on their own role's decisions with their own attribution. The direct the carrier for both roles; the world model's head alone beside for both (as §47); the CEM not run. The rounds' ε from the careful policy of each role (§47's setting), ten rounds, the finals on seeds 21–40.
- **The references, hand pairs on the same slots and seeds**, four: (1) ratchet × market rule — the world's control; (2) §47's mix × market rule — a hand-written bidder policy `mix47` that draws each decision's rule at §47's frequencies (ratchet 0.25, undercut 0.66, withhold 0.09; fixed numbers, not a model); (3) ratchet × floor — every requester at the reservation, §42's cell; (4) the learned pair's two halves alone — learned bidders × market rule, ratchet × learned requesters (the same two models at the final) — so the pair's effect is read against each role's effect alone.
- **The measures,** in one table paired against the control: the members' nominal surplus (real beside), the customers' tasks per 1,000 nominal (and a requester's wallet — the hoard), P_t and the members' price, **the floor's binding** (the awards under the floor, the state's price over the market, the requests marked below the reservation and their ticks waiting, the state's deliveries) — the question of (iii) is whether the bidders' descent (§47: to 12, the floor not binding) and the requesters' descent (§44: to the reservation, 50–87% of postings) meet at the reservation and what happens there — the state's share, honesty (false approvals, suspensions), arrivals and departures, both mixes by period.
- **Cost.** ~9,500 bid decisions and ~3,500 posting decisions an episode, ~30 s; the chain the size of §47's two together. No calibration, the equilibrium the reading.


## 50. The ruling after run 34, and the plant's life by stage (October 25, 2026) — a conformance item found on the plant's path at w_A

**The ruling (verbatim):** "Run 34 recorded as the stability track's first finding against the rules: honesty is not a stable equilibrium of a self-play reviewer population with disputes off; the audit layer is captured through the plant-blind seats. Classified: design revision 2.8.0, the plants reach every seat (audits of plants, planted appeals, planted resubmissions, adjudicated by truth), drafted after: (a) the disputes-on self-play cell; (b) the half cell; (c) your diagnostic — the plant's life by stage by case kind in the learned population, a conformance item if any stage differs, before the plant's life is extended. Both measures in the population tables from here. (iii) held until these are read. Q1's reading of the plant-blind seats corrected on the record."

**On the record.** §49 is run 34, the stability track's first finding against the rules. The ruling after run 26 (§17) made the plant-blind seats — a revised submission, a panel, where no plant is received — a standing reading on the ground that a learner stamping them was a learner reading the rules' shape; run 34 reads the same seats as the route of the capture: the population stamps them 94–97%, the audit seat among them, and the layer that was to catch the initial stamps confirms them. The reading is corrected: the plant-blind seats are where the plant record cannot reach and where a self-play population stamps; 2.8.0 — the plants reach every seat — is the reviewer's to draft after (a), (b), (c). **Both measures** — the members' nominal surplus and the slots' lifetime real, paired — are in the population tables (bidder and reviewer) from here (`redteam.run_q`). **(iii)** stays declared in §49 and held.

**(c) The diagnostic, built** (`drivers/stamping.py`, `redteam/run_q.py`; a reading, no rule). At a root's **first initial seat** — the review's own assignment tick `t_assign`, for every root a driver-controlled reviewer sits on, by case kind — the driver accumulates the plant's life beside a real root's: the ticks **posted → awarded** (`t_award − t_post`), **awarded → submitted** (`t_submit − t_award`), **submitted → seated** (`t_assign − t_submit`), whether the ceiling was **raised** (`Task.raised_n`, counted at the driver's raise), whether the award was the **floor's** (a plant, `plant_at_floor`) or a **troop's** (a real root, `troop_award`), and whether the seated reviewer had the case in its **seen set** (a free member saw it available) with the ticks from the sighting to the seat. The row carries the means by kind (`life_real_*`, `life_plant_*`); run_q's reviewer evaluation carries the table with each stage as real / plant and the paired difference plant − real by seed. Roots that never reach a driver-controlled initial seat (a troop reviewer's) are not in it.

**The stage that differs by construction, found before the diagnostic ran.** The hand's population on seed 21 (run 24's control) reads the plants **at the floor 18.0%** of the time and the real roots **delivered by a troop 0.04%**; posted → awarded 0.38 for a plant against 0.83 for a real root; awarded → submitted 1.08 against 1.00; submitted → seated 0.24 against 0.14; raised 49% against 46%. The engine's summons pass explains the first figure, and it is a conformance item on the plant's path: the 2.6.0 rule says a plant unbid for $w_A$ "goes to its plant actor at $p_T$ … **as a real root would go to a troop**", and a real root past $w_A$ goes to a troop only **beyond the free fundable members** — patch 1.7.1's per-request reading (the oldest $w_A$-old requests, as many as there are free fundable members, are left to the market; the rest are summoned). The plant's block in `_summon_troops` applies the price test and the run-21 refusals (capacity, credit) and then awards **every** plant past $w_A$ at the floor at once, with no count of the free fundable members — so in a market where a free member nearly always exists (the real roots' 0.04%), a plant that waited two ticks for a bid goes to the floor where a real root in the same position is left to the market and bid on a tick later. The plant's life differs at that stage: it is awarded sooner (the floor at $w_A$ against the market's wait), at $p_T$ (not a member's bid), to its actor at $\tau_T$, and is seen by fewer free members for fewer ticks — the direction of §49's seen-set separation (a real root's reviewer had seen it 13.8% against a plant's 10.4%, 4.6 ticks before the seat against 2.9), and a difference the learned population widens by thinning the free members (16–17 alive against 19.4). The 1.7.1 correction reached the real roots' path in 2.4.0 (the pass gathers β ≥ $p_T$ first) and the plant's path got the refusals after run 21; the count it did not get.

**The corrections, proposed and not made** (the engine's; every conformance correction to the plant's path has been ruled first): the plants past $w_A$ at β ≥ $p_T$ join the pass's `waiting` list with the real roots, oldest first; the free fundable members are counted once over both; the oldest `left` of them, plants or real, are the market's; the rest are the state's — a real root summoned (`_summon_delivery`), a plant awarded at the floor (`_award_plant`, at $p_T$ and $\tau_T$), each through its own refusals. No rule moves: the plant takes the pass's step a real root takes. The goldens move (the plants' lives change in every world with a free member at $w_A$), to be re-pinned once at the correcting commit as before; `test_v26` gets the regression (a plant past $w_A$ with a free fundable member waits as a real root does). And the second: `_plant_draw` reads `_recent[key]` whole while no period has closed (the default only before the first real root), so the first period's plants submit and post as the real roots of the period do. Expected effect: `plants_at_floor` falls from 14–38% to the real roots' order; the plants' posted → awarded lengthens to the real roots' and their seats and sightings follow; the first period's lag disappears; the seen set's separation should close — §49's probe (`probe-online10`) re-read on a corrected chain is the test. The goldens move once at the correcting commit; `test_v26` gets both regressions.

**The diagnostic in the learned population** (`learned-rev-careful/direct/eval/life/`: the §49 direct model on seeds 21–40 against `careful`, `stamper`, `hand`): per root that reached its first initial seat, by case kind (real / plant), and plant − real paired by seed (mean ± SE over 20):

| population | roots seated (real / plant) | posted → awarded | Δ | awarded → submitted | Δ | submitted → seated | Δ | raised share | Δ | troop (real) / floor (plant) | seen share | Δ | ticks since sighting | Δ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| learned (the §49 direct) | 2,262 / 620 | 1.29 / 0.61 | −0.68 ± 0.07 | 1.00 / 1.08 | +0.08 ± 0.00 | 0.05 / 0.09 | +0.04 ± 0.01 | 0.454 / 0.555 | +0.100 ± 0.010 | 0.008 / **0.381** | 0.144 / 0.106 | −0.038 ± 0.006 | 3.26 / 1.86 | −1.40 ± 0.12 |
| hand (run 24's 60/40) | 2,457 / 633 | 0.72 / 0.30 | −0.42 ± 0.07 | 1.00 / 1.08 | +0.08 ± 0.00 | 0.13 / 0.17 | +0.03 ± 0.01 | 0.332 / 0.345 | +0.013 ± 0.007 | 0.008 / **0.138** | 0.100 / 0.072 | −0.029 ± 0.005 | 2.52 / 1.74 | −0.78 ± 0.13 |
| all-careful | 2,636 / 270 | 0.20 / 0.07 | −0.13 ± 0.03 | 1.00 / 1.19 | +0.19 ± 0.00 | 0.06 / 0.07 | +0.00 ± 0.00 | 0.148 / 0.137 | −0.011 ± 0.003 | 0.001 / 0.030 | 0.063 / 0.070 | +0.008 ± 0.004 | 1.40 / 1.48 | +0.08 ± 0.10 |
| all-stamper | 870 / 148 | 0.91 / 0.43 | −0.48 ± 0.05 | 1.00 / 1.34 | +0.34 ± 0.01 | 0.03 / 0.26 | +0.23 ± 0.04 | 0.185 / 0.192 | +0.007 ± 0.010 | 0.142 / 0.305 | 0.095 / 0.089 | −0.006 ± 0.008 | 3.29 / 1.67 | −1.62 ± 0.18 |

Three stages differ; two by construction, one as their consequence; and one reading beside.

1. **Posted → awarded: the floor at $w_A$ without the free-member count — the conformance item above, sized.** In the learned population 38.1% of the plants are awarded at the floor against 0.8% of the real roots delivered by a troop; in the hand's 13.8% against 0.8%; in the all-careful 3.0% against 0.1%. A plant's wait in the market is capped at $w_A$ (on seed 21's control, 0–3 ticks) where a real root's runs to 7; the gap in the mean wait −0.13 → −0.42 → −0.68 as the free members thin (all-careful → hand → learned), and the learned population is where it is widest — the thinner the market, the more plants the pass sends to the floor while the real roots beside them wait for a bid.
2. **Awarded → submitted: the first period's draw.** Every real delivery takes one world tick (the members' τ 2 on a driver clock a tick ahead; 2,407 of 2,409 on seed 21), and so does every plant's — except the first period's, which take two: 50 of 637 plants on seed 21, all posted before the first close. `_plant_draw` reads the last k real roots' terms with k the count the closed periods hold; before the first close k is 0, the pool is empty and the draw falls to its default — τ, 2 ticks — although `_recent` already holds the period's real roots. The same default serves the ceiling (`price_level()`) before the first close. The gap is a constant of the first period's share of the plants (+0.08 ± 0.00 in the hand's and the learned populations, +0.19 and +0.34 where fewer plants are posted later), and a first-period plant is told by its lag. **A second conformance item on the instrument:** before the first close, the draws read the real roots posted so far (`_recent[key]` whole), the default only before the first real root.
3. **Submitted → seated: derivative.** Market-awarded plants wait a tick for their seat 26.5% against the real roots' 16.9% on seed 21 (later periods), **and conditional on the market wait the shares match** (awarded at once: 30.1% against 28.7%; after a tick: 7.0% against 7.3%): the driver seats in the working set's posting order, a case that waited longer in the market stands earlier in it, and the plants wait less by item 1. The engine's bond is sized alike (G_V × the index for both kinds); there is no plant-specific rule in the seating. Item 1's correction removes it; a seating by `seat_since` with the posting order as the tie-break would be the instrument's cleaner form, not proposed now.
4. **The seen set: derivative of item 1.** A real root that waits is seen by more free members for more ticks before its seat; a plant's wait is capped. In the learned population the reviewer had seen a real root 14.4% of the time and a plant 10.6%, 3.26 ticks before the seat against 1.86; in the hand's 10.0% against 7.2%, 2.52 against 1.74; in the all-careful, where real roots are awarded at once (0.20), no gap (+0.008, +0.08). §49's probe gap (+0.032/+0.036 on the population's data, carried by `seen_ticks_since`; +0.005 on the ε set's) is this: the cell's ε set is an all-careful world.
5. **Raised: the trailing window under a trend — a reading, not a fault.** The plants are raised 10 points more often than the real roots in the learned population (+0.100 ± 0.010), 1.3 points more in the hand's, 1.1 fewer in the all-careful: a plant's ceiling is the last k real roots' *as posted* (2.6.1), a real root's is the market rule at the live B̄, so where the price trends up (the learned population's P_t 20 → 22.5) the plants post below the live market and are raised, and where it trends down (all-careful, 20 → 14.4) they post above it. The ceiling alone separates plant from real at AUC 0.59–0.60 in §49's probe but adds nothing beyond the context (the trend is public). By design; the reviewer may want the window's length read against the trend.

**(a) and (b), launched as ruled** on the careful-weighted basin (the basin test answered in §49), the direct the carrier and the head alone beside, the rounds' ε uniform as in §48, references `careful` / `stamper` / `hand`: **(a)** `--dispute-policy truthful` with every member a slot (`results/redteam/stability/learned-rev-disputes/`) — the challenger outside the reviewers' population; **(b)** `--learner-pop half` (`learned-rev-half/`) — half the members (stampers included) and half the arrivals learn, the rest by hand, so the audit layer is not wholly the learners'. Both run on the instrument as it stands; if the correction above is ruled, they are re-run on the corrected one and the §49 chains with them. Read in §51.


## 51. The ruling after run 34's addendum: two conformance corrections and erratum 2.6.2, landed (October 25, 2026) — the plant's path at w_A, the first period's draws, the ceiling as a ratio

**The ruling (verbatim):** "Run 34's addendum recorded. (1) and (2): conformance corrections, adopted — plants past w_A join the pass's waiting list and the free-member count (Appendix S's ruling after run 21); the draws read the real roots posted so far before the first close; goldens once, test_v26 the regressions. (3): errata — the ceiling and the raise drawn relative to the market price at posting (β/B̄) and applied to the current B̄; land it as 2.6.2 with the two, or fold it into 2.8.0, your call; re-read the ten points after. Re-run §49's two chains and (a), (b) on the corrected instrument; the record keeps both readings. The stage-by-kind reading is a standing check in every population cell from here. 2.8.0 after the re-reads."

**My call on (3): erratum 2.6.2, landed with the two.** It amends the plant's-disguise text (§7.6, "Rule — the plant's life") and nothing a member may do; 2.8.0 is a design revision not yet drafted, and an erratum should not wait on it. Appendix E carries E6; the spec in force stays 2.7.0 with its errata. My reading of "the ceiling and the raise": the window holds each real root's β/B̄ at its posting (`post_root`), a plant draws a ratio and applies it to the B̄ of its own posting (`post_plant`; the ratio default 1.0 before the first real root — the plant at B̄, which before any award is P_t); the raise was already relative — the plant requester raises to the current market price as the population's requesters do (2.6.1, the driver's `raise_below_market`), so no code moves for it and the text says so.

**The corrections, made** (`sim/engine.py`): **(1)** `_summon_troops`'s award pass gathers the roots past w_A in one list in creation order — a real root on its conditions as before, a plant at β ≥ p_T (below it waits) — counts the free fundable members once, leaves the oldest `left` to the market whatever their kind, and sends the rest to the state: a real root to `_summon_delivery`, a plant to `_plant_floor_award` (the run-21 refusals — capacity, credit — then the floor at p_T and τ_T). `awards_waiting_now`, `members_free_now`, `awards_left_to_members` now count the plants in the queue, as the principle has it. **(2)** `_plant_draw`'s pool is the real roots posted so far while no period has closed (`_recent[key]` whole); the default serves only before the first real root.

**What moves.** Run 24's control on seed 21 (the hand's population) before → after: the plants at the floor 18.0% → **0.14%** (1 of 704) against the real roots' troop deliveries 0.04% → 0.0%; the plants' posted → awarded 0.38 → 0.33 against the real roots' 0.83 → 0.47 (the market itself changed: P_t 20.20 → 19.71, the awards 2,409 → 2,496, the plants 637 → 705 — more plants bid on, cheaper); awarded → submitted 1.08 → **1.00** against 1.00 (the first period's lag gone); submitted → seated 0.24 → 0.22 against 0.14 → 0.18; the raised share 49% → 27% against 46% → 28% (the ratio's effect: the plants post where the real roots post). One stage still reads apart on this seed — posted → awarded, −0.14 — to be read on the corrected chains with the standing stage table; a candidate is the requester's wallet: a real root posted below the market by a requester short of credit waits until it can raise, where a plant's raise is minted. The goldens re-pinned once (seed 1 b08ab798… / 9,738 entries, seed 2 22acc239… / 10,460; the previous pins in the file's comment); `test_v26` updated to the ruled behaviour (the floor tests put every actor on duty so no free member exists; the window tests read ratios) and three regressions added: a plant past w_A waits while a free fundable member exists and goes to the floor beyond the count, in the one list with a real root (the older is the market's whichever kind it is); the draws read the roots posted so far before the first close (the submission at the real deliveries' one tick, the ceiling at the ratio on B̄, not τ and P_t); the ceiling a ratio applied to the current B̄ (a root posted at 3× the market of 20 gives a plant at 3× the market of 50). The spec: §7.6 amended, Appendix E's E6, Appendix S's entry after run 34's addendum. **The suite:** 345 passed, 0 failed, 32 expected failures in all three modes (342 + the three regressions; `EXPECTED_FAIL` unchanged); run 24's control changes to the decimal (the goldens' re-pin), so the committed results of runs 20–34 are read against the uncorrected instrument from here, as the record keeps them.

**The standing check.** run_q's stage table (the plant's life by stage, plant − real paired) is written in every population cell's evaluation from here — the bidders' and the reviewers' (`write_life_table`; the `life_*` keys in `POP_KEYS`).

**Re-runs.** §49's two chains (the careful and the stamper basins) and (a) the disputes-on cell and (b) the half re-launched on the corrected instrument at `results/redteam/stability/learned-rev-{careful,stamper,disputes,half}-2/` — the record keeps the first readings at the original paths. The uncorrected (a) and (b) chains, launched under §50, run to their end and are read beside. Read in §52; 2.8.0 after.


## 52. The re-reads on the corrected instrument (October 26, 2026; `results/redteam/stability/learned-rev-{careful,stamper,disputes,half}-2/`, the first readings of (a) and (b) at `learned-rev-disputes/`, `learned-rev-half/`) — run 34's finding holds on the corrected instrument to the decimal; the dispute layer is captured with the audit layer; a learning half is dishonest by half as much; the plant's path matches a real root's within a period at every stage but the raise the wallet refuses

**What the record holds, exactly.** The two chains launched under §50 — (a) the disputes-on cell and (b) the half — ran their offline collection and their ten self-play rounds in one process each, started at 21:31 and 21:34 on October 25, and the corrections landed in the tree at 22:12 (`3d213bcb`); a process reads the engine once, so every round and the in-round evaluations (`eval/round-10/`, seeds 21–40, the careful and stamper references, no hand) ran on the uncorrected instrument, while the chains' final evaluations (`eval/final/`, the four references) started after their rounds ended and read the same learned policies in the corrected world. Both are kept and both are read below: the round-10 reading is the first reading of (a) and (b) proper; the final is the uncorrected policy on the corrected instrument. The four `-2` chains ran on the corrected instrument throughout. (The stage tables show the seam: the plants at the floor 0.130 and 0.314 in the round-10 readings against 0.003 and 0.022 in the finals; awarded → submitted 1.07–1.08 against 1.00.)

Two readings added to the standing tables for this section, both the driver's and run_q's only: the share of seated roots whose ceiling the requester's wallet **refused to raise** at least once, by case kind (`life_*_raise_refused_share`; `raise_below_market`'s refused branch — a plant's raise is minted, so a plant is never refused one), and the **dispute layer** in the reviewer population table (disputes filed / won, the false approvals disputed / overturned). The finals that ran before the addition lack the two columns; the raise diagnostic below carries them.

The half cell has its own basin: **care 0.70 / approve blind 0.29**, reached in the second period and held, the same from the uncorrected rounds, from the corrected chain and from the head alone on both (0.68 / 0.32, 0.71 / 0.29). The half is spent where the full population spent it — the plant-blind seats stamped 71–86%, the initial reviews 10–13%, plants and real alike — and the slots are the survivors of their cohort: 11.2 alive of 20.4 against the hand's 9.9 (the hand's stampers die; a learner stamps only where no plant comes and keeps its seat). Honesty against the hand on the same slots: the standing false approvals 27.7 against 11.6 (+16.0 ± 2.1 on the corrected chain; every seed), the bad plants approved 68 against 62, the suspensions *fewer* (10.5 against 14.0), the audit layer's overturn rate below the hand's again (0.039 against 0.060). Surplus and tasks: the direct's slots +2,300 nominal and **+2,480 real** against the hand (18,634 against 16,156 — in the half cell the two measures agree in sign against the hand), −690 ± 710 real against all-careful slots, the head alone's +620 nominal and −260 real against the hand (its slots die more: 9.3 alive); the customers' tasks per 1,000 at the hand's figure (33.0 against 32.7) and 6% under all-careful's; P_t 20.1 against 20.4. So a learning half beside a hand half settles less dishonest than a learning whole (0.29 stamped against 0.51; two and a half times the hand's false approvals against nine to ten) and the mechanism is the same seat: the plant-blind seats, where the hand half's careful auditors still overturn a tenth of what reaches them and the learners' audit seats confirm.

† the uncorrected direct's dispute counts are from the raise diagnostic's run on seeds 21–30 (`learned-rev-disputes/direct/eval/raise/`; its final ran before the dispute columns existed).

With the challenger outside the reviewers' population — every requester disputing a false approval it can see (§38: 89 of 92 caught in the hand's world; the hand's standing false approvals 3.6 here against 11.6 with disputes off) — the self-play population settles where §49's did: **care 0.53–0.60 / approve blind 0.40–0.47** (the direct 0.55 / 0.44 on the corrected chain, the head alone 0.60 / 0.40), the plant-blind seats stamped 87–92%, the initial reviews 18–30%. The standing false approvals 36–42 against the hand's 3.6 — **ten to twelve times**, every seed — the bad plants approved 103–155 against 66, the suspensions 20–26 against 16, the audit layer's overturn rate 0.006–0.008 against 0.013. **The dispute layer is reached by the same capture** (`learned-rev-disputes/direct/eval/raise/`, seeds 21–30, the dispute columns): the population's requesters file 105–152 disputes a run against the hand's 92 — there is more to contest — and win **69–75%** of them (the corrected chain's direct 112 of 150, its head alone 73 of 105), where the hand's win 89 of 92 (97%) and all-careful's 26 of 27; the 32–43 lost a run are the standing false approvals. A dispute is adjudicated by a panel drawn from the reviewers' population, and a panel seat is a plant-blind seat, stamped 91% — the challenger outside the population files truthfully, and the population it hands the case to confirms the approval. Run 34's finding holds with disputes on: the audit layer and the dispute layer are both captured through the seats a plant never reaches. Surplus and tasks: the members +2,100 to +3,400 nominal and −670 to −1,630 real against the hand (the corrected chain's direct 31,435 against 32,106 real), the customers' tasks per 1,000 −3 to −7% against the hand and −17 to −21% against all-careful; P_t 21.7–23.1 against 19.7 from 17–19 hands alive against 19.8; a requester's wallet 57–70 real against 118 and all-careful's 986.

### The stage table — the standing check after the corrections

**What the corrections closed.** Read across the finals (the corrected world) against the round-10 readings (the uncorrected): the plants at the floor 0.130 → 0.003 in the half cell's learned population against the real roots' troop deliveries 0.002, and 0.314 → 0.022 against 0.010 in the disputes cell's (the hand's 0.013 against 0.010; all-careful 0.004 against 0.005 and 0.001 against 0.001); awarded → submitted 1.07–1.08 → **1.00 against 1.00** in every cell and every reference (the first period's lag gone); the seen set — §49's probe carrier — from −0.013 / −0.036 share and −0.60 / −1.15 ticks since the sighting to −0.001 / +0.009 and +0.00 / +0.15 (the hand's −0.005 / −0.011, within two SE of nothing; all-careful −0.003 / +0.006); posted → awarded from −0.33 ± 0.06 and −0.55 ± 0.05 to −0.05 ± 0.02 and +0.14 ± 0.05 (the hand's −0.09 / −0.13, all-careful −0.13 / −0.09 — a plant is awarded a tenth of a tick sooner than a real root in the hand's and the careful worlds, later in the disputes cell's learned one); submitted → seated +0.00 to +0.03 (the hand's +0.02, the stamper reference's +0.07 / +0.21 — plants seated after a wait while the real roots' seats are the troops'). In the corrected basins (disputes off, 16 hands alive, the state at 3–7%) the plants reach the floor 6–13% against the real roots' troop deliveries 3–7% (the direct 0.063 against 0.034, the head alone 0.127 against 0.069): the pass is one list now, and the gap that is left is the summons' own refusals for a real root — the requester's pay-in in `_summon_delivery`, which a plant's floor award mints — read below on the all-stamper reference where it is largest.

**What remains, read pooled — the ten points re-read.** The pooled stage table still reads the plants raised more often than the real roots: +0.107 ± 0.009 and +0.089 ± 0.012 in the corrected basins (§50 read +0.100 before the erratum — erratum 2.6.2 moved it by nothing), +0.073 ± 0.011 in the corrected disputes cell, +0.010 to +0.026 in the half cell and the hand's world, −0.004 to +0.011 in all-careful; and in the corrected basins the plants are now awarded *later* than the real roots (+0.34 ± 0.06 and +0.24 ± 0.05; 1.68 against 1.34 ticks) and seen by more free members (+0.035 ± 0.006), where the hand's and the careful worlds read them a tenth of a tick sooner (−0.09, −0.07). Taken at face value these are stages that differ. They are not the plant's path.

**Read by period, the plant's life matches a real root's at every stage** (`post_ratio2.py`, seeds 21–23; the ceiling as posted over the market price the posting read, by period and kind; the hand's world, all-careful and the corrected basin's learned population):

|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | 2.750 / 0.012 / 0.013 | 3.269 / 0.000 / 0.000 | 157 (1,590) |
| 1 | 1.086 / 0.461 / 0.460 | 1.724 / 0.237 / 0.237 | 346 (1,416) |
| 2 | 0.725 / 0.739 / 0.743 | 0.741 / 0.708 / 0.714 | 503 (1,301) |
| 3 | 0.712 / 0.781 / 0.783 | 0.697 / 0.758 / 0.762 | 488 (1,246) |
| 4 | 0.667 / 0.792 / 0.788 | 0.647 / 0.796 / 0.798 | 490 (1,251) |
| pooled | 0.952 / 0.529 / 0.529 | 0.846 / 0.604 / 0.607 | 1,984 (6,804) |

Three things are in these tables. (i) The population's requesters post by the **wallet rule** (`--ceiling-policy wallet`, the default: the ceiling a share of the unlocked wallet, not a multiple of B̄), so the ceiling's ratio to the market swings with the requesters' wallets — 2.3 in the first period, 0.94 in the second as the first period's tasks are paid for, 1.6 as the wallets refill — and in the learned population, where the price is a half higher and the false approvals are paid for, it falls to 0.67 and stays there (a requester's wallet 55 real against the careful world's 1,100): four roots in five are posted below the market and raised. (ii) **Within a period, from the third on, the plants and the real roots agree to a point** — the ratio's median 1.125 against 1.127, posted below 0.430 against 0.418, raised 0.430 against 0.420 in the hand's third period; 0.741 / 0.708 / 0.714 against 0.725 / 0.739 / 0.743 in the learned basin's — and in the first two periods the plants lag the swing (the window is the last two periods' real roots: a plant in period 1 draws from period 0's ratios, 1.08 against 0.94; in the learned basin 1.72 against 1.09), which puts the plants *under* the real roots' raised share early. (iii) **The plant rate follows the dial**: as the population stamps, π_t rises and the plants go from 157 a period to 490 while the real roots go from 1,590 to 1,251, so the plants crowd into the periods where every root is raised — and a pooled plant − real difference reads that mix as a difference in the plant's path. The pooled raised share in the learned basin is 0.607 against 0.529; within periods 2–4 it is 0.714 / 0.762 / 0.798 against 0.743 / 0.783 / 0.788. The same composition carries the pooled posted → awarded and seen-set readings in the learned basins (the later periods have fewer hands alive and longer waits for every root), which is why their sign flipped against the hand's world. **The standing check is period-matched from here** (`8b26ebf6`): the driver keeps the stage counts by period and kind (`life_by_period`), and run_q writes under the stage table the plant − real difference with the real roots' stage means reweighted by the plants' count in each period; the pooled table stays beside it. Read on the corrected basin and the corrected disputes cell (`eval/stage-matched/`, seeds 21–40, the direct, the hand and all-careful), **period-matched the plants are raised 3–4 points *less* than the real roots** in the learned populations and the hand's world (−0.044 ± 0.004 and −0.030 ± 0.004; the hand's −0.027 ± 0.003 and −0.030 ± 0.005; all-careful +0.001 and −0.001) — the trailing window's lag under the wallets' swing, read above — **and awarded sooner** (−0.14 ± 0.05 and −0.15 ± 0.02; the hand's −0.16 ± 0.02 and −0.23 ± 0.02; all-careful −0.07 and −0.08), with awarded → submitted +0.00, submitted → seated −0.01 to +0.01 and the seen set −0.004 to +0.006 (the hand's −0.010 ± 0.002) — nothing of §49's +0.038. The pooled +0.107 and +0.34 were the mix. What the period-matched table leaves is one stage, posted → awarded, and its carrier is the next paragraph's.

**The one difference within a period — the raise the wallet refuses — a conformance candidate, not made.** The reading added for it (`life_*_raise_refused_share`): in the disputes cell's learned population 19.1% of the seated real roots had a raise refused by the requester's wallet at least once (`raise_ceiling` locks the difference from the wallet), 15.7% in the hand's world, 4.7% in all-careful's — and 0.0% of the plants in every one, because a plant's raise is minted (2.6.1). A refused real root is raised later nearly always (98.8% in the hand's world, 99.6% awarded by the end) — so the refusal does not change whether a root is raised, only when: the refused real roots wait **2.60 ticks** to their award against 0.46 for the unrefused and 0.60 for a plant (`raise_fate.py`, seeds 21–23; all-careful 1.63 / 0.08 / 0.06; the learned population 2.26 / 0.43 / 0.69). So within a period a plant is awarded a tenth of a tick sooner than a real root in the hand's world (the pooled −0.09 ± 0.01) — the ninth root in a hundred that waits two ticks for its requester's wallet is never a plant. Under the stated principle (at every step a plant takes the step in the pass a real root would, its parameters drawn from the trailing real roots' at that step), the candidate is the raise refused as the real roots' are: the plant requester's raise drawn — refused in the pass with the probability the trailing real roots' raises were refused in theirs, and granted at the real roots' delay — the window's ratio kept. The size of it is a tenth of a tick of the award and a tell within the convention ("a root that waits two ticks below the market is a real root") that Q2's probe has not read; the plant's disguise is otherwise whole at every stage the instrument reads. Ruled first, as every plant-path correction has been. Beside it, a reading on the degenerate reference only: in the all-stamper world (the members die, the state at 26%) the plants reach the floor 37.6% against the real roots' troop deliveries 25.6% — the summons for a real root has refusals a plant's floor award has not (the requester's pay-in in `_summon_delivery`; a plant's is minted); invisible in every living world (0.001–0.063 against 0.001–0.038).

### What this adds to run 34's finding

- **The finding's scope.** Four cells, two learners, two instruments: no self-play reviewer population settles honest. Every member learning stamps 44–51% of its reviews and passes eight to twelve times the hand's false approvals whether disputes are on or off; half the members learning stamps 28–32% and passes two and a half times the hand's. The dishonesty scales with the learners' share of the seats that judge them — the audit layer in every cell, the dispute layer where disputes are on — and nowhere else: the initial reviews are stamped 7–38% while the plant-blind seats are stamped 72–97% in every learned cell, with 23–31% of the review decisions on them. The equilibrium is the same from a careful start and a stamper start, from the direct and the head alone, and it is reached by the third period.
- **For 2.8.0's draft.** The seats the revision names are the seats the readings name: the revised submission, the panel (an appeal's, a dispute's) and the audit seat. The dispute-layer reading says what a challenger outside the population buys without it — 69–75% of its disputes where the hand's wins 97% — so the planted appeal and the planted dispute are of a piece with the audit of a plant.
- **The two measures.** The members' nominal surplus and their real lifetime disagree in sign against the hand in every all-member cell (+2,100 to +3,400 nominal, −670 to −3,100 real: the price is a half higher and there are a sixth fewer hands) and agree for the direct in the half cell (+2,300 / +2,480: the slots there are the survivors of a cohort the hand's stampers leave). Both stay in the tables, as ruled.
- **The standing check.** The stage table is read period-matched from here (`8b26ebf6`), the pooled table beside it; on the corrected instrument the plant's life matches a real root's within a period at awarded → submitted, submitted → seated and the seen set in every cell, lags it by 3–4 points of the raised share in the first two periods (the window), and leads it by a tenth to a quarter of a tick to the award — the raise the wallet refuses a real root and never a plant, the one conformance candidate, for the ruling. Q2's probe is not re-run here: the seen-set difference it read (§49) reads −0.004 to +0.006 on the corrected instrument; it can be re-run on the `-2` chains' round-10 collections if the ruling wants the number.
- **(iii)** stays held; nothing of it built.

**2.8.0 is the reviewer's to draft.**


## 53. The ruling after run 35: the raise refused, and the probe on the learned population's collection (October 26, 2026; `b1f07ee6`; `results/redteam/stability/learned-rev-careful-2/probe-raise/`, `learned-rev-careful-2/direct/eval/raise-corrected/`, `results/redteam/stability/raise-correction/`)

**The ruling** (verbatim): "Run 35 recorded; the finding stands to the decimal on the corrected instrument; the dispute layer's capture recorded with it; the half as the dose–response; the first (a)/(b) readings kept with the seam stated. The raise conformance item adopted: the plant requester's raise refused at the trailing real requesters' rate and granted at their delay; then one probe read on the learned population's collection for the floor under stress. 2.8.0 to follow on Baruchi's go; §8 will be this run's four chains on 2.8.0 against 2.7.0, honesty first."

**Headline.** The raise correction is made in the instrument (the driver's `raise_below_market`; no rule moves) and closes the one within-period difference §52 left: on run 24's world the refused share plant − real goes −0.137 → +0.019 and posted → awarded −0.087 → +0.017 (ten seeds, paired), with nothing else moving outside noise; in the learned population the period-matched posted → awarded goes −0.14 → +0.02 ± 0.02. Q2's probe on the learned population's collection on the corrected instrument reads the gap raw − context at **+0.012 ± 0.002**, from §49's +0.032 / +0.036, against a floor of **+0.002 ± 0.001** on the same cell's ε set (§49's +0.005): the seen set's carrier (`seen_ticks_since`, +0.017 / +0.020 in §49) adds +0.0003 now, and what remains above the floor — 0.010, 2.6.1's single-slot figure — is the trailing window's lag on the plant's ceiling and the seating gap's 2.6.1 figure. Honesty is where §52 left it: care 0.52 / approve blind 0.46, the false approvals 7.9 times the hand's.

### The correction (`b1f07ee6`; Appendix S's entry after run 35; §7.6's raise clause)

A real requester's raise is refused when its wallet cannot lock the difference (`raise_ceiling`'s `LedgerError`) and retried every tick until it can; a plant's was minted (2.6.1) and never refused, so the ninth root in a hundred that waited two ticks below the market was never a plant (§52). The correction is the instrument's, where the posting step's was after run 24: the driver keeps a window of the real roots' **first raise attempts** — refused or granted, and for the refused the ticks from the first refused attempt to the grant — and reads it as the engine's `_plant_draw` reads its (the last k entries, k the count the last `trailing_periods` closed periods hold; everything so far while those hold none, as after run 34's addendum). At a plant's first attempt in the pass its raise is drawn refused with the window's rate (`Task.raise_first_t`, `raise_refused_t`, `plant_raise_at`; the draws on their own stream, `raise_rng`, so no other draw moves); a refused plant's raise is held and retried each tick, counted as a real root's refusals are (`raise_refused_n`, so `life_plant_raise_refused_share` reads the rate), and granted at the drawn delay — the difference minted as before. Before any real raise a plant's is not refused (the real roots' rate is 0 so far); before any refused real root is granted the delay is one tick (the least a retry takes). The row carries `plant_raises_refused`, `plant_raise_delay_mean`, `raise_refusal_rate_window`. The engine is unchanged, the goldens do not move, no reviewer test pins the driver; the suite in three modes at `b1f07ee6`: 345 passed, 0 failed, 32 expected failures.

**Run 24's world, ten seeds paired against the committed driver** (`raise-correction/hand-10seeds.md`): the plants whose raise was refused 0 → 15% (the real roots 13%; the window's rate at the end 0.38, the drawn delay 1.48 ticks); plant − real on the refused share −0.137 → +0.019, on posted → awarded −0.087 → +0.017; the raised share −0.03 for both kinds; P_t +0.63 ± 0.75, the members +0.2 ± 0.4, the false approvals +0.6 ± 1.5, the awards +3 ± 27, the plants posted −29 ± 18 (1.6 SE; the plant rate follows the dial), awarded → submitted and submitted → seated unchanged. One seed (21) diverges through membership — 18 members against 21 from the first hold at the second period's boundary, P_t 23.7 against 19.7 — as a single run of this world does under any perturbation; the ten say nothing systematic moves.

### The learned population on the corrected driver (`direct/eval/raise-corrected/`; §49's basin, the direct's round-10 model, seeds 21–40, against careful and hand)

**The stage table.** Pooled, the plants are now refused their raise *more* often than the real roots in the learned population (0.352 against 0.279) and awarded later (1.99 against 1.44 ticks, +0.55 ± 0.03): the composition §52 read — the plant rate follows the dial into the late periods, where the requesters' wallets are drained and seven raises in ten are refused — now carries the plant's wait too, since the plant waits with the refusal as a real root does. | direct (learned) | **+0.02 ± 0.02** | −0.14 / −0.15 | −0.050 ± 0.004 | −0.028 ± 0.004 | −0.02 ± 0.00 | −0.004 ± 0.004 |

The award gap closes in the learned population and halves to a third in the hand's and the careful's. What is left of it sits with the two shares the trailing window lags: the plants are raised 2–5 points less often and refused 1–3 points less often than the real roots of the same period — the window reads the last k roots' ratios and refusals while the requesters' wallets drain within the period (the refusal rate rises through the run as the ceiling's ratio to the market falls, §52) — and a root raised or refused less often is awarded a little sooner. The window's length against the trend is the reading §50 named (`--trailing-periods`; the knob is the reviewer's); it is the plant's draw by design, not a step the plant skips, and I propose nothing on it.

### The probe on the learned population's collection (`probe-raise/`)

**The collection.** One round of the learned population on the corrected instrument — the direct's round-10 model of the `-2` chain as the policy, ε 0.1 uniform as the chains' rounds were, seeds 1–20 (`collect_pop.py`: `online.py`'s round without its fit and its eval; 27,958 initial reviews on seeds 1–10, 28,304 on 11–20, the plants 22.2% of them) — and beside it, as the floor, the same cell's hand-written ε set collected afresh on the corrected instrument (`collect --policies eps`, seeds 1–20: an all-careful population with ε on the same forty slots, 30,416 / 30,335 initial reviews, the plants 14.8%). Q2's probe on each, the initial reviews only, trained on seeds 1–10 and read on 11–20, with the per-field ablation; the latent column is the `-2` world model's (`wm/models/wm-online-10.pt`, trained before the correction — a reading on a stale latent; the raw, the context and the case's fields are model-free). My reading of "the floor under stress": the gap raw − context in the population that stresses the instrument (the dial at π 0.3, a sixth of the members suspended, the plants a fifth of the initial reviews, the wallets swinging), read against the ε set's.

| probe (AUC; SE over seeds) | §49: the population (wm chain, round 10) | §49: the ε set | **now: the population (direct, round 11, corrected)** | **now: the ε set (corrected)** |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| raw observation | 0.744 ± 0.006 | 0.665 ± 0.012 | 0.644 ± 0.003 | 0.656 ± 0.009 |
| context alone | 0.712 ± 0.004 | 0.660 ± 0.013 | 0.632 ± 0.004 | 0.654 ± 0.009 |
| the case's fields alone | 0.669 ± 0.009 | 0.560 ± 0.010 | 0.609 ± 0.003 | 0.530 ± 0.012 |
| **gap raw − context** | **+0.032 ± 0.003** | **+0.005 ± 0.001** | **+0.012 ± 0.002** | **+0.002 ± 0.001** |
| `seen_ticks_since` added to the context | +0.017 ± 0.002 | +0.000 | +0.0003 ± 0.0003 | — |
| `seen_before` added | +0.008 ± 0.002 | +0.000 | < 0.0003 | +0.0001 |
| `seen_beta_rel` added (removed from the raw) | +0.003 ± 0.001 | −0.000 | +0.0019 ± 0.0007 (−0.0074) | +0.0007 ± 0.0004 (−0.0013) |
| `rv_gap` added | +0.0025 ± 0.0009 | +0.0026 ± 0.0005 | +0.0025 ± 0.0008 | +0.0009 ± 0.0006 |
| latent | 0.695 | 0.636 | 0.626 | 0.637 |

**Reading.** The gap falls to a third — +0.012 ± 0.002 from +0.032 / +0.036 — and the field that carried it in §49 is gone: `seen_ticks_since` (the ticks from a free member's sighting of the case to its seat) adds +0.0003 where it added +0.017 / +0.020, and `seen_before` nothing readable; the stage table says the same (the seen share −0.004 ± 0.004, the ticks since the sighting matched within the period). The gap is not at the floor: 0.010 ± 0.002 above the ε set's +0.002 (five seed-level SE), and it is 2.6.1's single-slot figure (+0.011 ± 0.002, §17) read again on the population's data. Two fields carry what is left, both small: `rv_gap`, the seating gap (+0.0025, the same figure in §49's population and §49's ε set — the review's assignment tick less the submission's, which the plant's engine-side fallback and the seating order leave at a reading the probe has had since 2.6.1), and `seen_beta_rel`, the ceiling as first seen relative to P_0 — the field whose removal costs the raw probe the most (−0.0074) and that adds +0.0019 to the context: a plant's ceiling is the trailing ratio applied to the current market, a real root's the wallet rule at posting, so under this population's swing of the ceiling's ratio (2.3 → 0.94 → 0.67 across the periods, §52) the plant's ceiling lags the real roots' by the window — the same lag the period-matched raised share (−0.050) and refused share (−0.028) read. The raw and the context both read a tenth lower than §49's (0.644 / 0.632 against 0.744 / 0.712): this is the direct's population where §49's was the head alone's, and a collection one round later; the two populations' contexts separate plant from real differently, and the comparison the ruling asked for is the gap, which is paired within each collection. The ε set's own gap also fell (+0.005 → +0.002) and its raw and context did not move: the corrected instrument is cleaner for the hand's population too.

### What this adds

- **The raise item closed.** The plant takes the raise step with the timing a real root's wallet gives it; the one within-period difference §52 found is gone in the learned population (posted → awarded +0.02 ± 0.02) and reduced to the window's lag in the hand's and the careful's. Nothing else in run 24's world moves outside noise over ten seeds.
- **The probe under stress.** The gap raw − context on the learned population's collection is +0.012 ± 0.002, a third of §49's, the seen set no longer its carrier; 0.010 above the ε floor, carried by the trailing window's lag on the ceiling (by design; the window's length the reviewer's knob, §50) and the seating gap at its 2.6.1 figure. In my reading the instrument's within-period disguise is as whole as the window lets it be; I propose no further correction, and the standing checks (the stage table period-matched, the probe on request) keep their place.
- **The finding.** Run 34's basin on the corrected driver to the decimal: care 0.52 / approve blind 0.46, the plant-blind seats stamped 91%, the false approvals 7.9 times the hand's, the overturn rate below the hand's. §8 of 2.8.0 has its 2.7.0 side here.
- **(iii)** stays held; nothing of it built.

**2.8.0 to follow on Baruchi's go — the reviewer's draft.**

## 54. §8.2 on the 2.8.0 side (October 26, 2026; `results/redteam/stability/learned-rev-{careful,stamper,disputes,half}-280/`, the derived tables at `results/redteam/stability/2.8.0-s82/`) — the capture closes by more than half and the audit and dispute layers answer as the hand's do, but the population is not honest: three times the hand's false approvals stand, and the audit seat is still stamped three times in four

**What ran.** Run 35's four self-play chains on the 2.8.0 engine (`fef85faa`; `Params.plants_every_seat` on, the default), the recipe of the `-2` chains to the flag: the offline collections (careful-weighted for the careful basin, stamper-weighted for the stamper basin, with disputes on, with half the slots), ten rounds of self-play for the direct (the carrier) and for the world model (the head alone beside), the finals at seeds 21–40 with the `careful`, `stamper` and `hand` references on the same slots. Paired against the `-2` chains' finals — the same seeds, the same references, the same policies' class, on 2.7.0 (§52–§53) — the rule the only difference between the two sides. The rounds settled by round 5 and did not move after (the careful basin's direct: care 0.686 / approve blind 0.306 at round 5, 0.681 / 0.311 at round 10 and in the final; the head alone 0.687 / 0.313 and 0.690 / 0.310; the other chains the same within 0.03); each chain 82–195 minutes.

Paired by seed, 2.8.0 − 2.7.0, the learners (the direct · the head alone): the standing false approvals **−59.7 ± 5.8 · −57.2 ± 3.7** in the careful basin, −55.0 ± 5.3 · −56.4 ± 4.4 in the stamper basin, −29.2 ± 2.7 · −28.8 ± 2.3 with disputes on, −5.0 ± 2.4 · −9.3 ± 1.8 in the half; the care share +0.168 ± 0.011 · +0.159 ± 0.011, +0.172 · +0.146, **+0.003 ± 0.008 · −0.004 ± 0.011** (disputes on: the mix did not move), +0.025 ± 0.008 · +0.103 ± 0.013; the bad plants approved −34 ± 6 · −38 ± 3 in the basins (−5 ± 7 and +5 ± 3 in the other two); the audit layer's overturn rate +0.010 ± 0.005 · +0.016 ± 0.004 in the basins (to the hand's; nothing with disputes on); the disputes won +16.9 ± 5.6 · +26.2 ± 5.3; the members' nominal surplus −3,322 ± 537 · −4,042 ± 347 in the careful basin and −4,152 · −3,954 in the stamper's (+970 ± 563 · −373 ± 400 with disputes on; −928 ± 485 · +291 ± 686 in the half); the customers' tasks per 1,000 +0.4 ± 0.5 · +0.9 ± 0.2 and +0.9 · +0.5 in the basins, −0.8 ± 0.4 in both disputes-on learners, −0.3 · −0.6 in the half.

**The reading.** 2.8.0 moves the self-play basin and does not make it honest.

- **The basins agree, and they moved together.** From a careful start and a stamper start the direct settles on **care 0.68 / approve blind 0.31–0.32** and the head alone on 0.69–0.70 / 0.30–0.31 (2.7.0: 0.50–0.55 / 0.45–0.47); the standing false approvals **38–39 against the hand's 11.4 — three and a half times — from 94–98, eight to eight and a half times**; the head alone 30–31, two and a half to three times. Every seed of twenty above the hand in both basins, as before. The dial's F̂ 0.21 from 0.29–0.30 against F* 0.10.
- **The audit layer's capture ends; the audit seat's stamping does not.** The overturn rate is the hand's — 0.060–0.064 against 0.062 (2.7.0: 0.045–0.049, below it with eight times the false approvals before it) — and still the audit seats are stamped **77–90% by the direct and 98% by the head alone**, plants and real cases alike. The hypothesis read the audit seat's stamp rate falling to the first review's and under; it fell by a tenth in the direct and not at all in the head alone. What fell was the **initial seat's** stamping (0.38 → 0.25 of the real cases, 0.31 → 0.22 of the plants) and the panels' (91–97% → 13–38% in the two basins, where a panel is an appeal's or the third seat an audit's disagreement opens — the view's question reads "appeal" for both, and the by-question table below separates them). The false approvals that stand are the real cases stamped at the first review whose audit the population stamps too — fewer first-review stamps, the same confirmation behind them.
- **Where the plant's price landed — on the odds, read at the initial seat.** The population's stamping was Q1's sleeper by standing and odds (§49): care while pinned and when the odds are high, a stamp when they are low. Under 2.8.0 a stamp at an audit seat on a bad plant is a failure — the bond burned, the odds doubled, the record down — and the plants are 14% of the audit seats (1,322 of 9,623 in the careful basin's direct) with 43% of the plants reaching an audit bad (111 bad approved against 146 good approved), so **five to six audit stamps in a hundred fail** where none did. The learner's answer is read in the odds bands: the stamp rate in the 0.7–1.0 band 0.66 → 0.37 (the head alone 0.64 → 0.38), pinned 0.36 → 0.28; the stamps a slot makes as a member 1,509 → 898 a run (the head alone 1,473 → 776), the odds at its stamps 0.858 → 0.904, **the false approvals it passes per suspension 5.6 → 3.4** (§49's "six to seven a suspension" — the plants holding the market alive — is three and a half now). The price reached the seat where the odds are read, which is the first review; at the audit seat itself a stamp still costs six in a hundred of a bond against a certain two or three credits of care, and the population keeps stamping it. The dial's π_t sets that fraction (0.21 here; the plants' share of audit seats follows the plants' share of pending approvals); the rc's own red team of §8.7 — the dial pinned at its floor — is the test of the lever, and the arithmetic says what it will read: a plant prices a seat in proportion to π_t β_P and the seat's penalty, and at the audit seat on the defaults that price is under the price of care. [Erratum, November 6, 2026 (§72): care within the cap K is bought and reimbursed from the allowance in the same call (1.1.0 §4.3; the engine conforms), so it has no price in money — the comparison is restated in §72: the stamp saves nothing and carries the larger expected bond loss.] Recorded as a reading; the classification is the reviewer's (§19: an incentive that looks wrong under fixed rules is an evaluation).
- **The dispute layer answers as the hand's.** With disputes on, the population's requesters win **95% (the direct: 129 of 135) and 98% (the head alone: 99 of 102)** of the disputes they file, against the hand's 96% and 2.7.0's 69–75%; the standing false approvals 11.2 and 7.2 against the hand's 4.0 — two and three quarters and one and three quarters times, from eleven and ten times — and the head alone is above the hand on 15 seeds of 20, the first learned cell in the stability track not above it on every seed. **The mix did not move** (care 0.55 / 0.60 on both sides of the rule; approve blind −0.04 · −0.03 and reject blind +0.04 · +0.03): the population stamps the first reviews as before (0.27 / 0.22 of the real cases) and the audits as before (0.87 / 0.91); what changed is the panel, where the challenger outside the population now wins.

**The seats by their question** (seeds 21–23; each chain's round-10 direct policy run through the environment on the 2.8.0 engine, the action tallied by the view's question, the case's kind and its truth; `2.8.0-s82/panel-by-question.txt`). The seat table's "panel" pools the appeal's panel, the dispute's panel and the third seat (the view's question is "appeal" for a challenge of the audit's kind); read apart:

  At a dispute's panel the 2.8.0 population **rejects blind 55–57% and cares 34–39%, stamping 5–11%** — the 2.7.0 chain's policy stamps 48–52% there and rejects blind 26–44% — and at an appeal's panel it stamps 58% (the 2.7.0 policy 51–53%): **it sides with the challenger**, which is why the disputes are won (both panelists stamping, the one way a truthful dispute is lost, 0.11² of the real dispute panels against 0.52² before) and the appeals too. At the third seat — opened when a careful auditor has just rejected a stamped approval, so the case before it is bad three times in four — it cares 76–85%. At the audit seat it stamps 85–92% whatever the case, in both cells, and the by-truth rows say why the price never reached it: **with disputes on no bad plant reaches an audit** — the plant requester disputes a bad plant's pending F first, at the real requesters' rate, which is one and truthful, exactly as the real requesters leave the audit seat nothing but good work (12 bad real cases against 2,975 good) — so a stamp at the audit seat is free and right there; with disputes off 75 bad plants reach it beside 120 good and 1,255 real, the population stamps them at 85% and pays for it (22 audit failures and 3.3 third-seat failures a run against 74 at the initial seat in the hand's world), cheaper than caring at some 480 audit seats a run [Erratum, November 6, 2026 (§72): struck — caring within the cap costs nothing in money; §72]. The plants' challenges are truthful by construction — the plant actor appeals only a good plant's N and the plant requester disputes only a bad plant's F, each at the real population's rate — so **a panelist that sides with the challenger is never failed by a plant**; in every standing cell the real challengers are truthful too (the drivers' `truthful` policies), so the heuristic reads as honesty and wins the disputes. A population whose challengers lie — an actor appealing every N, a requester disputing every F — would meet panels that side with it. The instrument mirrors the real challengers' truthfulness by the stated principle (at every step the plant takes the step at the trailing real rate), so this is not a conformance item; it is a reading for §8.7's red team — the plant prices a panel only against the challenges the population files. Not drafted.
- **The half cell.** Care 0.74 / 0.81 from 0.72 / 0.71, the standing false approvals 22.6 / 18.4 from 27.6 / 27.7 (−5.0 ± 2.4 · −9.3 ± 1.8) against the hand's 11.4 — two times and 1.6 times, from 2.4; the audit seats stamped 72% by the direct and 46–49% by the head alone (the one learner that cares at the audit seat half the time); the slots the survivors of their cohort as before (10.6 alive of 20.4 against the hand's 10.2), +897 ± 394 nominal and +1,776 ± 593 real against the hand.
- **The dishonest basin stops paying the members where disputes are off.** In both basins the learners' nominal surplus is now *under* the hand's (−1,221 ± 374 and −2,177 ± 385 for the direct; 2.7.0 read +2,765 to +3,377) and their real lifetime −3,100 to −4,500 under it; the customers' tasks per 1,000 −1.1 to −2.4 against the hand's (−5 to −8%; 2.7.0 −10 to −14%). With disputes on the learners keep +3,400 nominal against the hand (−1,700 to −3,800 real; P_t +3.6 ± 0.8 — a sixth fewer hands alive, 16–18 against 20). The two measures disagree in sign in every all-member cell, as in §52, and agree in the half.

**Hypothesis 1, read against its text.** Confirmed: the basin test (both starts, both learners, one place); the care share past the hand's 0.6 (0.68–0.70, where disputes are off); the disputes won back at the hand's (95–98% from 69–75%); the audit layer's overturn rate back *to* the hand's (not above it). Not confirmed: the audit seats' stamp rate did not fall to the first review's (77–98% against 22–26%); the standing false approvals did not fall within a factor of two of the hand's in any all-member cell (2.6–3.5× with disputes off, 1.8–2.8× with disputes on; the half 1.6–2.0×). Run 34's finding stands under 2.8.0 at a third of its size: a self-play reviewer population is not honest, the seat it stamps is the audit seat, and the price a plant puts on that seat is π_t's.

### Hypothesis 2 ahead of §8.3 — the honest populations, paired across the rule (the finals' `hand` and `careful` references, seeds 21–40; the rows at seeds 21–23 in `2.8.0-s82/hand-3seeds.txt`)
