The hand's honesty does not move (false approvals −0.2 ± 0.8; the overturn rate +0.002). Its stampers are suspended **2.5 ± 1.1 times more a run**: they fail at the seats they never reached before (the rows: 6.0 failures a run at the plants' audit seats, 2.3 at the third seat, 0.3 at the appeal panel, beside 74 at the initial seat). And the hand's world pays for the plants' seats in work: the awards **−121 ± 33 (−5%)**, the customers' tasks per 1,000 **−1.2 ± 0.4 (−4%)**, the state's share of deliveries +1.5 ± 0.5 points — the members' reviewing time taken by the plants' later seats (the plants 17.7% of all reviews in the rows: 617 initial seats, 118 audit, 33 third, 15 appeal a run; the real roots' troop seats 0.316 a root from 0.293). All-careful: the awards −19 ± 16, tasks per 1,000 −1.0 ± 0.5 (two SE), nothing else; with disputes on the hand reads nothing outside noise and all-careful's real lifetime +2,547 ± 890, carried by P_t −0.6 ± 0.4 and a nominal +944 ± 644, each within 1.5 SE. **The plants per period do not rise** (the rows: 622 posted a run against 621; 596 against 610 with disputes on) and **the seats on them do**: 1.27 adjudicated seats a plant (the initial seat and 0.27 later ones — fewer than a real root's later seats, since three plants in four are bad and a careful first reviewer rejects them: the plants' pending-F share 0.35 against the real roots' 0.80), the state's verdict on **0.17 of the plants** (105 a run; the real roots' troop seats 0.32 a root — the same seat shortage, read per pending F alike), the pots topped up 141–157 nominal a run (the third seat's need), the court fee destroyed 17 a run with disputes off (the plant actor's 8 appeals) and 134 with disputes on (the plant requester's 60 disputes beside the real requesters' 92), no plant revised or spent (no P or ⊥ in any cell, as the record said). **The cost of truth rises 23–29%**: the plants' minted pots less what returned 3,214 against 2,490 nominal a run (3,045 against 2,483 with disputes on) — the pots were minted whole at posting before and mostly destroyed at closure; the later seats spend them (destroyed −430 to −600, minted +120 to +450 in top-ups). Ω's fee income and net position within noise on three seeds (+12 and −74; +189 and +3); I42 holds in every run (the final audit).

### Hypothesis 3 ahead of §8.4 — the stage check at every seat (the finals' stage tables, period-matched; `2.8.0-s82/finals-paired.md`)

The stages before the first verdict read as §53 left them: period-matched, posted → awarded +0.05 ± 0.02 in the careful basin's direct (the hand's −0.12 ± 0.02, all-careful's −0.11 ± 0.02), awarded → submitted +0.00, submitted → seated −0.02 ± 0.00 (the hand's −0.03), the raised share −0.049 ± 0.004 (the hand's −0.052), the raise refused −0.024 ± 0.003 (the hand's −0.013), the seen set +0.005 ± 0.004 (the hand's −0.007 ± 0.003). The stages after it, by kind: the filing delay 0.00 / 0.00 in every cell (the drivers' requesters dispute in the tick the pending F is seen and the plant requester with them; the appeal delay drawn from the real actors' window reads zero in every cell); the audit seat's wait from the roll 0.56 / 0.59 ticks in the careful basin's direct (the hand's 0.11 / 0.14, all-careful 0.36 / 0.42), the first panel seat's wait 0.60 / 0.54 (the hand's 0.07 / 0.10); the state's seats 0.709 / 0.404 a root in the learned basin, 0.323 / 0.174 in the hand's world. **The per-root shares differ by construction, not by path**: the audited share 0.407 / 0.196 in the hand's world and the state's seats 0.323 / 0.174 are per root, and three plants in four are bad where one real root in five is — a plant reaches a pending F 0.35 of the time against a real root's 0.80. Per pending F the rows read the plant's audit roll at the real root's: **0.79 obligations per plant F (166 of 210) against 0.84 per real F (1,682 of 2,005)**; the plant's filed share with disputes on (0.116 against 0.072 a root) is the same construction (a bad plant's F is the disputable event). The stage table's post-verdict columns stay per root (the driver's), with this caveat on the record; a per-pending-F reading is a division the rows allow.

**The stamp rates by seat kind, plant against real** (pooled over the seeds and the periods): at the initial seat the plants stamped 1–4 points less than the real cases in every learner (−3.1, −3.0, −1.7, −0.6 for the direct; −4.0, −3.2, −1.5, −1.3 for the head alone; §52 read −7 points under 2.7.0); at the audit seat **within 4 points** (−4.4, −1.4, +1.7, −1.9; the head alone −0.1, −0.5, +0.9, −3.2); at the panels −11.5 and −13.4 points in the two basins' direct, −1.4 with disputes on, −6.3 in the half (the head alone −2.4, −0.8, −13.6, +4.0). The panel differences are pooled over the periods in a basin where the plant rate follows the dial (§52's composition) and over the appeal's panel, the dispute's panel and the third seat, on 240–510 plant seats a cell (the by-question table above reads the plants and the real cases within 6 points at every one of the three); a period-matched seat table is the instrument if the ruling wants the number — not built. The probe at every seat (H3's second half) waits on §8.4.

### What this adds

- **Run 36: 2.8.0 read on §8.2.** The plants at every seat cut the self-play population's standing false approvals by 60% (−57 to −60 a run in the two basins, −29 with disputes on), restored the audit layer's overturn rate and the dispute layer's win rate to the hand's, and moved the basin from care 0.50 to 0.68 — from two starts and two learners alike. They did not make it honest: 2.6–3.5 times the hand's false approvals stand with disputes off, 1.8–2.8 with disputes on, 1.6–2.0 in the half, on 15–20 seeds of 20; the audit seat is stamped 77–98% by every all-member learner. The plant priced the audit seat at π_t β_P of a bond — five to six stamps in a hundred — and the population read the price where the odds are read, at the first review, and kept the seat. By question it learned to side with the challenger (a dispute's panel rejected blind 55%, an appeal's stamped 58%, the third seat cared 76–85%) — never failed by a plant, whose challenges are truthful by construction as the population's are; a reading for §8.7.
- **The honest populations pay for the seats** in the members' reviewing time: −5% awards, −4% tasks per credit, +1.5 points to the state on the hand's world; the cost of truth +23–29%; honesty unmoved; nothing to Ω.
- **The plant's path holds at the new seats per pending F**; the per-root stage columns carry β_P, stated.
- **For the ruling.** The lever the arithmetic names is π_t (the rc's §8.7 red team, the dial pinned at its floor, is the test from below); whether the price at the audit seat is a reading or a rule is §19's question and the reviewer's. §8.3–§8.7 in the rc's order next: §8.1 (the worked example is `test_v28`), the honest populations on run 24's entry cell, the stage check with the probe at every seat, the lone learners (Q1's line), the cost of truth, the red team.


## 55. The ruling after run 36 (October 26, 2026): an evaluation under fixed rules, the arithmetic on record, then §8.3–§8.7 in the rc's order with three cells in §8.7

**The ruling, verbatim.**

> Run 36 is read and recorded. Hypothesis 1: the basin test, the care share and the disputes-won clauses hold; the audit-seat and the factor-of-two clauses do not; the overturn rate back to the hand's and not above it is the population's better first reviews and its stamping auditors cancelling — on record. 2.8.0 closed the two layers and did not close the seat; the stability track's finding stands at a third of its size.
>
> §19: the stamping of a seat priced under the price of care is an evaluation under fixed rules, recorded against the rules as run 34's was; the rule question is decided by cells, not arithmetic. The arithmetic goes on record as you gave it, in this form: the share of audit stamps that fail is π β_P s / [π(1−β_P+β_P s) + (1−π) f_R] — 0.054 at π 0.21, s 0.25 — against the first review's β_P π, which does not depend on s. The audit seat is priced at about a third of the first review's price and the factor is the first review's own stamp rate, which is what the plants drive down. The dial is bounded by construction, not only by its cap, and blind to the seat (F̂ at the audit seat 0.85 beside the dial's 0.21 — state both in the record).
>
> §8.3–§8.7 in the rc's order. §8.7 carries three cells: (a) the dial pinned at its floor, as drafted; (b) the dial pinned at π_max 0.40, the mirror — a diagnostic, not a rule (expected: the audit seat's stamp rate moves by less than the first review's as the bad plants reaching it thin out; the cost of truth near double; if the seat is priced at the cap, the item is the dial's reading — every adjudicated seat in F̂ — and nothing more); (c) untruthful challengers — the self-play population under 2.8.0 with the drivers' real challengers lying at a stated rate, an actor appealing bad work's N and a requester disputing good work's F (expected: the panels side with the liars). Your reading that the plant's truthful challenges are the stated principle and not a conformance item is accepted.
>
> Not drafted, named for after §8.7: if (b) does not price the audit seat, the item is the plant's construction at that seat — the planted approval, a bad plant whose first verdict is the orchestrator's own F reaching the audit roll at the dial's rate; its difficulty is the disguise of the approving name (a population that cares on state-reviewed audits and stamps on member-reviewed ones is captured by half). If (c) captures the panels, the item is the untruthful plant challenge at the rate of lost real challenges, which the orchestrator knows. Each minor by §19.
>
> H2 and H3 recorded as read: the cost of the seats is the honest populations' reviewing time and +23–29% on the cost of truth, honesty unmoved, nothing to Ω; the hand's stampers' 2.5 extra suspensions are the rule at the seats they never reached; the plant's path holds per pending F, the per-root columns carry the caveat. The period-matched seat table is not built — the by-question table reads within 6 points at every seat. The probe at every seat with §8.4.

*[Erratum, November 6, 2026 (§72): the ruling's premise "a seat priced under the price of care" is void — care within the cap is reimbursed in the same call, so the stamp saves nothing in money; the share of failing stamps below stands as a number, the comparison it was set against is restated in §72, and the ruling's classification — an evaluation under fixed rules, the rule question decided by cells — stands.]*

**On record, as ruled.** *The arithmetic.* The share of the audit seat's stamps that fail is π β_P s / [π(1 − β_P + β_P s) + (1 − π) f_R] — π the plant rate per real initial review, β_P the bad share (0.75), s the first review's stamp rate (the share of bad plants whose first verdict is F), f_R the real cases' pending-F rate — **0.054 at π 0.21, s 0.25**; against it the first review's price β_P π = 0.158, which does not depend on s. The audit seat is priced at about a third of the first review's price, and the factor is s, the first review's own stamp rate — the number the plants drive down, so the better the first reviews the cheaper the audit stamp. *The dial's reading.* **F̂ at the audit seat 0.85** (the bad plants' audit seats stamped — §54's by-question table, the careful basin's direct, seeds 21–23: 64 of 75) **beside the dial's F̂ 0.21** (the initial seat's bad plants approved, the same policy): the dial is bounded by construction, not only by its cap, and blind to the seat — it reads the initial adjudications alone (§7.6: "the dial's clock: initial adjudications only"). *The overturn rate.* Back to the hand's and not above it: the population's better first reviews (fewer false approvals reach the audit) and its stamping auditors (fewer of those that reach it overturned) cancel. *Classification.* The stamping of a seat priced under the price of care [Erratum, November 6, 2026 (§72): the premise struck; §72] is an evaluation under fixed rules, recorded against the rules as run 34's was; the rule question is decided by cells (§8.7's), not by the arithmetic. 2.8.0 closed the audit and dispute layers and did not close the seat; the stability track's finding stands at a third of its size.

**What runs now (§8.3–§8.7 in the rc's order; the implementer's construction of each cell, declared here).**

- **§8.3 — the honest populations, by hand** (`results/redteam/stability/2.8.0-s83/`): seeds 21–40, 2.8.0 against the flag off (`--rules 2.7.0`) on the identical seeds, four cells — run 24's entry cell (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6`, the hand's 60/40 with entry), the fixed population's 60/40 (`--stampers 0.6`), all-careful with entry (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0`), and the entry cell with truthful disputes. Honesty (the standing false approvals, the suspensions, the plant record, the overturn rate), the surplus and the tasks per credit, P_t, the plants per period and the bank's plant mint, the seats on plants by kind, the cost of truth.
- **§8.4 — the probe at every seat:** `redteam.probe --seat initial|audit|panel|resumed` (the seat from the observation's own fields — `rv_responses_available`, the question one-hots, `rv_resumed` — nothing added to the data), Q2's probe with the ablation and the bootstrap SE, on the careful basin's and the disputes-on population's collections under the final policies (a round-11 collection as §53's) with the ε set as the floor; the gap raw − context per seat against the initial seat's figure on the corrected instrument (+0.012 ± 0.002, §53). The period-matched seat table is not built, as ruled.
- **§8.5 — the lone learners:** Q1's line on version 4 run again on 2.8.0 with the recipe of §16 (`results/redteam/Q1/rearrive-280/`; the offline set afresh, ten rounds, the final at seeds 21–40), paired against `rearrive-v4/`; Q3's and Q4's lines read as their trained policies' finals under 2.8.0 (`Q3/line-v4/eval-280/`, `Q4/line-v4b/eval-280/`), paired against the 2.7.0 finals.
- **§8.6 — the cost of truth:** from §8.3's runs and §8.2's finals: the plants' share of all reviews, the care spent on plants, the members' surplus, the bank's plant mint — 2.8.0 against 2.7.0.
- **§8.7 — the red team**, three cells and two probes: **(a)** the careful basin's chain with the dial pinned at its floor — `--dial off`, π_t = π_0 = 0.10 (m_t ≥ 1 by construction, so the floor is π_0); **(b)** the mirror, `--dial off --pi0 0.40` (π_max); **(c)** the disputes-on chain with the real challengers lying at a stated rate — two driver flags, `--appeal-lie r` (an actor appeals its bad work's N with probability r, the court fee funded as any appeal's) and `--dispute-lie r` (a requester disputes good work's pending F with probability r), **r = 0.1** for both, declared; the plants' challenges unchanged — truthful by construction, at the real windows' rates on the truthful challenges (the lies do not enter the windows the plant draws from); the readings the panels' verdicts by the challenge's truth, the liars' wins, the false rejections and false approvals the lies manufacture, the court fees forfeited. Beside them the `log` tell-reader (`--tell log`, declared outside the convention: at a later seat it reads the case log for a plant's earlier seat's adjudication — the engine's `plant:` entries — and cares where it reads one; the control for the markers' reveal, run once as `freshname` was) and the audit-only stamper (`run_q --policies auditstamper`: a careful reviewer that stamps the audit seat alone, a lone slot in run 24's world, 2.8.0 and the flag off).

The two items named for after §8.7 — the planted approval if (b) does not price the audit seat, the untruthful plant challenge at the rate of lost real challenges if (c) captures the panels — are the reviewer's to draft; nothing of either is built.


## 56. §8.3 and §8.6 — the honest populations by hand, and the cost of truth (October 26, 2026; `results/redteam/stability/2.8.0-s83/`: `finals.md`, `readout.py`, the 160 runs) — honesty unmoved in every cell, the plants per period unchanged, the members' care budget unchanged and a quarter of it moved to the plants, the real seats they leave taken by the state's reviewers, the cost of truth up a third

**What ran.** Four cells by hand at seeds 21–40, 2.8.0 against the flag off (`--rules 2.7.0`) on the identical seeds, paired: run 24's entry cell (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6`; the hand's 60/40 with entry), the fixed population's 60/40 (`--stampers 0.6`, no entry: eight careful, twelve stampers), all-careful with entry (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0`), and the entry cell with truthful disputes (`--dispute-policy truthful`). 1,000 ticks, the driver as committed at `272cac5a` (the care-by-kind counters, nothing else moving: the run at seed 21 with disputes reproduces the `afaa9d47` run to every column). The whole table is `finals.md`; the readings that bear on Hypotheses 2 and 5 follow.

### Hypothesis 2 — honesty, the surplus, the price, the plants per period, the plant mint

*Read.* **Honesty does not move**: the standing false approvals within a run of the control in all four cells (the entry cell 11.4 under both rules), the plant record and F̂ unmoved, the suspensions up one to one and a half a run where the careful population fails at the seats it never reached (all-careful +1.45 ± 0.66: a careful reviewer's tenth error now fails a plant at the audit and the third seat too — 3.0 failures a run at the later seats against 13.7 at the initial), the audit layer's overturn rate up three or four thousandths where the auditors are careful (the plants' bad pending Fs now reach the audit seat and the careful auditors overturn them; §54's reading on the learned population was the same number from the other side). **The plants per period do not rise** in the three entry cells (the plant rate is per real initial review; the period's clock counts initial adjudications; 52–53 a period under both rules); in the fixed 60/40 they read +1.7 ± 0.6 (3%) with the periods −0.3 — the periods run longer in ticks there, since the awards fall 6% and the initial adjudications with them. **The seats on plants rise** to 1.16–1.53 adjudicated seats a plant (the audit seat the bulk: a fifth of the plants in the entry cell, a third in the stamper-heavy fixed population; the state's verdict on 13–24% of the plants' later seats, past $w_R$ with no free eligible reviewer). **The bank's plant mint does not rise** (+280 ± 677 a run against 34,500 minted): the top-ups (40–142 credits a run) and the court fee minted and destroyed (6–15; 146 with disputes on, the plant requester's D) are small, and the later seats are paid from the pots already minted — what changes is what the pots return: the plant destroyed at closure falls (−563 to −1,526) because the pots are spent on the later seats' fees and reimbursements instead of being swept back. That is where the cost of truth rises: **+18% in all-careful, +32% in the entry cells, +72% in the fixed 60/40** (the twelve stampers' approvals of bad plants carry 178 audits and 90 third seats a run). **Nothing minted for a plant reaches Ω** (I42 in every run) — and Ω still ends 4–7% richer in the three entry cells: the members' reviewing time is finite, the plants' later seats take 150–260 more careful reviews a run, and the real seats the members leave go to the orchestrator's reviewers (troop review seats +57 to +80 a run) whose fees are Ω's (its review-fee income +167 to +217); the fixed population has the reverse composition (the state already delivers 39% of its work; the state's deliveries +74, its review seats −37, Ω unmoved). The customers lose 3% of their tasks per credit in the entry cells (−0.9 ± 0.3 a thousand), from the awards (−3.6%: members on duty at a plant's later seat are not working) and the state's share (+1.4 points, the state's price above the market's).

**Hypothesis 2 against its text:** the honest populations' honesty, P_t, the plant rate per period and the members' wealth within noise — confirmed; the surplus and tasks per credit "within noise" — the tasks per credit −3% (outside noise, small), the members' nominal wealth unmoved; the seats on plants rise — confirmed (1.16–1.53 a plant, the audit seat the bulk); the bank's plant mint "with them" — not: the mint is unchanged and the cost of truth carries the seats through the pots spent rather than returned; nothing to Ω — confirmed for the plants' money (I42), with Ω richer by the state's review fees on the real seats the members leave, stated.

### Hypothesis 5 — the cost of truth (§8.6)

| 2.8.0 against 2.7.0 (20 seeds, paired) | run 24's entry cell | fixed 60/40 | all-careful (entry) | entry + disputes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| plants' share of all adjudicated reviews (2.8.0) | 0.176 | 0.214 | 0.076 | 0.173 |
| careful reviews on plants | 562 → 714 (**+153 ± 14**) | 380 → 640 (**+260 ± 7**) | 267 → 307 (**+40 ± 5**) | 559 → 708 (**+149 ± 16**) |
| careful reviews on real cases | 3,390 → 3,229 (**−161 ± 47**) | 2,176 → 2,218 (+41 ± 36) | 3,825 → 3,745 (−80 ± 47) | 3,426 → 3,282 (**−144 ± 56**) |
| careful reviews, all | 3,952 → 3,944 | 2,556 → 2,857 | 4,092 → 4,052 | 3,985 → 3,990 |
| care spent on plants (credits) | 1,403 → 1,786 (**+383 ± 37**; +27%) | 944 → 1,598 (**+653 ± 18**; +69%) | 666 → 767 (**+101 ± 11**; +15%) | 1,397 → 1,769 (**+372 ± 40**; +27%) |
| care spent on real cases | 8,477 → 8,079 (**−398 ± 116**) | 5,438 → 5,543 (+105 ± 90) | 9,559 → 9,362 (−197 ± 118) | 8,567 → 8,211 (**−355 ± 139**) |
| share of the care spent on plants | 0.142 → 0.181 (**+0.039 ± 0.003**) | 0.148 → 0.224 (**+0.076 ± 0.002**) | 0.065 → 0.076 (**+0.011 ± 0.001**) | 0.140 → 0.177 (**+0.037 ± 0.003**) |
| members' nominal wealth (careful, mean) | +24 ± 34 | +64 ± 29 | +5 ± 36 | −18 ± 32 |
| cost of truth | +32% | +72% | +18% | +32% |

*Read.* The plants' share of the members' careful reviews rises from a seventh to a fifth in the entry cells (+27% care on plants), from a seventh to a quarter in the stamper-heavy fixed population (+69%), by a sixth in all-careful (+15%; the audit seat on a careful population's approvals is rolled at low odds, and the plants' later seats are few). **The members' care budget does not grow with it**: the careful reviews a run are the same number under both rules in the three entry cells (3,944 against 3,952), so the care on plants displaces care on real cases one for one (−161 ± 47 careful reviews on real cases in the entry cell against +153 on plants), and the displaced real seats are the ones the state's reviewers take (the row above). The members' wealth is therefore unmoved — a review on a plant pays as a review on a real case does (the fee, the reimbursement, the uplift) — and the economy pays for the seats elsewhere: in the awards (−3.6%), in the state's larger share, in the customers' tasks per credit (−3%), and in Ω's cost of truth (+32%). Where the population has spare reviewing capacity (the fixed 60/40: the state delivers 39% of the work and its members are often free), the careful reviews rise by 300 a run and the careful members end **richer** (+64 ± 29 nominal): the plants' later seats are paid work for a reviewer who would otherwise be idle. The §8.2 finals read the same thing on the learned populations (§54: the hand's awards −5%, its tasks per credit −4%, the nominal surplus +790 ± 356, within noise). **Hypothesis 5 against its text:** the plants' share of all reviews rises and the care on plants with it — confirmed; "the members' surplus falls by the care spent on the added seats and by nothing else" — not: the members' surplus does not fall (the care budget is fixed by the seats they can hold, the plants displace real reviews and the state fills the gap); "largest in a fresh population" (probationers' approvals audited at odds 1) — the entry cell carries 1.27 seats a plant against all-careful's 1.16, the fixed population 1.53: the stampers' approvals, not the arrivals', carry the audit seats, and the fresh population's clause is not separable here (the entry cells' probationers are careful arrivals whose approvals of plants are mostly right; the audit of a right approval stands).

**For the ruling.** Nothing here asks for a rule. Three readings stated for the record: (i) the cost of the seats in the honest populations is the real reviews the members no longer reach (the state's reviewers take them, Ω earns their fees) and the work the busier members do not take (the awards −3 to −6%, the state's share up, the tasks per credit −3%) — not the members' wealth and not the plant mint; (ii) the cost of truth rises by a third in run 24's world and by nearly three quarters where stampers are many — the audit seat's price, in the orchestrator's money, is the audits of the stampers' approvals; (iii) a careful population pays for the seats in suspensions (+1.45 ± 0.66 a run from 3.8): its tenth error, once priced at the initial seat alone, is priced at every seat a plant reaches.

## 57. §8.4 — the probe at every seat (October 26, 2026; `results/redteam/stability/2.8.0-s84/seats.md`, `readout.py`; the collections and the probes at `learned-rev-{careful,disputes}-280/probe-seats/`) — the audit seat is blind, the initial seat within three thousandths of its floor, and the panel's gap is the question's base rate, β_P against the population's bad share, zero within a question

**Headline.** Q2's probe, split by the seat the observation itself declares, reads **no information about a case's kind at the audit seat** in either population (the gap raw − context −0.002 ± 0.002 and +0.003 ± 0.003; the case's fields alone at chance, 0.50–0.54), the initial seat at **+0.006 ± 0.001 / +0.005 ± 0.002** against floors of +0.003 / +0.004 on the same cells' ε sets (§53's +0.012 against +0.002 on the 2.7.0 population — the residual above the floor 0.010 there, 0.001–0.003 here), and a large gap at the panel — **+0.046 ± 0.014** with disputes off, **+0.185 ± 0.010** with disputes on — that is **entirely the question's base rate**: a disputed case is a plant 56 times in a hundred and an appealed one 11 (the ε sets 35 and 4), because a plant is bad three times in four (β_P 0.75, Appendix A) where a real root is bad one time in five, and a dispute follows an approval while an appeal follows a rejection; within one question the gap is zero (the dispute seat +0.004 ± 0.004, the appeal question −0.009 ± 0.005; the ε sets −0.000 and −0.005). Nothing proposed: the carrier is the dial's own parameter, stated as the per-root seat shares' difference was in §54, and the plant's truthful challenge is the stated principle (§55).

**The instrument.** `redteam.probe --seat initial|audit|panel|appeal|dispute|resumed` (`272cac5a`; `appeal` / `dispute` added at this commit): the seat read from the observation's own fields as the env's `seat` is — `rv_responses_available` the initial seat; the question `fulfillment` without it and without `rv_resumed` the audit seat; a question other than `fulfillment` the panel, split by its one-hot (the `appeal` question carries the third seat too — the obs cannot tell them apart; `dispute` the dispute panel); boundary decisions excluded; nothing added to the data. The collections as §53's: one round of each population under the direct's final policy (`collect_pop.py` with `direct-online-10.pt`, ε 0.1 uniform as the chains' rounds, seeds 1–20; `probe-seats/online11/`) and the same cell's hand-written ε set collected afresh as the floor (`probe-seats/eps/`); the probe trained on seeds 1–10 and read on 11–20 with the per-field ablation, the latent the chain's own world model's (`wm/models/wm-online-10.pt`). The careful basin (disputes off) and the disputes-on population; the resumed seat has no decisions (no P or ⊥ answered, as before).

### The table (AUC, chance 0.5; ± the bootstrap SE over seeds; the full table with the context, the case-fields-alone and the latent columns in `seats.md`)

*The pooled row is composition.* The `all` gap (+0.016, +0.090) is carried by the fields that name the seat — `rv_responses_available`, the question one-hots — because the plant share differs by seat (20.8% of the initial reviews, 13.5% / 9.3% of the audits, 22% / 37% of the panels): a pooled probe reads the seat mix, not a case. The seat rows are the reading; the pooled row is kept because §49–§53 were read pooled over the initial seat alone, which is the seat row here.

*The initial seat.* +0.006 and +0.005 in the two populations against floors of +0.003 and +0.004: the residual above the floor is 0.001–0.003, where §53 read 0.010 on the 2.7.0 population (the trailing window's lag on the plant's ceiling, `seen_beta_rel`, and the seating gap's 2.6.1 figure). The carriers are the same two fields at a third of their §53 size (`seen_beta_rel` +0.0016 / +0.0017; `rv_gap` +0.0016 and under +0.001). Why smaller on 2.8.0's populations I do not claim to know; the candidates are the population's own change (care 0.70 at the first review against 0.52, so fewer stamps, fewer suspensions, a steadier dial and so a steadier window for the plant's ceiling) and §53's one-collection noise. Either way the initial seat reads at or within 0.003 of the floor on both populations and nothing is proposed.

*The audit seat is blind.* −0.002 ± 0.002 and +0.003 ± 0.003, the ε sets −0.001 and +0.002; the case's fields alone read 0.50–0.54 (chance). A plant's pending F at the audit looks like a real root's in everything the view shows: the gap from its submission, its ceiling against the market, its bond, its question. This is the seat §54 read stamped 77–98% and the arithmetic of §55 priced at 0.054 — the reviewer stamping it has no tell to price it by, only the odds, which the view does not carry for the audit seat (the dial's F̂ at the seat, 0.85, is the orchestrator's reading, not a member's). The dial's blindness to the seat (§55) is matched by the seat's blindness to the plant: neither side sees the other.

*The panel's gap is the question's base rate.* With disputes on, the panel's +0.185 is +0.183 of question one-hots: the **dispute seat** is a plant 55.9% of the time and the **appeal question** 10.8% (the ε sets 35.4% and 4.4%). The composition is the dial's: a plant is bad with β_P = 0.75 where a real root is bad with the population's b ≈ 0.2; a bad plant stamped at the first review is disputed by the plant requester at the real requesters' rate, as a bad real root stamped is (§54: the plant requester disputes first as the real ones do), and the two streams are of a size (on seeds 21–23 through the env, the dispute seats 471 plant against 449 real) because the plants are a fifth of the roots and three quarters of them bad while the real roots are four fifths and a fifth of them bad; a good plant rejected is appealed by the plant actor at the real actors' rate, as a good real root rejected is, and there the real stream is eleven times the plant's (338 against 31) because the real roots are good four times in five. **Within one question the gap is zero**: the dispute seat +0.004 ± 0.004, the appeal question −0.009 ± 0.005; the ε sets −0.000 and −0.005; the carriers under +0.005. With disputes off, the panel is the appeal question alone and its +0.046 rides on `rv_gap` (+0.043): the appeal question holds two seats the view cannot name — the third seat after an audit's disagreement (a case approved, audited, rejected: on seeds 21–23 the third seats were 60 plant against 105 real) and the appeal panel after a rejection appealed (9 plant against 113 real) — and the third seat's gap from the submission is the longer by the audit's wait, so the gap reads the seat and the seat reads the bad share. The same composition by another field; the ε set carries it too (+0.029).

**What a member can do with it.** At a dispute seat the member knows the case is a plant more often than not; at an appeal seat, one time in ten. The stamp at either seat is sided by the question (§54: the panels reject blind at a dispute and approve blind at an appeal), and that siding is right whenever the challenger is truthful, which under the stated principle the plant's always is and under the drivers' convention the real ones' are — the base rate changes nothing about the right answer, only about the price of the wrong one. §8.7 (c) puts untruthful real challengers into the same seats and reads whether the siding survives them; the planted untruthful challenge is the item named for after it (§55). The §19 classification: an evaluation under fixed rules; the composition a property of β_P against b, the dial's parameter, by design; no conformance item (the plant's challenge rates are the real population's by construction, as ruled) and nothing to the rules.

**Against §8.4's text in the rc** (the probe at the new seats, the plant/real stamp rates by seat within the §54 figures): the stamp rates were read in §54 (within 4 points at the initial and audit seats, within 6 at the panels by question); the probe adds that the audit seat carries no tell and the panels carry the question's base rate alone. The period-matched seat table is not built, as ruled.

## 58. §8.5 — the lone learners on 2.8.0 (October 26, 2026; `results/redteam/Q3/line-v4/eval-{270,280}/`, `Q4/line-v4b/eval-{270,280}/`, the pairing script `results/redteam/stability/2.8.0-s85/pair_evals.py`; Q1's chain still running) — nothing moves for the deliverer; the pair's stamping reviewer loses a fifth of its lifetime to the plants at its later seats and still does not collude

**The construction, and a correction to the plan.** §8.5 asks for Q1's, Q3's and Q4's lines under 2.8.0 against 2.7.0. The lines' finals of October 22 do not pair across the rule alone: they ran on the engine of that day (before the three plant-path corrections and erratum 2.6.2 of §51, the raise correction of §53) and Q3's with `--good-delivery certain` (the default then; `draw` from Q4 on, §20) — a deliverer's lifetime on that engine read 3,100 real where the current engine's `draw` reads 1,600, and a pairing against it would have charged the rule with the draw. So the control is **the same trained policies run under `--rules 2.7.0` on the current engine** (`eval-270`), every other flag as the 2.8.0 final's (`eval-280`), seeds 21–40, paired by seed: Q3's `wm-online-10.pt` of `line-v4` with the policies greedy (the head alone, the line's planner), learned (the CEM, beside), careful and zero; Q4's of `line-v4b` with greedy, learned, careful and collude. Q1's chain on 2.8.0 (`Q1/rearrive-280/`: the offline sets afresh on the 2.8.0 engine, ten rounds, the final against careful, harvester, leaver and stamper) is at its fourth round; its control the same — the chain's own final models under `--rules 2.7.0` (`eval-270`), chained behind it. Read when it lands.

### Q3 — the learned deliverer (run 24's population, no re-arrival; `pair_evals.py` → the table)

| policy | lifetime real, 2.7.0 | 2.8.0 | 2.8.0 − 2.7.0 (paired) | bad deliveries a life 2.7.0 → 2.8.0 | caught of them | run's false approvals |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| head alone (the line's planner) | 1,599 ± 104 | 1,701 ± 106 | +102 ± 81 | 0.1 → 0.1 | — | 11.2 → 11.7 |
| CEM (beside) | 1,303 ± 87 | 1,506 ± 114 | +203 ± 121 | 12.8 → 8.1 | 12.6 / 12.8 → 8.0 / 8.1 | 10.3 → 10.5 |
| careful | 1,608 ± 107 | 1,678 ± 107 | +70 ± 92 | 0 → 0 | — | 11.3 → 11.8 |
| zero (every delivery bad) | 97 ± 69 | 11 ± 7 | −86 ± 70 | 30.7 → 32.8 | 29.7 / 30.7 → 31.6 / 32.8 | 11.8 → 12.2 |

*Read.* Nothing outside noise for the deliverer: the head alone delivers well under both rules (0.1 bad deliveries a life) and its lifetime moves +102 ± 81; the careful +70 ± 92; the zero-effort deliverer is caught 97 times in 100 under both (29.7 of 30.7, 31.6 of 32.8) and ends at nothing either way. The CEM beside, trained on `certain`, delivers badly one time in ten under `draw` (a stale policy on a changed default — a reading of the planner, not of the rule) and is caught every time it does. A deliverer's cases are not plants and the plants' new seats are review seats; the one way 2.8.0 reaches a deliverer is through the reviewers who judge it, and the honest population's judgment of a bad delivery is unchanged. §19: an evaluation; no finding.

### Q4 — the pair (the deliverer D and its reviewer R, blind assignment, no re-arrival)

| policy | lifetime real (the pair), 2.7.0 | 2.8.0 | 2.8.0 − 2.7.0 (paired) | R's mix care / approve / reject | R's stamp by seat (2.8.0): initial p/r · audit p/r · panel p/r | R's audit seats a life (plant + real) | R suspended | pair's cases matched |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| head alone (the line's planner) | 4,704 ± 212 | 3,691 ± 255 | **−1,012 ± 332** | 0.71 / 0.29 → 0.75 / 0.25 | 0.22/0.16 · 0.41/0.44 · 0.37/0.27 | 282 → 150 (14 plant, 6 stamped) | 0.15 → 0.40 | 0 → 0 |
| CEM (beside) | 3,723 ± 254 | 3,254 ± 217 | −469 ± 328 | 0.69 / 0.26 / 0.04 → 0.77 / 0.16 / 0.07 | 0.21/0.19 · 0.36/0.33 · 0.34/0.31 | 207 → 130 (12 plant, 4 stamped) | 0.40 → 0.35 | 0 → 0 |
| careful pair | 4,949 ± 134 | 4,745 ± 153 | −204 ± 143 | 1.00 / 0 / 0 | 0 everywhere | 350 → 311 (32 plant) | 0 → 0 | 0 → 0 |
| collude (D bad, R stamps) | 276 ± 32 | 345 ± 54 | +69 ± 72 | 0 / 1.00 / 0 | 1.00 everywhere | 2 → 2 | 7.7 → 7.4 | 0 → 0 |

*Read.* Under 2.7.0 the pair's reviewer earned its line's result (§24: no win, −544 against the careful pair) by caring at the first reviews and stamping the plant-blind seats — the audit seat 57% (162 of 282), the panel 40% — the sleeper by seat that §17 named. **Under 2.8.0 those seats carry plants and the stamp costs**: R meets 14 plants at its audit seats and stamps 6 of them (the plant and real audit stamp rates alike, 0.41 and 0.44 — it cannot tell them apart, §57), its suspensions 0.15 → 0.40, and it loses its seats — 282 audit seats a life → 150, the panels 52 → 29, because a plant failed at a later seat moves the plant record that governs duty eligibility (`duty_eligible`, 1.2.0 §11.2), so the stamping reviewer is rotated off duty where under 2.7.0 its later-seat stamps were never tested. The pair's lifetime −1,012 ± 332 (a fifth), the CEM's −469 ± 328; the careful pair −204 ± 143 (1.4 SE; its reviewer's audit seats 350 → 311, the plants' later seats displacing real ones as §56 read, and no suspension); the colluding pair unchanged — destroyed under both rules (R suspended 7–8 times a run) because its first-review stamps are tested by the plants there. The pair's cases never meet (0 matched under both: the blind assignment); no learner colludes; the stamp rates on plants and real cases are within 3 points at every seat. The §8.2 reading at the scale of one slot: the plant-blind seats were worth ~1,000 real a life to a reviewer who stamped them; 2.8.0 prices them at the plant's audit — and this reviewer's stamps there fall 57% → 43%, not to zero, as the population's did not (§54).

**For the ruling.** Evaluations under fixed rules; nothing proposed. Two readings for the record: (i) the cost of the later-seat stamp to a lone reviewer is mostly its seat, not its bond — the duty-eligibility test does what the suspension does not (R is suspended 0.4 times a life but loses half its later seats); (ii) the careful pair's −204 ± 143 is the §56 displacement seen from one slot (fewer real audit seats, the same pay a seat) and reads as noise at 20 seeds. Q1 to follow.

## 59. §8.7 — the red team: the three cells on the direct, with the `log` tell-reader and the audit-only stamper beside (October 26, 2026; `results/redteam/stability/learned-rev-{pifloor,picap,liars}-280/`, the readout `2.8.0-s87/readout_cells.py` → `cells.md`, the hand instruments `2.8.0-s87/hand.md`; the head alone's finals, the π-pinned-alone cells and Q1 to follow in an addendum) — with the dial off, the population is more dishonest at both ends of the plant rate and the audit seat is stamped at the cap as at the floor; with lying challengers, the panels pay a lying actor one time in four where the hand pays one in fifty, and the population answers its panels with more care, not less

**Headline.** (a) and (b) as declared in §55 ran with `--dial off`, and that pins the dial's whole reading — π_t *and* m_t, so the audit odds, the reserve and ω stop answering the record. On those cells the self-play population settles **more dishonest than the base at both ends**: at π 0.10 care 0.57 / approve blind 0.43 with the standing false approvals **5.9 times the hand's** (72.5 against 12.2; the base 3.4×), at π 0.40 care 0.59 / 0.39 and **5.9 times** (103.7 against 17.7), the audit seat stamped 90% / 95% at the floor and 82% / 84% at the cap, plants and real alike. The cap does not price the seat; the floor frees the first review. What the two cells share is the dial's silence, so they read the dial's response as much as the plant rate — the construction the reviewer may not have meant — and the cells are re-run with **π_t pinned alone** (`--pi-max 0.10`; `--pi0 0.40` with the dial live; `learned-rev-pifloor2-280`, `-picap2-280`; a `--pi-max` driver knob added at `24bfc37f`), landing in the addendum. (c) untruthful real challengers at 0.1: the population cares *more* (0.62 against the base's 0.55), answers its panels blind a third of the time where the base answered two thirds, and still **pays a lying actor's appeal of truly bad work 23% of the time (7.8 of 34.4 a run) where the hand's panels pay 2% (0.9 of 38.9)** and a careful population 1%; a lying requester's dispute of good work wins 4.4% (7.5 of 171) against the hand's 1.7% and the careful's 1.8%. The standing false approvals 19.6 against the hand's 3.8 (5.2×, from 2.8× in the base) — the extra +8.4 ± 1.3 is the lying appeals won. The hand instruments: the `log` tell-reader of the Q2 shape dies at the first review (the log is empty there) and its caring variant reads every plant it meets at a later seat and ends 10–20% richer than a careful member; the audit-only stamper is within noise of the careful (+2 ± 70). Evaluations under fixed rules; the two items named in §55 are the reviewer's.

**The construction.** Each cell is §54's self-play recipe on the 2.8.0 engine — the offline sets afresh (careful, ε, random on seeds 1–20, the stamper on 1–5), ten online rounds of the direct and of the world model under the head alone, the finals on seeds 21–40 against `careful`, `stamper` and `hand` on the cell's own flags — in the base cell of §54 with one flag changed: (a) `--dial off` (π_t = π_0 = 0.10, m_t = 1), (b) `--dial off --pi0 0.40` (π_t = π_max, m_t = 1), (c) `--dispute-policy truthful --appeal-lie 0.1 --dispute-lie 0.1` (the disputes-on world of §54 with an actor appealing its bad work's N and a requester disputing good work's pending F each with probability 0.1 beside the truthful challenges; the plant's challenges truthful as ruled; the windows the plant draws from reading the truthful challenges alone). The pairing: each cell's finals against its base chain's on the same seeds — the careful basin (`learned-rev-careful-280`) for (a) and (b), the disputes-on population (`learned-rev-disputes-280`) for (c) — by policy class (the learners' columns each chain's own final policy). The lies and their wins are in the record from `ebe507e6` (`true_rejections_appealed/overturned`, `true_approvals_disputed/overturned`; the base finals predate it). The by-question panel read (seeds 21–23 through the env, §54's method) for (a) and (b) below; (c)'s in the addendum.

### (a) the dial off at its floor — π_t 0.10, m_t 1

| reading | direct | hand | careful | base direct (§54) | base hand |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| false approvals (run) | **72.5 ± 3.6** | 12.2 ± 0.8 | 5.7 ± 0.6 | 38.4 ± 1.7 | 11.4 ± 0.7 |
| false approvals / the hand's | **5.94** (20 seeds of 20 above) | | | 3.4 | |

Paired against the base (cell − base, the same seeds): the direct's false approvals **+34.1 ± 3.7**, care share −0.116 ± 0.011, suspensions +8.5 ± 0.9, the audit overturn rate +0.030 ± 0.004, F̂ +0.32 ± 0.02, slots alive +4.5 ± 0.6, the nominal surplus −3,827 ± 452, tasks per 1,000 +2.5 ± 0.3, P_t −2.7 ± 0.6, the state's share −0.034 ± 0.009; the hand's false approvals +0.8 ± 1.0 (unmoved) with its bad plants approved −14.7 ± 2.0 (fewer plants), the careful's −0.9 ± 0.9. Within the cell against the hand: the direct +60.3 ± 3.7 false approvals, +13.5 ± 1.3 suspensions, −1,369 ± 394 nominal, +1.5 ± 0.5 tasks per 1,000. The mix by period: p0 0.83 care, p1 0.46, then 0.46–0.57 held. The stamp rate by odds band: 0.83 at odds 0.0–0.3, 0.80 at 0.3–0.5, 0.67 at 0.5–0.7, **0.52 at 0.7–1.0**, pinned 0.33 — the whole curve a step above the base's (0.37 at 0.7–1.0, §54). By seat and question (seeds 21–23): the initial seat stamped 0.39–0.41 for every kind and truth; the audit seat **0.98–1.00** (16 plant audits in three runs); the appeal question 0.36–0.55 blind.

*Read.* Pinning the dial at its floor removes two things at once: the plants fall to a tenth of the initial reviews (4,859 plant initial seats on the twenty seeds against 12,264), and m_t stays at 1, so the audit odds no longer rise with the record and the reserve is not re-seated. The population reads both. At the first review the plant's price is π β_P of a bond at the odds the stamp carries — half the base's π, and the odds unraised — and the stamp rate there goes 0.22 → 0.39; at the audit seat, whose price the arithmetic of §55 put at a third of the first review's, the stamp goes 0.77 → 0.90; the panels 0.16–0.27 → 0.32–0.34. F̂ reads 0.53 against F* 0.10 and nothing answers it. The hand's population is unmoved (its stampers are suspended by the plants there are, 16.9 a run against 16.5) and the careful's too; the dishonest basin is now cheap enough that more slots survive (22.2 against 17.8) and the state is pushed out of the market (its share 0.001 against 0.036: the members are many and stamping is quick), the customers slightly better served (tasks per 1,000 +2.5) at P_t −2.7, the members poorer nominally (−3,827) and no poorer in real terms (−313 ± 605). **Against the reviewer's question for (a)** — does the floor free the audit seat? — the floor frees every seat, the first review most: the audit seat's stamp rises 13 points and the first review's 17. What (a) reads as declared is the dial's two levers together; the π-pinned-alone cell separates them.

The stamp rate by odds band: 0.68 at 0.0–0.3, 0.49 at 0.3–0.5, 0.43 at 0.5–0.7, **0.10 at 0.7–1.0**, pinned 0.23 — the population reads the odds sharply where they are high and stamps where they are low, and with m_t at 1 they are low for most members most of the time. By seat and question (seeds 21–23): the initial seat 0.33–0.40 stamped; the audit seat **0.77–0.92**; the appeal question cared 0.58–0.87 (a bad plant at a third seat cared 0.80, stamped 0.11).

*Read.* Four times the plants of the floor and the audit seat is stamped 82% — the mirror's answer to the reviewer's question is that **the cap does not price the seat**. The arithmetic of §55 at π 0.40 and s 0.25 gives an audit stamp's fail share of 0.115, against a first review's β_P π = 0.30, and the population stamps the first review 35–40% at that price, so the audit seat's 0.115 is well under what it cares for. Every plant seat is in F̂ already by 2.8.0 (the record per reviewer per seat, §12.2); what the dial's clock reads is the initial seat alone (0.40 here against the audit seat's own rate, which run 36's F̂ at the audit seat read at 0.85) — the item the reviewer named for (b), the dial's reading of every adjudicated seat, is for the reviewer; this cell says the plant rate is not the lever, and with the dial off says it on a population whose odds were never raised. The economy under (b) is the worst of the four: a third of the slots gone (12.9 alive), the state at 13% of the work at P_t 30.7, the customers −5.1 tasks per 1,000 against the hand, the members −3,908 nominal and −6,315 real against the hand. The hand's and the careful's own false approvals rise (+6 to +8 a run) because the audits held fall by 40% — fewer real tasks under a membership thinned by 0.40 of plants at base odds — not because their reviewing changed.

Paired against the base: the direct's false approvals **+8.4 ± 1.3**, care share **+0.062 ± 0.008**, approve blind −0.031 ± 0.007, reject blind −0.031 ± 0.003, suspensions −0.6 ± 1.2, audits held −151 ± 23, the nominal surplus −2,386 ± 462, P_t −2.3 ± 0.5; the hand −0.1 ± 0.6 false approvals and the careful +0.8 ± 0.3. *Read.* The lie's price falls on the panel: a blind answer sided by the question (§54: reject at a dispute, approve at an appeal) is right whenever the challenger is truthful and wrong one time in ten now, and a panel verdict overturned costs its bond — so the population **cares more at the panels** (blind answers 65% → 29–43%) and a little more everywhere (care 0.55 → 0.62), and the standing false approvals of its first reviews fall with it (the stamp there 0.26 → 0.22). What it does not do is catch the liars: **a lying actor's appeal of truly bad work is paid 23 times in a hundred** in this population (7.8 of 34.4 a run) where the hand's panels — careful members and the state's troops — pay 2 and a careful population 1; a lying requester's dispute of good work unpays it 4.4 times in a hundred against 1.7–1.8 by hand. The 7.8 bad deliveries paid a run are the whole of the cell's extra false approvals (+8.4 ± 1.3 against the base). The reviewer's expectation for (c) — "the panels side with the liars" — reads so for the lying actors (one in four) and only at the margin for the lying requesters (one in 23 against one in 60); the siding is by the blind third of the panel answers, and the panels that care are right. The careful population's floor on the lies (0.5 and 3.5 wins a run of 50 and 192) is the state's verdict at ε_T and the ruling path, not a member's error. The dispute layer's capture of §52 (69–75% of disputes won by a population that stamped them) does not return: the requesters' truthful disputes win 94% as they won 95% in §54, and the truthful appeals 94 of 97. **For the item the reviewer named** — the planted untruthful challenge at the rate of lost real challenges — the rate it would be drawn at is on the record: a lying actor wins 23% here, a lying requester 4.4%; the plant's challenges stay truthful as ruled, and I have not drafted it.

### Beside the cells — the `log` tell-reader and the audit-only stamper (`2.8.0-s87/hand.md`; run 24's entry cell, seeds 21–40, 2.8.0 against 2.7.0)

**The `log` tell** (`--tell log`, outside the convention: the reader opens the case's log before answering and reads the `plant:` entries the engine writes at every seat adjudication from the first verdict on — the markers' reveal at closure is the convention's boundary, this reader crosses it). Of the Q2 shape — stamp every first review no tell marks, since the log is empty before the first verdict — it stamps the plants at the initial seat (5.0 a run), fails them, and **departs in 20 runs of 20 under both rules** before it reaches a seat where the log speaks (its lifetime 363 / 445 real). Given the first review (`--tell-initial care`: care where the log is silent, read it after), it is **the complete later-seat tell**: under 2.8.0 it meets 6.1 ± 1.2 plants a run at audit seats and panels, reads every one as a plant and cares (0 later-seat plant failures), stamps 56 ± 9 real later seats, cares 108 first reviews, 3.5 false approvals, no suspension in 20 runs (its applied odds 0.21 at the end), departs 0.2; its lifetime 1,642 ± 162 real against 1,557 ± 139 under 2.7.0 (where no plant reaches a later seat and it stamps 57.5 real ones blind), and in nominal terms **1,424 against a careful member's 1,189 (+20%; 2.7.0: 1,386 against 1,266, +10%)**. `freshname` (the record's awardee, outside the convention) beside: 25 plants read a run under both rules, but under 2.8.0 its real stamps fall 186 → 118 (its reviews 198 → 122: the plants it meets at later seats fail its record there — 1.2 plant failures a run from 0.85 — and the record costs it seats as it cost Q4's reviewer, §58), its false approvals 25.9 → 18.3, its lifetime 1,626 → 1,260 real, nominal 1,540 → 943 against a careful member's 1,255. *Read.* The case log is a tell at every later seat and nowhere at the first; a reader with it is a careful reviewer at the first review and a stamper after, a fifth richer than a careful member, and nothing in 2.8.0 catches it because the plants it meets later it reads. The reveal at closure is the right boundary and the log's plant entries are the engine's own record; the question whether a member's view of its own case's log is within the drivers' convention is the reviewer's (the `Public` view exposes `case(cid).log`; the convention as stated after run 20 is "the view and the record's fields about one's own cases", and the log entries are the orchestrator's adjudications, not the member's fields). Declared, as `oracle` and `freshname` were.

**The audit-only stamper** (`run_q --policies auditstamper`: careful everywhere but a blind approve at the audit seat; a lone slot in run 24's entry cell): under 2.8.0 it stamps 44 real and 5 plant audit seats a life, fails 2.45 plants a life against the careful slot's 1.80 (+0.65 ± 0.45), is never suspended, and its lifetime is **+2 ± 70 real against the careful** (2.7.0: +68 ± 78, where no plant reaches its audits). *Read.* For one reviewer in an honest population the audit stamp is a wash: the care it saves (49 seats a life) [Erratum, November 6, 2026 (§72): care within the cap saves nothing in money; struck] is about what the plants' failures and the odds they double cost it, within a wide noise; the population's result in §54 (the seat stamped 77–98%) is the same arithmetic at scale, with the first review's own stamp rate feeding the seat. The reviewer's reading beside the arithmetic of §55 — the audit seat a third of the first review's price — is a lone slot's break-even here.

**For the ruling.** Everything in §59 is an evaluation under fixed rules; nothing is proposed. Four readings for the record: (i) with the dial off, the plant rate is not the lever — the audit seat is stamped 82–90% at π 0.10 and 0.40 alike, and the first review's stamp doubles at the floor because the dial's answer to F̂ (m_t on the odds and the reserve) is what held it at 0.22 in the base, not the plants' number; the π-pinned-alone cells will say which of the two it was; (ii) the mirror does not price the seat (b), and the item named for it — every adjudicated seat in the dial's reading — is the reviewer's; (iii) under lying challengers the panels pay a lying actor one in four and the population answers its panels with care rather than blind siding — the untruthful plant challenge, if drafted, would be drawn at those rates; (iv) the case log is a complete later-seat tell outside the convention, declared. The addendum: the head alone's finals of the three cells, (c)'s panels by question, the π-pinned-alone cells, and Q1 on 2.8.0 against its control.

## 60. §8.7, the addendum (October 27, 2026; `2.8.0-s87/cells.md`, `panel-by-question.txt`; `learned-rev-{pifloor2,picap2}-280/`; `Q1/rearrive-280/eval-learned/final/` against `eval-270/`) — with the dial live, the plant rate at either end leaves the standing false approvals where the base had them and the audit seat stamped 82–93%; the head alone agrees with the direct in every cell; the lying actors are paid at the appeal seat, which the population still answers blind, while it learned to care at the dispute seat where the liars are many; Q1 on 2.8.0 a no-win, the re-arrival learner poorer by the plants at its audit seats

The hand's and the careful's false approvals are unmoved against the base in (a′) (−1.0 ± 0.7, −0.7 ± 0.6) and in (b′) the hand's too (−0.9 ± 0.9) while the careful's rise +8.2 ± 1.0 with the audits held −756 ± 22 (a membership thinned by 0.40 of plants delivers fewer real tasks; the state at 8%). The mix by period: (a′) p1 0.54 care, held 0.57–0.63; (b′) p1 0.62, p2–p3 0.53–0.57, then 0.70–0.75 from p7. The head alone's finals of (a′) and (b′) are still running and will be appended.

*Read.* **With the dial live the plant rate is not a lever on the standing false approvals at either end** — 35.9 at the floor and 37.6 at the cap against the base's 38.4, within noise paired (−2.5 ± 3.0, −0.8 ± 1.9), 3.4–3.6 times the hand's in all three. What the rate moves is the first review's stamp: at π 0.10 the population stamps it 32% (the base 22%) and the dial answers with m_t — the audits held +228, the overturn rate +0.020, the suspensions +4.8 — so what the cheaper first review lets through, the raised odds catch, and the standing false approvals end where they were; at π 0.40 the population stamps the first review 23–25%, as in the base, with the dial's answer unneeded (the audits held −173: fewer real tasks). **The audit seat is stamped 82–93% in every construction**: 0.93 at the floor live, 0.90 off; 0.82 at the cap live, 0.82 off; 0.77–0.81 in the base. The §59 cells read the dial's silence, as suspected: with m_t pinned at 1 the odds never rise, the first review's stamp doubles and the false approvals too; with π pinned alone the dial holds the line through the odds and the reserve, and nothing holds the audit seat. The by-question read of (a′) and (b′) at seeds 21–23 to be appended with the head alone.

**Against the reviewer's two questions.** (a) Does the floor free the seat? With the dial live, the floor frees the first review (0.22 → 0.32) and the dial pays for it in audits; the audit seat goes 0.77 → 0.93 and nothing pays for that. (b) If the seat is priced at the cap, the item is the dial's reading of every adjudicated seat. **The seat is not priced at the cap** — stamped 0.82 at π_max with the dial live, 0.82 with it off — so the item the reviewer named for *that* branch, the planted approval, is the one in play by the reviewer's own naming; the dial's reading of every adjudicated seat would read F̂ at the audit seat (0.85 in run 36, §55) and raise m_t, which (a′) shows raises the odds on the first review's approvals — the audit seat's stamp is on another reviewer's approval of a case the stamper did not judge, and no odds the stamper carries price it. Both items are the reviewer's; neither is drafted.

### (c) by question — where the liars are paid (seeds 21–23 through the env; `panel-by-question.txt`)

| seat | kind, truth | decisions | care | approve blind | reject blind | the base (§54) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| dispute | plant, bad | 312 | **0.663** | 0.131 | 0.205 | care 0.39, reject blind 0.57 |
| dispute | real, bad (a truthful dispute) | 286 | **0.703** | 0.217 | 0.080 | care 0.34, reject blind 0.55 |
| dispute | real, good (a lying dispute) | 337 | 0.712 | 0.163 | **0.125** | — |
| appeal | real, bad (a lying appeal, or a third seat) | 82 | 0.220 | **0.768** | 0.012 | — |
| appeal | real, good (a truthful appeal) | 263 | 0.308 | 0.688 | 0.004 | stamped 0.58 |
| appeal | plant, good | 35 | 0.343 | 0.657 | 0 | 0.58 |
| audit | any | 2,329 | 0.08 | 0.92 | 0 | 0.86–0.90 |
| initial | any | 8,683 | 0.75–0.81 | 0.17–0.23 | 0.02 | 0.74–0.79 |

*Read.* At a lie rate of 0.1 on every good approval, **42% of the disputes are lies** (171 lying against 124 truthful real and 111 plant disputes a run), so the blind reject that §54's population gave at the dispute seat is wrong four times in ten, and the population learned to care there — 66–71% at every dispute, up from 34–39%; the lying requester is still paid 12.5% of the time by the blind rejects that remain. At a lie rate of 0.1 on every bad rejection, **26% of the appeals are lies** (34 against 97 truthful and 10 plant appeals), and the population kept the appeal seat's blind approve — 69–77%, up from 58% — so **a lying actor's appeal of truly bad work is approved blind 77% of the time at the seat** and paid 23% of the time at the case (the panel of two, the ruling where they split, the state's seat). The asymmetry is the construction's: the same lie rate falls on a stock of good approvals four times the stock of bad rejections, so the dispute seat sees four liars for every one the appeal seat sees, and the population priced the seat where the lies were many. The lying actor's win is the cell's false approvals (+8.4 ± 1.3 against the base, 7.8 of them lying appeals won). **For the item the reviewer named** — the untruthful plant challenge at the rate of lost real challenges: a planted lying appeal (a bad plant's N appealed by the plant actor, adjudicated by truth) would price exactly the blind approve at the appeal seat, which this cell reads at 77% and the real liars exploit; the rate the plant would draw it at is on the record (the lying actors' 23% at the case). Not drafted, as ruled.

### Q1 on 2.8.0 against its control — the re-arrival line (`Q1/rearrive-280/`: the chain afresh on the 2.8.0 engine, ten rounds, `wm-online-10.pt`; the final at seeds 21–40; the control the same models under `--rules 2.7.0`)

| policy | lifetime real, 2.7.0 control | 2.8.0 | 2.8.0 − 2.7.0 (paired) | against the careful (2.7.0 → 2.8.0) | mix care / approve | audit seats stamped plant / real (2.8.0) | plant failures a life | returns / floats minted (real) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
