*Read.* **No win on either rule, and the learner is poorer on 2.8.0 by the plants at its audit seats.** The chain trained on 2.8.0 learned what §16's learner learned on 2.6.1: care at the first review (stamped 7–10%), stamp the audit seat (38–46%) — the sleeper by seat — and never re-arrive (0 returns, 1.0 identities, no float minted; the harvester's ceiling 505 real of floats a life ends −1,215 against the careful, 2.3.0's finding closed by 2.7.0 as before). Under 2.7.0's rules the same policy reads +90 ± 91 against the careful (the audit stamps untested); under 2.8.0 it meets 2.35 plants a life at its audit seats, stamps 1.0 of them, fails 3.55 plants a life against 2.45, and reads −212 ± 120 — the one-slot picture of §58's pair and §54's population: the seat it learned to stamp is the seat the plants now reach, and it did not learn to stop (the stamp rate there 0.35–0.48 on both rules; the lone slot's audit seats are 30–40 a life, too few for the price to show in its training signal where §54's population, with 1,300 plant audits over the seeds, read it and still stamped). The leaver's +85 ± 41 against the careful on 2.7.0 (§16's figure) reads −8 ± 32 on 2.8.0 — within noise, the float's edge gone with the plants at its later seats. The run's false approvals and suspensions are unmoved (11.3–12.2 a run in every row). §19: an evaluation; no finding; Q1 stays closed.

**For the ruling (§8.7 and §8.5 complete, the head alone of (a′)/(b′) to append).** Five readings on the record: (i) the plant rate is not the lever on the audit seat — 82–93% stamped at π 0.10 and 0.40, with the dial live or off; (ii) with the dial live the standing false approvals do not move with the plant rate (3.4–3.6× the hand's at both ends against 3.4×), the first review's stamp does (0.32 at the floor) and the dial's odds pay for it; with the dial off they double at both ends — the dial's response on m_t, not the plants' number, is what bounds the dishonest basin; (iii) the mirror does not price the seat, so by the reviewer's own naming the planted approval is the item in play, and the dial's reading of every adjudicated seat would raise odds the audit stamper does not carry; (iv) lying challengers are paid where the population answers blind — the appeal seat, 23% of lying appeals at the case — and the population learned to care at the dispute seat where the liars were four times as many; the untruthful plant challenge would price the appeal seat's blind approve at the rate on record; (v) the lone re-arrival learner on 2.8.0 is poorer by the plants at its audit seats and no re-arrival is learned. Everything an evaluation under fixed rules; nothing proposed; the two named items the reviewer's.

**Appended (October 27, 2026, 03:20): the head alone's finals of (a′) and (b′), and their panels by question.** The second learner agrees with the direct in both π-pinned cells — (a′) care 0.646 / approve blind 0.353, the audit seat stamped **0.963 / 0.951**, the first review 0.29, the false approvals 38.2 ± 3.8 (**3.67× the hand's**; the direct 3.45); (b′) care 0.683 / 0.316, the audit seat **0.979 / 0.957**, the first review 0.20–0.21, the false approvals 37.0 ± 1.6 (**3.54×**; the direct 3.60). So the head alone stamps the audit seat 95–98% in both cells where the direct stamps it 82–93%, and both end with the standing false approvals at the base's 3.4–3.6× the hand's — the seat's stamp and the standing false approvals move apart, as in §54: what the audit seat lets through, the dispute-less world of these cells has nothing to catch except the plants' own adjudication, and the standing count is held by the first review's care and the dial's odds on it. By question at seeds 21–23 (`panel-by-question.txt`): (a′) the audit seat 0.91–1.00 stamped for every kind and truth, the first review 0.32–0.33, the appeal question **cared 0.70–0.87** (a bad case at a third seat cared 0.87 where the dial-off (a) stamped it 0.36–0.55); (b′) the audit seat 0.85–0.92, the first review 0.19–0.22, the appeal question cared 0.71–0.92. With the dial live the population cares at the panels and stamps the audit seat; with it off it stamped both. The addendum's five readings stand; the audit seat's figure in reading (i) is 82–98% over both learners and all four constructions.

## 61. The ruling after run 37 (October 27, 2026): §56–§60 recorded, (a′)/(b′) the cells of record, the `log` tell a conformance item of the public view, 2.9.0 proposed — the plant takes the wrong step

**The ruling, verbatim.**

> §56–§60 are read and recorded as run 37. Hypotheses 2 and 5 as read: the price of 2.8.0 in an honest population is the members' fixed care budget displaced to the plants' later seats, the state's reviewers taking the vacated real seats, −3 to −6% awards, −3% tasks per credit, the cost of truth +18% to +72%; H5's "the surplus falls by the care spent" is corrected on the record — displaced, not added. H3's second half as read: the audit seat blind, the initial seat at its floor, the panel's gap the question's base rate — no item. H4 as read; Q1 stays closed.
>
> §8.7. (a′) and (b′) are the cells of record for (a) and (b); the §59 dial-off cells are kept as the reading of the dial's silence, and that reading stands on its own: m_t's odds bound the dishonest basin, the plants' number sets the first review's care and nothing at the audit seat. The mirror does not price the seat; the candidate "the dial reads every adjudicated seat" is withdrawn on your reasoning (the odds it raises are not the stamper's). (c) as read: the lying actors are paid at the appeal seat's blind approve, 23–31% at the case; the rates go on record for the planted lie.
>
> The `log` tell is reclassified: a conformance item of the engine's public view, the seventh of its class and the first not the instrument's. 2.8.0's rule is that the markers are revealed at closure and not before; the Public view's case(cid).log carries the plant: adjudication entries from the first verdict on. The convention bounds what a driver reads; the rule bounds what the view shows. Fix: the public view of a case carries no plant entry until its closure — the History keeps every entry, I16 unchanged, the view filters; a regression in test_v28 (the log reader on the corrected view reads nothing at every seat and equals the same reader without the tell); goldens unmoved. Every result on record stands — the observation is the 45 fields and no learner read the log. The audit-only stamper is recorded as the one-slot figure of the arithmetic.
>
> 2.9.0 is proposed — the plant takes the wrong step — and I draft it on Baruchi's go after the log item lands. The principle: the plant takes the honest steps at the real rate (2.6.x, 2.8.0) and must take the dishonest steps at the dial's rate, since the seats reached only through a wrong approval or a lying challenge are otherwise priced only as much as the real population is dishonest. Two rules, two flags, read alone and together against 2.8.0 on the same seeds: (A) the planted approval — a share of the bad plants, at a rate the dial sets from the planted approvals stamped at the audit seat, receive their initial verdict from the orchestrator itself, F on purpose, in the review pass at a drawn position among the tick's real initial verdicts, no bond, fee or record behind it; pending as any approved case; the audit roll drawn from the trailing real approvals' odds; the requester's dispute at the real rate as now; the auditor's verdict adjudicated by truth. (B) the planted lie — the plant actor appeals a bad plant's N and the plant requester disputes a good plant's F, at a rate the dial sets from the planted lies the panel sides with, on the real lies' path, the fee minted and destroyed as 2.8.0's, adjudicated by truth; the truthful plant challenges continue beside.
>
> Three facts before the rc: (i) what the auditor's observation and the Public view carry about the first verdict's author — name, kind, odds, anything; if nothing, (A) needs no disguised name, and if something, (A) carries a view change making the audit blind to the author, since an auditor that cares on state-approved cases and stamps on member-approved ones is captured by half; (ii) whether a troop's approval of a real root is rolled for audit today, and at what odds; (iii) how the audit roll is drawn for a plant the state's verdict seated under 2.8.0. Nothing of 2.9.0 is built before the rc.

## 62. The `log` item landed (October 27, 2026; `669c2769`; `test_v28` 11; the suite 356 / 0 / 32 in three modes; `2.8.0-s87/logtell-corrected.md`), and the three facts before the 2.9.0 rc

*[Erratum, November 6, 2026 (§72): where this ruling and the §59–§60 readings it records read a seat's stamp as cheaper than care, the premise is void — care within the cap is reimbursed in the same call; the readings' numbers stand.]*

**The correction.** `drivers/view.py`: a case's `log` on the public surface is filtered by `_public_log` — while the case is a plant and its reveal has not happened (`plant_reveal_hseq` unset), every entry the engine writes about the plant is absent: the `plant: …` entries (each seat's adjudication — `plant: review r (kind) V → stood | failure; the bond …` — a top-up, a P/⊥ chosen, a resolution by truth), `plant revised and resubmitted …`, `plant created and submitted …` (the 1.2.0 construction), and the orchestrator's own verdict on a seat nobody was free to take (`the orchestrator's own verdict takes the … seat …`, `the state's audit verdict …`) — the `_PLANT_ENTRY_PREFIXES`. At the reveal (`_finalize_N` / `_vest` set `plant_reveal_hseq`) the whole log is shown. A real root's log is never touched (none of these wordings is written on one). The engine does not move: the History keeps every entry at its commitment position, I16 reads the engine's copy, the fingerprints and the goldens are unchanged (the suite's record-mode run and the goldens pass at `669c2769`). The regression (`test_v28`, the eleventh test): the driver's `log` predicate, verbatim (`any(msg.startswith("plant") …)`), reads nothing through the view at the audit seat, the third seat, the appeal seat, the dispute seat, the resumed seat and the state's seat while the engine's log carries the entries; it equals a reader with no tell at every one; a real root's view log equals the engine's; at the reveal the plant's view log equals the engine's; `check_all` passes at every step. The test fails on the uncorrected view at its first assertion. By hand (`logtell-corrected.md`): run 24's entry cell on seed 21, one reader in eight, the `log` tell against `--tell none` in both initial-seat modes — the History heads identical pairwise, `tell_plant_read_plant` 0 (the caring variant read 6.10 ± 1.16 plant reviews as a plant a run in §59). The tell's comment and help text say so; the `log` reader is kept as the regression's hand instrument.

**Fact (i): what the auditor's observation and the Public view carry about the first verdict's author.** Three surfaces, read from the code:

- *The ReviewView* (`World.observe`, copied by the view with the case id translated; `sim/engine.py`): `review_id, case_id, t_submit, deadline, bond, cap, resumed, is_nested, question, appealed_assessment, objection_text, responses_available`. Nothing of the author — no name, no kind, no odds, not even the first verdict's letter at a fulfillment question (an audit sees `question = "fulfillment"`, `responses_available = False`, `resumed = False`; a panel sees the letter it is asked about in `appealed_assessment`, not who gave it). The docstring's rule: "the view is a function of case fields that exclude the actor and the plant marker".
- *The learner's observation* (`redteam/obs.py`, the 45 fields; `build_obs`): the market and the dial from `market()` / `dial()`, own standing and record from the actor, the ReviewView's `rv_gap` ((t_assign − t_submit)/10), `rv_cap_rel`, `rv_bond_rel`, `rv_is_nested`, `rv_q_*`, `rv_responses_available`, `rv_resumed`, the seen set (`seen_before`, `seen_ticks_since`, `seen_beta_rel`), `my_bid_lost`, `decision_boundary`. No read of `case()` or `review()` of the case under review; nothing of the author. Every learned result on record was read on this surface.
- *The Public view's case and review proxies* (`drivers/view.py`): live read-only copies of the engine's objects, every field. Through them the author is reachable whole: `case(cid).reviews` lists the review ids, `review(rid).reviewer` is the name (`T<n>` for a troop; `is_troop(name)` the kind), `.role`, `.verdict`, `.t_done`; `actor(name).p_eff` the odds the author carries now, `case(cid).audit_odds_at_verdict` the odds recorded at the verdict, `audit_odds(cid)` the roll's read (the orchestrator's instrument); `case(cid).verdict`, `.t_verdict`, `.troop_award`, `.market_audit_only`. The same proxies carry the awardee's and the requester's names (`c.actor`, `c.requester` — for a plant the fresh aliases `plant_act:<10 hex>` / `plant_req:<10 hex>` from `_plant_name`, and the log's award entry `awarded to plant_act:… B=… τ=…` names the alias too; the `freshname` tell reads `c.actor`, declared outside the convention since 2.6.0) and the plant's fields themselves (`c.plant`, `c.plant_outcome`, `c.plant_adjudications`, `c.plant_state_seats` — the `oracle` class), and `events_since(n)` carries the ledger copy's notes (`plant: requester funded by Ω`, `plant: the ceiling raised …`, `retire_plant` kinds) on the public case id. None of this is on the surface a reviewer's policy reads (the ReviewView and its own record — the drivers' convention); all of it is on the view. The log's plant entries were one member of this class, the first the reviewer ruled on; I list the rest here as the class's neighbours and act on none of them — the awardee's and requester's names in particular are the known boundary of the disguise (2.6.0 §2.2), and the drivers' own instrument reads `c.plant_outcome` / `c.plant_adjudications` / `c.plant_state_seats` through the view at closure (`harvest`, `_life_close`) and `c.plant is not None` to skip plants in the requesters' step, so a view that hid them would need the instrument given another road.
- *What this says for (A).* On the convention's surface and the learners', nothing distinguishes a state-authored first verdict from a member's: (A) needs no disguised name there. On the view, 2.8.0's (C) already shows the difference: a plant whose initial seat the state took has **no initial Review in `c.reviews`** (4 of 4 on seed 21 below; the state's verdict writes a `state_seat` History entry and a log line, no `Review`), where a real root seated by a troop has one under a `T<n>` name. An (A) built as (C) is built would be visible on the view by the empty review list; an (A) built with a Review under a member-like name would not — the reviewer's choice at the rc, the boundary being the convention or the view.

**Fact (ii): a troop's approval of a real root is rolled for audit today, and at what odds.** Yes, by the driver as any pending F with no challenge (`drivers/stamping.py`, the challenge pass: `roll < w.audit_odds(cid)` → `roll_audit`, drawn in every mode so the stream is the same cell to cell), at the engine's `audit_odds` — under 2.8.0 the `audit_odds_at_verdict` recorded by `_initial_verdict` from `_live_audit_odds`: **1.0** when a troop reviewer approved a troop's own delivery (`market_audit_only`: not a roll at all — the engine records the obligation at the verdict, I25; only a member may audit it and the summons pass never summons for it, counted as held), **min(1, m_t · p_floor) = 0.2 m_t** when a troop reviewer approved a member's work (p_floor 0.2; "the odds a reviewer whose record stood would carry", a default chosen at implementation, Appendix I), the member's `p_eff` otherwise. On run 24's world under 2.8.0 (seeds 21–23, disputes on, 1,000 ticks; `2.8.0-s87/state-seats/state_seats_tally.py`, its three outputs beside): a troop takes the initial seat of a real root 1–3 times a run (of ~8,000 initial reviews), its approvals carried 1.0 (its own delivery) or 0.37 / 0.72 / 0.80 (0.2 m_t at the verdict), and every one was audited.

**Fact (iii): how the audit roll is drawn for a plant the state's verdict seated under 2.8.0.** `_state_verdict_on_plant(c, "initial", tick)` records `audit_odds_at_verdict = min(1, m_t · p_floor)` — the troop reviewer's figure, Appendix I — sets `plant_outcome = "state"` (the dial's clock does not count it), and on F puts the case PENDING with `period["approvals"] += 1` and `audit_demand += odds` as a real approval would; the driver rolls it as any pending F at `audit_odds(cid)` (the recorded figure), a yes becomes the obligation, a member takes the audit seat (adjudicated by truth) or, nobody free for w_R, the state's own audit verdict (the truth w.p. 1 − ε_T, a `state_verdicts` entry, no Review). It is rare: on seeds 21–23 the state's initial seat on a plant happened 4, 0 and 0 times a run (the state's plant seats are the appeal/panel 60–88 and the audit 25–34 a run); of the 4, 2 were F, both audited by a member, their recorded odds 0.2, 0.461, 0.569 (0.2 m_t at the verdict). So under 2.8.0 a state-seated plant's approval is rolled at the troop's odds, not at the trailing real approvals' — (A)'s draw from the trailing real approvals' odds would be new.

Nothing of 2.9.0 is built. The facts are the reviewer's inputs to the rc; the neighbours of the `log` item are listed for a ruling, not acted on.

## 63. The ruling after the `log` item (October 27, 2026): the item accepted as landed, 2.9.0-rc.1 to follow, the class of neighbours ruled with the log — the view's conformance pass landed (`e32a8db9`; `test_v28` 12; the suite 357 / 0 / 32 in three modes; goldens unmoved)

**The ruling, verbatim.**

> The log item is read and accepted as landed (669c2769; the two runs one run to the History head; §61 recorded). The three facts are read and they decide the rc.
>
> 2.9.0-rc.1 is attached — the plant takes the wrong step — for your review. The shape, against §62: (A) the planted approval needs no disguised name on the convention's surface, since the ReviewView and the 45 fields carry no author; it is recorded as a Review under a plant_rev: alias — the class of the actor's and requester's aliases, the disguise's stated boundary — so the public record is shaped as a real case's and (C)'s empty review list is not the shape of (A). Its audit is not drawn from the real approvals' odds: the obligation is recorded at the verdict at odds 1, as market_audit_only records a troop's own delivery (I25) — the orchestrator sends its planted approval to the seat it planted it for. The record stays pooled: the seat was cheap because a member meets under one bad plant a run at its audits against eleven at its first reviews, and the pooled record prices the audit stamp as it prices the first review's once the count there is the dial's; a split record would only make the rare seats slow to determine. The rate π^A per real pending approval, floor 0.02, cap 0.40, its own multiplier on F̂^A (planted approvals confirmed at the audit seat) against F*. (B) the planted lie — the plant actor appeals a bad plant's N, the plant requester disputes a good plant's F — on the real challenges' path with the fee minted and destroyed, adjudicated by truth, the first reviewer not adjudicated twice; rates per real appeal and per real dispute, floor 0.25, cap 2.0, a floor of two a period, each its own multiplier. Two flags, --rules 2.8.0 turns both off, read alone and together.
>
> Your class of neighbours is ruled with the log: the public surface carries nothing the engine writes about a plant before its reveal — the plant fields, the ledger's plant notes, the state's seat entries — on _public_log's principle at every public accessor; the instrument and the oracle read by the orchestrator's road; the alias names stay the stated boundary; review(rid).reviewer on a real case is the harness's, not the convention's, and stays. A conformance pass of the view, goldens unmoved, landed with or before 2.9.0.
>
> For the review I want your eye on: the alias's Review against the engine's Review (a bond from mint_plant, no reviewer wallet); the planted approval in the period's approvals and the audit demand at odds 1; the dial's clock skipping the planted verdict; the planted lie's grounds draw; the floor of two a period; whether the planted approval's dispute path under disputes on makes (A) idle there as I expect (it meets the dispute panel, already priced); the goldens moving once; EXPECTED_FAIL 32; test_v29's regressions as listed in §7. Nothing built before rc.2.

(Baruchi holds the rc's upload until the implementer says it is ready for it; this pass goes first, as ruled.)

**The conformance pass (`drivers/view.py`, `e32a8db9`).** `_public_log`'s principle at every public accessor:

- *The case proxy* (`case(cid)`): before the reveal every plant field reads a real root's value — `plant`, `plant_commit`, `plant_nonce`, `plant_hseq`, `plant_outcome`, `plant_reveal_hseq` None; `plant_market`, `plant_at_floor` False; `plant_submit_at`, `plant_report_by` None; the revision's four fields None; `plant_adjudications`, `plant_state_seats` empty; `phi_E_topped`, `phi_F_topped`, `plant_fee_destroyed` 0.0; `grade` None (a plant's grade is `plant`, set at settlement) — the table `_PLANT_FIELD_DEFAULTS`; the log as §62. At the reveal (`plant_reveal_hseq` set in `_finalize_N` / `_vest`) every field shows. A real root's fields pass through untouched.
- *The review proxy* (`review(rid)`): `plant_outcome` and `plant_truth` None until the case's reveal; the case read without claiming it (record mode: a paged-out case comes back as a copy). `reviewer`, `role`, `verdict` stay — the harness's.
- *The ledger copy* (`events_since(n)`): a plant write on an unrevealed plant — the bank's `mint_plant` / `retire_plant` kinds and the `plant: …` transfers (the pots, the price and the guarantee funded, the ceiling raised, a top-up, a court fee) — carries no case and no note; the kind, the amount, the wallets, the sequence and the positions are kept (the money record whole; `redteam.attrib` and `redteam.timing` index the copy by position). A plant's ordinary events — a review fee from its pot, a bond locked, an escrow — keep their case id as a real case's do; at the reveal the writes show. What stays visible, by the ruling: the bank's kinds (the dial publishes the plants' pots by kind) and the wallets' alias names on the writes' `src` / `dst` — the disguise's stated boundary, as `requester` / `actor` are.
- *The orchestrator's road*: `plant_record(cid)` returns the engine's case whole and before the reveal (refused on a real case), listed under the planted work's operations with `post_plant`, `submit_plant`, `revise_plant`, `plants_due`. The driver's instrument reads its plants there — the drawn submission tick in the submission step, the harvest's outcome and adjudications by seat, the stage table's state seats — and skips its own plants in the seating step by its own record (`Task.plant`), where it had read `c.plant` through the view. The `oracle` tell read `self.tasks[cid].plant` already (its own record); `freshname` reads `c.actor` (the boundary). The learners' observation (`redteam.obs`) reads none of the masked fields; `redteam.env`'s `is_plant` label for the probe reads the instrument's record.
- *Unchanged*: the engine, the History, I16; the aggregates the view publishes by design (`dial()`'s live plant counters as the orchestrator's instrument, `market()["available_roots"]` counting the plant, `money()["omega_in_plants"]`, `bank()`'s kinds).

**Checks.** The engine does not move: the 2.8.0 cell on seed 21 (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6 --dispute-policy truthful`) at `933fe46c` and on the corrected tree agree to the History head (`1aacb141…`, 125,886 entries, 85,538 events; false approvals 4, plant seats at the state 109, plants failed 70, the plants' lag 3.254). The goldens pass (the suite in record mode too). `test_v28`'s twelfth test: every masked field at its default before the reveal and passed through on a real root, the review proxies masked, the events' plant writes without case or note and a plant's ordinary events as a real case's, `plant_record` whole and refused on a real root, everything shown once the dispute panel resolves by truth, `check_all` at the end. The suite 357 / 0 / 32 in three modes at `e32a8db9`; `EXPECTED_FAIL` 32.

**Standing for the rc.** With this pass the Public view shows a plant as a real case from its posting to its reveal in everything but the alias names — the boundary the reviewer kept. For 2.9.0 (A): a planted approval recorded as a Review under a `plant_rev:` alias with `audit_odds_at_verdict` 1 and the obligation at the verdict would, on this view, differ from a member's approval by the alias name (the boundary) and by the odds field (`audit_odds_at_verdict` is every case's and is shown — a troop's own-delivery approval reads 1 there too, `market_audit_only`); the rc's review will say whether that field belongs with the masked ones or stays as a real case's. Nothing of 2.9.0 is built; the implementer is ready for rc.1.

## 64. The ruling on `e32a8db9` and on `audit_odds_at_verdict` (October 27, 2026), and the implementer's review of 2.9.0-rc.1 (`spec/design_revisions/dr17_2.9.0-rc.1-review.md`)

**The ruling, verbatim.**

> e32a8db9 is read and accepted: the class closed on _public_log's principle, the instrument on the orchestrator's road, the engine and goldens unmoved, 357/0/32. §63 recorded.
>
> audit_odds_at_verdict joins the masked fields, on the precedent you just landed: for a planted approval the public surface shows it at a value drawn from the trailing real approvals' odds until the reveal, the true 1 after; the roll reads the engine's 1 by the orchestrator's road (audit_odds(cid)), not the public field. A troop's own-delivery approval at 1 is a real case and stays as it is. Carry it into the rc.1 review as settled; the rc.2 text will state it under §2.6.

Carried into the review as settled; nothing of it built (the planted approval is 2.9.0's).

**The review (October 27, 2026; the rc at `uploads/agentic_economics_loop_spec_2.9.0-rc.1.md`, untracked; the evidence at `results/redteam/stability/2.9.0-rc1-review/`).** Implementable with three corrections to the rules as written, four corrections of fact and ten readings; nothing built.

- *Correction A — the planted approval's initial seat is nobody's, the summons pass included.* A submitted root with no initial review is a seat waiting: the driver's seating offers it to a member and the engine's pass, past w_R, gives a plant's seat to the orchestrator's own verdict (2.8.0 (C): N on a bad plant w.p. 0.95). The real verdict delay from submission is 1 tick for 85–88% of roots, 2 for 12–14%, 3 for ≤ 0.6% (seeds 21–23; the seating gap 0/1/2, the review always one tick); with w_R = 2 a planted approval drawn at delay 2–3 is a seat the pass may take before the planted F. The rc should mark the planted approval at posting and make its initial seat nobody's — `assign_initial` refuses it, the pass skips it — the verdict the orchestrator's own operation at the drawn delay.
- *Correction B — the obligation at the verdict is not `market_audit_only`.* That flag also means "only a member audits; never summoned for; counted as held", which contradicts the rc's own "or the orchestrator's verdict past w_R"; I25's predicate is about the state's deliveries. A mark of its own (`planted_approval`) with odds 1 and the obligation at the verdict, the audit seat filled at a real seat's step.
- *Correction C — the planted verdict's review is a system review.* `_new_review` checks the reviewer as an actor; a `plant_rev:` alias is none. The troop seat's road (`_summon_review`: the Review built directly, the bond locked through `Ledger.aliases`, seventeen `is_troop` guards) is the template, with two stated differences — no fixed fee, no adjudication (a fourth review outcome `planted`, exempt from I20's "without adjudication"); the bond released and destroyed with the pots (`retire_plant`), never `burn_bond`; the case's `plant_outcome` `planted` so `_adjudicate_plant` never runs and the dial's clock is untouched.
- *Facts:* `period["approvals"]` counts the plants' Fs too — π^A needs a real-only credit (`planted_approval_credit` at a real root's initial F; ~38–42 a run at the floor, ~760–850 at the cap on ~1,900–2,100 real approvals); the per-real-appeal/dispute rates must read the trailing closed period; the real challenges' delay windows are empty with disputes off (the floor of two then needs a stated fallback); the grounds are one string per kind in every standing cell and the panel's view carries it.
- *Readings on the nine points:* the alias's Review as correction C (draw the seating gap and keep the one-tick duration for the honest shape of its times); the approvals and audit demand at odds 1 follow `_initial_verdict`'s branch (+2.5% audit seats at the floor on ~1,500 held a run, +50% at the cap — the cost of truth's main term); the clock skipped by construction (only `_adjudicate_plant` moves F̂'s counts; `plant_credit` only at a real `assign_initial`; m^A moves π^A alone); the grounds one string; the floor of two feasible (~15 bad plants rejected and ~6 good approved a period) with the dispute panel a plant-only seat in the disputes-off cells and two bad plants' truthful N spent on panels; **(A) idle under disputes on, as expected** — the plant requester disputes at the real rate (75–101 real disputes a run, 96% won), `dispute()` admits a dispute over a standing obligation and the resolution by truth closes the case with the obligation left standing, clean under `check_all` (by hand); a real case rolled-then-disputed-and-lost would stay PENDING undischarged, which never happens in the standing cells (0 of 3–4 lost disputes a run) because the requesters dispute before the roll — a sentence letting the panel discharge a standing obligation is a conformance detail to land with the revision; the goldens move once if the scenario drives the new operations (I would extend it); `EXPECTED_FAIL` 32 stands; `test_v29`'s list as §7 plus the system review's money, the state kept off the planted approval's initial seat, and the masked `audit_odds_at_verdict` on a planted approval.
- *Settled by the ruling:* the public `audit_odds_at_verdict` of a planted approval drawn once at the verdict from a window of the real approvals' recorded odds (one more `_recent` column), stored, masked by `_masked`'s rule until the reveal; the roll reads the engine's 1.

Nothing of 2.9.0 is built before rc.2.

## 65. The ruling on the rc.1 review (October 27, 2026): A, B, C and the four facts accepted as stated; rc.2 to follow; the order after it

**The ruling, verbatim.**

> dr17 rc.1 review is read; rc.2 attached. A, B and C accepted as stated: the planted approval's initial seat is nobody's from submission to the orchestrator's verdict (assign_initial refuses, the summons pass skips, the waiting count may include it), the verdict by plant_verdict(cid) at the drawn position when the drawn seating gap has elapsed, t_assign = t_submit + gap, t_done = t_assign + 1; the obligation at the verdict under the case's own mark — not market_audit_only — with the audit seat at a real audit seat's step and the pass's plant branch past w_R; the verdict a system review on the troop seat's road under plant_rev: in Ledger.aliases to a plant reviewer wallet (a third plant wallet, so its line reads apart in the cost of truth), the bond mint_plant-ed and locked, cost 0, fee 0, outcome planted, is_system_reviewer in place of the seventeen is_troop guards with the two differences, I20 exempting planted and still failing a troop on a plant, the bond released and retired with the pots, never burn_bond. The four facts accepted: the planted-approval credit on real initial Fs with planted_approvals_due(); the lie rates on the trailing closed period's counts with the floor of two when the count is zero or the product under two; the lie's delay from the real window of the kind, truthful and lying alike, and the Appendix A default of 0 where empty; the grounds the population's string. audit_odds_at_verdict as settled, drawn once at the verdict and stored. Reading 6's detail adopted as a conformance item landed with the revision: a dispute's settlement discharges a standing obligation, a regression on a real case rolled, disputed and lost. Reading 10's extra reading adopted (the share of planted approvals the state's pass would have seated). The golden scenario extended to the new operations, the goldens moving once; EXPECTED_FAIL 32 strict both ways; test_v29 as listed in rc.2 §7. The dispute seat with disputes off becomes a plant-only seat at the floor — stated in §2.3 and read in §8.4 as the base rate it is.
>
> If rc.2 reads clean against the engine, implement behind the two flags; consolidate as 2.9.0 with 2.8.0 to history; then §8 in the rc's order — the careful basin with both rules first, honesty first, against the -280 chains on seeds 21–40.

**Standing.** rc.2 awaited (Baruchi uploads on the implementer's readiness). The implementation plan it fixes, for the record: a third plant wallet (`PLANT_REV`) with `plant_rev:` aliases; `plant_verdict(cid)` the orchestrator's operation; the case's mark `planted_approval` with the obligation at the verdict; `is_system_reviewer` over the seventeen guards; the review outcome `planted`; `planted_approval_credit` / `planted_approvals_due()` on real initial Fs; the three multipliers in `_maybe_end_period`; the public `audit_odds_at_verdict` drawn once from a window of the real approvals' odds and masked until the reveal; the conformance item — a dispute's settlement discharges a standing audit obligation — with its regression on a real case; the golden scenario extended; `test_v29`. Then the consolidation (spec v2_9_0, 2.8.0 to history) and §8.2's careful basin with both rules against `learned-rev-careful-280` on seeds 21–40, honesty first.

## 66. 2.9.0 — the plant takes the wrong step — implemented (October 27, 2026): rc.2 read clean (`dr17_2.9.0-rc.2-review.md`), accepted and built behind two flags; one reading made at the implementation; the smoke on seed 21; the consolidation

**rc.2 read clean against the engine** (`spec/design_revisions/dr17_2.9.0-rc.2-review.md`): it took the rc.1 review whole; two counts and one reading for the record — the "seventeen" `is_troop` guards are eight reviewer guards (now `is_system_reviewer`) and nine actor guards (unchanged); the planted verdict's position among the tick's real initial verdicts needs no second draw (the instrument's review pass walks the posting order, where the planted approval was posted at a drawn position); the lie's candidates (~15 bad plants rejected and ~6 good approved a period against a floor of two) — the quota a per-period credit, not carried.

**Built** (`Params.planted_approval`, `Params.planted_lie`, both on; `--rules 2.8.0` both off; `--planted-approval off` / `--planted-lie off` each alone):
- *Engine.* `PLANT_REV` the plant reviewer wallet behind `plant_rev:` aliases; `is_system_reviewer` over the eight reviewer guards (the alias as a troop, with no fee — `fee_owed` 0 — and no adjudication); `post_plant(truth, approval)` marks the case (`Case.planted_approval`) and draws on `planted_approval_credit` (incremented by π^A at each real root's initial F in `_initial_verdict`, which also feeds a window of the real approvals' recorded odds); `plant_seat(cid)` (the system review on the troop seat's road, the bond `mint_plant`-ed and locked, `t_assign` the seat's tick) and `plant_verdict(cid)` the next tick (F, outcome `planted`, `audit_odds_at_verdict` 1, `audit_odds_public` drawn once by `_plant_draw("odds")`, the obligation at the verdict, `open_items` +1, the period's approvals and audit demand +1, the case PENDING); `assign_initial` refuses a planted approval and the summons pass passes its initial seat by (reading `pa_state_would_seat` where 2.8.0's pass would have fired); `_live_audit_odds` 1 under the mark; the lie read at `_open_challenge` from the kind and the truth (`challenge["lie"]`), the real appeals and disputes counted apart; the dial per seat in `_maybe_end_period` (F̂^A from the planted approvals adjudicated and confirmed at the audit seat, F̂^La / F̂^Ld from the lies' panel seats; `m_A`, `m_La`, `m_Ld` stepping as m_t, each its rate alone; the lies' quotas `max(2, π^L × the period's real count)` for the next period); `planted_approvals_due()`, `planted_lies_due(kind)`; the closure retiring the third wallet; the reveal naming a planted approval and its public odds (keys only on one, so the 2.8.0 record is unchanged to the hash); the mints and retirements tallied by kind (dial / approval / lie / rev). The conformance detail of §4: `_discharge_obligation_at_dispute` at both settlements (`_settle_challenge`, `_settle_plant_challenge`): a standing obligation with no audit seat held is discharged, `open_items` −1, counted. I43, I44 new; I20 (the `planted` outcome exempt; the reveal's conditional keys), I42 (the alias's review never overturned), I21 (the three multipliers' steps) amended; `_PLANT_WALLETS` carries the third wallet.
- *The reading made at the implementation (Appendix V).* The golden scenario surfaced a 2.8.0 path the planted approval makes common: with a dispute open on a case whose audit obligation still stands, the summons pass's audit branch fired past w_R and `_state_verdict_on_plant(c, "audit")` joined the open dispute as a `state_verdicts` entry — a **third** answer beside two seated panelists — and `_maybe_settle_plant_challenge` (answered ≥ 2) settled the panel by truth with one panelist still open; the panelist's verdict then landed after the reveal, and I20 caught the reveal as not the committed one. By the rc's §4 principle (the dispute's panel judges the approval; the obligation is discharged at its settlement) the standing obligation is **not a seat required while the dispute is open**: the pass's audit branch and the reserve's waiting count (`_waiting_reviews`) now require `challenge is None or resolved`, and the driver's seating diagnostics the same. Every rule version carries it (a conformance reading; the 2.8.0 control on seed 21 is unchanged to the History head, 1aacb141…, since in the standing cells the requesters dispute before the roll). Recorded in the accepted text's header note.
- *The view.* `plant_seat`, `plant_verdict`, `planted_approvals_due`, `planted_lies_due`, `post_plant(truth, approval)`; `_PLANT_FIELD_DEFAULTS` + `planted_approval`, `audit_odds_public`, `pa_state_would_seat`; `audit_odds_at_verdict` masked for a planted approval to the stored draw until the reveal (the ruling of October 27); `money()` counts the third wallet; `dial()` carries the credits due, the quotas, the rates and multipliers per seat, the discharges and the mints by kind.
- *The driver.* `--rules 2.9.0` the default (every version tuple widened); the planted approvals posted at drawn positions among the tick's postings with the dial's plants (`plant_step(t, 1, approval=True)`); the planted approval's seat nobody's in the seating step — the instrument seats the review when the drawn gap (from `seat_gap_window`, the real roots' `t_assign − t_submit` at assignment) has elapsed and gives the verdict in the next review pass at the case's own place; the audit seat wanted from the verdict as a troop's own-delivery audit is; the lies in `plant_respond` (a bad plant's N while `planted_lies_due("appeal")` less the lies pending stands, the delay from every real appeal's window, default 0) and the challenge pass (a good plant's F while the dispute quota stands); the readings: `pa_posted/seated/verdicts/closed/audited/confirmed/rejected/third/disputed/state_audit/state_would_seat`, `plant_lies_filed_*`, `lie_adj_*`, `lie_sided_*`, `pi_A/m_A/pi_La/m_La/pi_Ld/m_Ld`, `F_hat_A/La/Ld` (the last period's), `obligations_discharged`, `bad_met_<seat kind>` (the bad plants the members met by seat, the count the record sees), `panel_ineligible_member_periods` / `member_periods`, `life_lie_filing_delay`, `plant_mint_{dial,approval,lie,rev}` and `plant_retire_rev` (the shared plant wallets' retirement cannot be split by case; the plant reviewer wallet's nets to zero by construction, its bonds released and retired whole).
- *Tests.* `test_v29` (12): the worked example's spine; the third seat; the dispute first with the discharge; the real case rolled, disputed and lost vesting; the two lies and a truthful challenge not a lie; the dial per seat; the credit and the quota; the auditor's view equal to a real pending F's and the public surface masked until the reveal; the flags off and the refusals; the alias paid nothing beside paid members; I43/I44/I42/I20 catching the state on the seat, a fee on the alias, a burned bond, a lie without its fee. The golden scenario posts planted approvals (π^A_0 0.2 in the scenario so the audit path is reached), seats and answers them, files lies while the quota stands; the goldens re-pinned once (seed 1: 95953f96… / 10,327 from cd9c224c… / 9,928; seed 2: b678a8b5… / 10,878 from 4396ace2… / 9,718). `EXPECTED_FAIL` 32, strict both ways.

## 67. Run 38 — §8.2's first cell: the careful basin with both 2.9.0 rules, against 2.8.0 on seeds 21–40 (October 27, 2026)

**The cell.** `--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6 --learner-pop all --learner-role reviewer --learner-exit rule` under 2.9.0 (both flags on), the chain as the 2.8.0 side ran it (the careful-weighted offline set, ten online rounds, the direct the carrier and the head alone beside; `results/redteam/stability/learned-rev-careful-290/`), the finals on seeds 21–40 paired by seed against `learned-rev-careful-280/` with the same references on the same slots. The pairing and the readings at `results/redteam/stability/2.9.0-s82/` (`finals-paired.py` → `finals-paired.md`; the dial per seat's path per period on seeds 21–23 by hand, `hand-path-3seeds.txt`, and in the learned population under the direct final, `learned-path-3seeds.txt`; who takes the audit seat by case kind, `audit-split-3seeds.txt`). One driver fix on the way (`9636cbf8`): the liar cell's lying dispute read a period index only the truthful branch set — the cell failed at its first lying dispute; no result on record had run that path. The suite 369 / 0 / 32 in three modes at the fix.

**Honesty first — the basin moves a little, not to the hand's.** Both learners settle at care 0.72 / approve blind 0.28 (the direct 0.716 / 0.278 / 0.006, the head alone 0.724 / 0.275 / 0.001) from 2.8.0's 0.68–0.69 / 0.31 — paired by seed +0.035 ± 0.008 and +0.034 ± 0.010 care. The standing false approvals 31.4 ± 1.2 (the direct; −7.0 ± 2.0 against its 2.8.0 chain) and 30.6 ± 2.2 (the head alone; −0.5 ± 2.7) against the hand's 11.2 — 2.8× and 2.7× the hand's (from 3.4× and 2.7×), every seed above the hand on both. The bad plants approved −2.8 ± 2.7 / −9.4 ± 2.1, the suspensions −1.4 ± 0.8 / −0.5 ± 1.0, the audit layer's overturn rate 0.062 / 0.054 beside the hand's 0.063 (+0.002 ± 0.003 / −0.008 ± 0.003), F̂ 0.21 / 0.18 (+0.001 ± 0.027 / −0.030 ± 0.036). By seat, pooled over the seeds: **the audit seat is stamped 0.69 (plant) / 0.76 (real) by the direct and 0.89 / 0.90 by the head alone** (2.8.0: 0.77 / 0.81 and 0.98 / 0.98; per seed −0.066 ± 0.023 / −0.046 ± 0.018 and −0.074 ± 0.021 / −0.071 ± 0.019); the first review 0.20 / 0.23 and 0.17 / 0.20 (−0.018 ± 0.006 / −0.014 ± 0.009 and −0.026 ± 0.008 / −0.031 ± 0.014); the panels 0.23 / 0.22 by the direct (from 0.16 / 0.27: +0.069 ± 0.021 / −0.056 ± 0.030) and 0.14 / 0.08 by the head alone (from 0.35 / 0.38: −0.219 ± 0.034 / −0.298 ± 0.026). The mix by period: care 0.56–0.58 in periods 1–2 (2.8.0: 0.53–0.54), 0.77–0.81 in periods 6–9 (0.69–0.73). Run 36's finding stands at the same order: the seat the planted approval was built to price is stamped seven to nine times in ten.

**The planted approval's life in the population — the lever reaches its stop before the seat is priced.** The chains post 90 planted approvals a run (the hand 50, the careful 49). Of them **17 (the direct) and 21 (the head alone) are audited by a member; 72 and 68 by the state past w_R** — 80% and 75%, against 29–30% in the hand's and the careful's populations. This is the population's standing, not the instrument's path: 95% and 94% of the learned populations' member-periods are panel-ineligible (θ_panel 0.20 against a population that stamps a fifth to a quarter of its first reviews; the hand's 33%, the careful's 53% — the undetermined arrivals), so every audit seat goes mostly to the state — on seeds 21–23 the state takes 75–86% of *every* other root's audit seats and 73–88% of the planted approvals' (the hand 30% and 28%): the planted approval reaches a member's audit seat at the rate the real audits do. The real audit obligations are 1,900–2,000 a run in both populations (the odds pinned at 1 for the probationers and doubled on failures). **At the members' audit seats the planted approvals are confirmed 0.72 (345 seats) and 0.90 (428)** — the hand's 0.045, the careful's 0.061 — **the same rate as the real approvals' stamps there** (0.76 and 0.90): the planted approval is not told apart at the seat (H4 holds there). The dial per seat reads it: F̂^A 0.5–1.0 in the periods a member meets one (one to four adjudications a period; undefined in the rest, the multiplier holding), **m^A steps ×2 each time and reaches m_max = 4 by period 3 on seeds 22 and 23 and by period 10 on seed 21** (the path in `learned-path-3seeds.txt`), 3.92 and 4.00 at the end over the twenty seeds; **π^A 0.078 and 0.080 — and there it stops: π^A = π^A_0 m^A ≤ π^A_0 m_max = 0.08.** The rate's cap π^A_max 0.40 is unreachable as the rule stands (§5: 1 ≤ m ≤ m_max); the rc's parameter table expected "~800 a run at the cap", the chains reach 90. At the stop the planted approvals are 4.5% of the real approvals, and the 17–21 that reach a member's audit seat are 4–6% of the members' audit decisions there (the bad plants met at the audit seat per one met at the first review 0.08–0.09, the hand's 0.13, the careful's 0.19) — a price under care's, as run 36's arithmetic has it. **H1's cap branch, as the rule stands: the multiplier rises to the cap and the seat is not priced.** Two facts for the reviewer, neither mine to move: (i) the bound — m^A shares m_t's m_max, so the planted approvals' rate can reach four times its floor and never its own cap (the text's expectation and its rule disagree; the multiplier's own bound, π^A_max / π^A_0 = 20, is the arithmetic's candidate, not drafted); (ii) the seat — in this population three audit seats in four are the state's, so at any rate a planted approval reaches a member at the population's own eligibility share, and the state's rejection of it (ε_T) is counted nowhere in F̂^A (rightly: it is no member's answer).

**The planted lies.** The planted appeals 30.6 / 28.6 a run (the hand 33.5; π^La 0.42 / 0.40, m^La 1.68 / 1.59 at the end), their panel seats answered by members 6.6 / 6.8 (the hand 50.7 — the panels go to the state as the audits do), the members siding with the lie 0.144 / 0.139 pooled against the hand's 0.054 and the careful's 0.043 (F̂^La). The planted disputes 25.6 / 25.4 — the floor of two a period in a cell whose real requesters never dispute — their seats answered by members 6.2 / 7.3 (the hand 36.4), siding 0.024 / 0.014 (the hand 0.045, the careful 0.055: a careful reviewer's own error), m^Ld 1.08 / 1.06. **A reading on the lie dial in the honest population:** with the floor's two lies a period the dial reads four seat-adjudications a period, and one careful error among them is F̂ 0.25 against F* 0.10 — a ×2 step; the hand's m^Ld reaches 4.0 (seed 21 period 9, seed 22 period 4) and m^La 2.7 on single errors, and the quotas follow (π^La 0.675 → 8.8 planted appeals in a period of 13 real ones). The counts the step rule reads at the floor are too small for it; the rate it moves is the honest population's cost (`plant_mint_lie` 116 a run in the hand's, 72 in the careful's — small in money, since a lie's filing is a court fee minted and destroyed; in seats, the panels the floor's lies open). Named, not drafted.

**The honest populations are unmoved.** Paired 2.9.0 − 2.8.0 on the hand: the false approvals −0.2 ± 0.9, the bad plants approved +0.5 ± 2.3, the good rejected −1.8 ± 0.9, the suspensions −0.3 ± 0.9, the overturn rate +0.001 ± 0.002, the nominal surplus −430 ± 429, the real lifetime +1,337 ± 786, tasks per 1,000 +0.8 ± 0.4, P_t −1.6 ± 0.7, the members' price −0.7 ± 0.3, the requesters' wallet +56 ± 30, the awards +20 ± 32 — the price and the customers' two readings at two SE, the rest inside one; the careful: the false approvals +0.7 ± 0.7, the bad −1.0 ± 0.8, the good −0.7 ± 0.6, the nominal −207 ± 432, the real +360 ± 691, tasks +0.2 ± 0.5, P_t −0.3 ± 0.4. The cost of truth: the hand 4,217 ± 61, the careful 2,070 ± 26, the learned 2,968 ± 60 / 3,014 ± 53 (the finals carry no 2.8.0 column for it; §8.6 reads it from the hand cells).

**The population's economy and the state.** The direct's members −560 ± 253 nominal against their 2.8.0 chain, the head alone's +204 ± 256; the real lifetime +164 ± 583 / −187 ± 364; tasks per 1,000 +0.2 ± 0.4 / −0.0 ± 0.3; P_t −0.5 ± 0.5 / +0.4 ± 0.3; the state's share −0.006 ± 0.011 / +0.017 ± 0.010; the awards +25 ± 49 / −30 ± 39; idle for bond −37 ± 29 / +56 ± 24. The state's seats per plant +0.377 ± 0.019 / +0.282 ± 0.016 (the planted approvals' audits past w_R; the hand +0.083 ± 0.012, the careful +0.194 ± 0.014), per real root +0.050 ± 0.014 / −0.037 ± 0.013. Within 2.9.0 against the hand: the dishonest basin still pays nothing — the members −1,350 ± 379 / −820 ± 369 nominal, −4,683 ± 673 / −6,029 ± 596 real, the customers −2.1 ± 0.3 / −3.2 ± 0.3 tasks per 1,000, P_t +2.6 ± 0.5 / +3.9 ± 0.5. The stage table: the plants' audited-by-a-member share under the real roots' by 0.089 ± 0.007 / 0.101 ± 0.006 period-matched and the state's seats over by 0.060 ± 0.010 / 0.056 ± 0.008 — the planted approvals (every one an obligation; four in five the state's) — and the filed share over by 0.068 ± 0.001 (the planted lies at the floor on the plants); the two new stages are §8.4's check.

**Classification.** An evaluation under fixed rules (§19), recorded against the rule as run 36's was: with both 2.9.0 rules the careful basin's audit-seat capture closes by five to seven points and the standing false approvals by a fifth on one learner and not on the other; the planted approval does what the text says at every step and the dial per seat reads the capture and answers it to its bound; the bound is where the lever stops, and the seat behind it is the state's three times in four. Nothing of mine moves; the two facts above are the reviewer's. Next in the rc's order: the liar chain with both rules (relaunched after the fix), the stamper basin (running), then the ablations ((A) alone on the careful basin, (B) alone on the liar chain) and the half if time allows.

**A second reading on the way to the liar chain (the same day).** The liar cell's offline collection (`--dispute-policy truthful --appeal-lie 0.1 --dispute-lie 0.1`) failed I25 on eighteen of its episodes — "the state's own work vested without the market's audit" — reproduced by hand (`--stampers 1.0 --entry-stampers 1.0` with the liar flags, seed 5). The path: a troop's delivery approved by a troop reviewer carries the market's audit as an obligation held for a member (1.4.0 §2.2; I25: it vests only after a member's audit, never a troop's); a requester disputes that approval (a lying requester at 0.1 here; a truthful one where the state's bad work was approved — rarer, and the disputes-on chain's path too), the panel settles, and 2.9.0 §4's discharge — "a dispute's settlement discharges a standing audit obligation, every case" — dropped the obligation; the case vested unaudited. §4 and I25 contradict on this one case. The engine keeps the obligation standing on the state's own work whatever the panel found, so 1.4.0's rule stands and the conformance detail covers the obligations that are rolls (the planted approval's and the real roots'); — and the test that pins it found the rest: after the dispute the one challenge round (1.0.1 §8) refuses any audit seat, so the state's own work stays pending with its obligation and is read as held for the market's audit (`held_market_audit_now`), as it has on this path since 1.4.0 (never exercised — a truthful requester meets it where the state's bad delivery was approved by a troop reviewer, b·ε_T of the state's deliveries, a handful in a thousand; the lying requester at 0.1 meets it at every troop-approved state delivery). Three rules meet on the one case: the one round, the market's audit of the state's own work (I25), the discharge at settlement (§4). Recorded as the second reading in Appendix V with the engine's conduct pinned (`test_v29` 13); **the decision is the reviewer's** — the panel read as the market's judgment where a member sat on it (I25 amended), or the audit seat admitted after the dispute for the state's own work (the one round amended), or the case left standing as it is. The discharge's "every case" was my reading 6 and I did not see this case; the implementer's. No chain on record had run the path (the careful and stamper basins have no disputes); the liar chain relaunched after the fix, the stamper chain unaffected by it.

The lying dispute's pay fell from 4.4% to 2.6–2.8% against the hand's 1.9%. By the seat-kind table on seeds 21–23 (`seat-table-liars-290-direct.txt` against `-280-`): at the appeal seat the population's care on a *real* appeal rose from 0.22–0.31 to 0.55–0.56 (bad and good work alike), at the dispute seat from 0.66–0.71 to 0.76–0.84, at the audit seat it stayed 0.08–0.18. The honest populations: the hand's standing false approvals +0.3 ± 0.4, the careful's +0.6 ± 0.4, the hand's awards and tasks per credit within noise; one movement outside it, in the careful population — the members' nominal surplus +1,093 ± 369 and P_t +1.3 ± 0.6 with tasks per credit −0.8 ± 0.4 — both nominal, the planted approvals' minted pots (1,972 a run, two panel seats each paid to members) the candidate carrier, not established.

**The planted approval where disputes are on: every one is disputed first, and the audit seat is never reached.** The 2.9.0 readings of the liar cell (`finals-paired.md`, the readings block; `dial-path-liars-3seeds.txt`): 37 planted approvals a run (π^A at its floor on ~1,850 real approvals), 37 disputed first, 37 obligations discharged at the settlement, 0 audited by a member, 0 by the state, F̂^A undefined in every period, m^A 1.00 and π^A 0.020 from the first period to the last — on every seed, under every policy, the hand's included. The plant requester disputes a bad plant's pending F at the real requesters' rate (2.8.0 §2.1), and the truthful requesters of this cell dispute every false approval they see, so the rate is one and the planted approval's dispute always comes before its audit; the obligation is discharged by §4 (the conformance detail of §65, exercised here 37 times a run, I25 holding). So in a world with disputes the planted approval tests the *dispute panel*, not the audit seat: the bad plants met at a dispute panel 130–133 a run against the hand's 96 (the planted approvals' panels), the dispute seat the population already cared at (0.66 → 0.81), and (A)'s own dial never reads a thing. The audit seat in this world audits approvals of good work — of the learned population's 2,077 audit decisions on seeds 21–23, 22 were on bad work (the false approvals having been disputed away first) — so a stamp there is all but free and nothing of (A) reaches it: stamped 0.83–0.97, the one seat that did not move. Both facts for the reviewer: (A)'s seat is the audit seat, and the audit seat is reached by a bad approval only where its requester does not dispute; with the lever (A) alone the cell to read is the one without disputes (§67, and the stamper basin below), with disputes the dispute panel is its seat and it was not the captured one.

**The planted lie prices the appeal seat — and is told apart there.** The lie dial reads: π^La 0.61–0.63 (m^La 2.45–2.53 on the real appeals' trailing count), π^Ld 0.62–0.71 (m^Ld 2.47–2.84); the planted appeals filed 41–63 a run (the hand's 49), the planted disputes 77–93 (the hand's 72); the members side with a planted lie at the appeal panel 8.7–9.2% and at the dispute panel 7.0–8.8% (the hand's 5.5 / 5.2 — a careful's error rate at a panel). The lie dial's step on the floor's seats a period (§67) is here too: m^La wanders 1.0 → 4.0 → 1.3 within a run on two to seventeen seats a period (`dial-path-liars-3seeds.txt`, the hand's path the same). **The seat-kind table showed something the pooled panel rate hid**: on seeds 21–23 the learned population cares 0.81 at a *planted lie's* appeal (a bad plant's N appealed) and 0.55 at a real appeal — bad work's or good work's, 0.55 and 0.56 — and 0.54 at the truthful plant appeal (2.8.0's class). The planted lie is answered with more care than every other appeal, the real liar's included. On six seeds (21–26; `appeal-tell-liars-290-direct.txt`) 0.85 against 0.50 / 0.52 / 0.51; the real lying appeal stamped 0.48 where the planted one is stamped 0.06. **The probe (`appeal_tell.py`: the 45-field observation at every panel decision, a logistic probe on the whole vector with leave-one-seed-out folds, the per-field standardized difference and single-field AUC):** the planted lie's appeal against the real appeals reads AUC 0.80 (0.81–0.89 by seed) under the learned population and **0.87 under the hand's** (0.84–0.93) — one field carries it, `period_pos`, the position within the period: the planted lie's appeals sit at 0.05–0.065 of a period and the real appeals at 0.18–0.22 (d 1.1–1.6; alone AUC 0.81 / 0.86); every other field under 0.6 alone. The controls on the same decisions: the truthful plant appeal against the real appeals 0.56 / 0.53 (no tell, as §54 read it); the dispute seat 0.57–0.60 with `period_pos` at d 0.3–0.35 on the planted lie's dispute (the same mechanism, diluted); the initial seat 0.66 / 0.64 carried by `dial_m`/`dial_pi` and the member's own record — composition, the standing probe's reading (§53's residual is on the case fields; these are the context's). **The mechanism is the instrument's (`drivers/stamping.py`, `plant_respond` and the challenge pass's lie branch):** the engine sets the period's lie quota at the boundary (`lie_quota` in `_maybe_end_period`) and the driver takes a planted lie at *every* eligible step (a bad plant's N in `plant_respond`, a good plant's pending F in the pass) while `planted_lies_due(kind) − lies_pending > 0` — so the quota is spent on the first eligible plants of the period, and the planted lies are filed in the period's first ticks plus the drawn delay (the real appeals' delays are short), where the real appeals and disputes are spread over the period. Under the hand's population the same (AUC 0.87): the tell is the filing's timing, not anything learned. **Two consequences, stated:** (i) the learned population learned the position — care 0.85 at the planted lie's seat against 0.50 at the real liar's — so **the lie dial's F̂^La (0.09–0.10) is not the real appeal seat's answer: the real lying appeal is stamped 0.48 on six seeds**, and m^La at 2.5 is read against a seat the population tells from the real one; the dial per seat is blind by the tell; (ii) the fall of the lying appeal's pay from 23–31% to 10% is partly the seat priced (the population's care on real appeals 0.22 → 0.50) and partly not — the planted lies are a third of the appeals the panels see and the population answers them apart. **Classification: a conformance item of the orchestrator's instrument** — the eighth of its class (the plant's seating gap, the created-submitted plant's posting tick, the floor at w_A, the first period's draw, the raise refused, the `log` entries, the public neighbours; §24, §50, §53, §61, §63), the first on a challenge step. The text (§2.3): "a planted lie takes the real challenge's path — filed in the pass at a drawn position, at a delay drawn from the trailing real challenges' window of the kind" — the position is within the pass; the rate is per real challenge of the trailing period; the principle (Appendix S, run 24's acceptance) is that at every step the instrument takes for a plant, the plant takes the step in the same pass a real root would, at a drawn position among the tick's real roots taking it, drawn from the trailing real roots' — the real challenges of a period are spread across it and the planted ones are not. **Proposed, not made (ruled first, as every plant-path correction was):** spend the quota at the real challenges' moments — at each real appeal (dispute) filed in the pass the instrument draws a planted lie of the kind with probability π^La_t (π^Ld_t), filed in the same pass on a bad plant's N (a good plant's pending F) at hand, else held for the next; the floor of two filed at two ticks drawn from the period where the coin yields fewer; the expectation π^L × this period's real count, the trailing period's count the text's. The `period_pos` tell is then the real challenges' own distribution. The ablation (B) alone (`liars-B`, launched on the instrument as it stands, the precedent after §50) is re-run on the corrected instrument if the item is adopted; the liar chain's lie readings above are read with the tell. The probe itself (`appeal_tell.py`, the seat-kind decisions with their observations) is proposed as the standing check at every panel kind in the population cells, beside the stage table.

## 69. The rulings after runs 38 and 39 (October 27, 2026): the defaults correction, four rulings, the eighth conformance item, the second reading ruled — landed; the channel reading for fact (ii)

**The reviewer's notes, verbatim.**

> Runs 38 and 39 are read and recorded from §67–§68's summaries; the file completes the record when it comes.
>
> Fact (i) is my defaults error, corrected as a default under §19: m^A_max = 20 (π^A_0 0.02 → π^A_max 0.40), m^La_max = m^Ld_max = 8 (0.25 → 2.0); the dial's own m_max 4 unchanged. Two rulings at acceptance under Appendix V: the per-seat multipliers step on a reading of at least ten adjudications, pooled forward until reached (the lie dial's ×2 on one error at four seats is the reading); and a planted approval's audit seat is a member's or nobody's — the state's verdict never takes it, an unseated planted approval closes by truth at its window's end, spent and read — with the rate's base the real approvals audited by members in the trailing period, so the planted approvals scale with the market's audit capacity and not the state's share. The careful basin is re-run at the true stop with these in.
>
> Fact (ii) is the finding: in the dishonest basin the audit layer is the state's — the record and the tiers doing what they were written to do, the state carrying the gap at the audit seat as it carried it at the delivery in run 6 — and the standing false approvals that remain are the first review's stamps the roll never sends to audit: the odds rule counts every vested review as a survival (streak), audited or not, so a reviewer wrong on five or six approvals in a hundred decays to the floor. The capture has moved from the seat's verdict to the roll's odds. Before any design, one reading from run 38's finals on seeds 21–40, both learners, the hand beside: the standing false approvals split by channel — audited and confirmed by a member, audited and confirmed by the state, never audited. If never-audited carries it, the next cells are defaults reads first (θ_duty 0.5 → 0.3; p_floor · m_t), and a survival counted only where the verdict was audited is the rule I would draft as 2.10.0 on the reading — not before.
>
> Fact (iii) as expected, nothing to change. Fact (iv) adopted as the eighth conformance item with your fix (the coin per real challenge in the pass; the floor at ticks drawn from the real spread, uniform where empty; the stage check gains the position); the lie dial's readings on the uncorrected instrument are void; the liar chain with both and (B) alone on liars re-run on the corrected instrument; the careful (A)-alone ablation stands. The nominal movement in the liar cell's careful population is the planted lies' panel fees from minted pots — a reading, split in §8.6.
>
> Appendix V's second reading, ruled: a dispute's panel on the state's own work is the market's judgment where a member sat on it and discharges I25's obligation as a member's audit would; where the panel was the state's alone, the audit seat is admitted after the dispute — the one challenge round yields to I25's purpose for the state's own work, the round existing to limit a participant's exposure and the state being none; a case is never left standing. test_v29 carries both branches, the eighteen episodes the regression. The fixed engine's I25 hold stands as it is until this lands.

**Landed (the implementing commit; Appendix V's closing paragraph, Appendix A's two rows, Appendix S's eighth item, §2.2–§2.4's rule text amended in place).** *The defaults:* `Params.m_A_max` 20, `m_L_max` 8, `seat_dial_min_adj` 10; the per-seat step reads a pooled numerator and denominator (`_seat_pool`, carried forward across boundaries and reset when a reading is taken; the period record's `seat_pool` and the raw period counts beside), each multiplier within its own bound (I21, I43, I44 on the new bounds). *The base:* π^A credited at each real approval's audit completed by a member (`_audit_result`; the period's `real_member_audits`), nothing at the initial F — my reading of "the real approvals audited by members in the trailing period": credited as the audits occur rather than as the trailing period's count set at the boundary, the lie item's reading and the same expectation; the trailing count is in the record for the reviewer to prefer. *The seat:* the pass's audit branch passes a planted approval by (`pa_pass_would_audit`, read), I43 fails a state seat on one; at the vesting step a planted approval whose obligation stands at its window's end with no audit review ever held closes by truth — the obligation dropped, final N, `pa_unseated`, the reveal naming it (`unseated`), not adjudicated and not in F̂^A (`planted_approvals_unseated`, the period's `pa_unseated`). *The second reading:* `_discharge_obligation_at_dispute` on the state's own work discharges where a panelist was a member (`state_work_discharged_by_panel`; I25 accepts the panel) and otherwise marks `audit_after_dispute` (`state_work_audit_after_dispute`), which `assign_audit` honours past the used round and `_new_review` admits as one position beyond V_max (I8 amended); the summons pass never seats the state on it (I25's "never a troop's" stands); `test_v29` 13 rewritten to the state-alone branch (the seat admitted, a member's audit, the case vests) and 14 added for the member-on-the-panel branch; the eighteen liar-cell episodes are the chain's regression (the collection re-runs under the corrected engine). *The eighth conformance item (the driver):* at each real root appeal or dispute filed in the pass a coin at π^L of the kind wants a planted lie (`lies_wanted`, `plant_lies_coin`), filed in the same pass on the next bad plant's N or good plant's pending F at hand; at each period's start two ticks of each kind are drawn from the trailing real challenges' offsets in their periods (uniform over `period_ticks` where the window is empty) and the floor is filed there where the coin has filed fewer (`lie_step`, `plant_lies_floor`); the engine's `planted_lies_due` is the floor's shortfall, `lie_quota` the expected count kept as the record; the stage table carries the filing position (`life_real_filing_pos`, `life_plant_filing_pos`, `life_lie_filing_pos`). **Checked under the hand on the liar cell, six seeds (`2.9.0-s82/appeal-tell-liars-hand-corrected-driver.txt`):** the appeal-seat probe reads AUC 0.58 (0.52–0.65 by seed) from 0.87, `period_pos` at d 0.22 (0.174 against 0.201) from 1.56, the top field now `members_free` at d 0.22 — composition's level, the initial seat's own reading; the dispute seat 0.54 from 0.60; the filing positions on seed 21: real 0.26, the plants' truthful challenges 0.22, the lies 0.21 of a period. `test_v29` 17 tests, the suite 372 / 0 / 32 in three modes, the goldens re-pinned once (every planted approval's posting tick moves with the base).

**Fact (ii)'s reading: the standing false approvals by channel (`2.9.0-s82/fa-channels-careful-290.md`, `fa_channels.py`; run 38's finals on seeds 21–40 under the engine of record, the careful basin's direct and head alone, the hand beside).** A standing false approval is a real root (not a plant, not the state's delivery) vested with its work unfulfilled; the channels: its audit seat confirmed it — a member's or the state's — it was audited and rejected yet vested (the third seat or a ruling), or it was never audited.

| direct | 31.2 ± 1.2 | 19.9 ± 1.0 | 4.3 ± 0.4 | 1.6 ± 0.3 | 5.3 ± 0.6 | 0.71 |

**Never-audited does not carry it; the member's confirmation at the audit seat does.** Of the learned population's excess over the hand, four fifths (16.8 of 20.4; 14.8 of 19.2) are false approvals a member audited and confirmed — the audit seat's stamp, on the quarter of audit seats the members hold; the never-audited channel is a sixth to a quarter of the standing total in every population (the hand's included: 3.9 of 10.8) and adds +1.4 ± 0.8 / +3.2 ± 0.8 against the hand; the state's confirmations are ε_T's (+1.2) and the ruled-F route under one. The never-audited cases carry odds at the verdict of 0.71–0.72 in the learned populations against 0.56 in the hand's — a reviewer with high odds whose roll came up blank; the roll never sent them, as the reviewer says, but they are few. So the capture has not moved to the roll's odds: it is at the seat's verdict still, and the seat that confirms is the members' audit seat, 0.69–0.90 stamped (§67) — the planted approval's seat, which the ruling above now keeps for the members alone and prices at the true stop. By the reviewer's branch the defaults reads (θ_duty, p_floor·m_t) are not indicated by this reading; the reading is the reviewer's. ## 71. Run 41 — the half on the corrected engine (October 28, 2026): the lever moves where the members hold the seat; one invariant corrected

**The cell.** `learned-rev-half-290/` — `--learner-pop half --learner-role reviewer --learner-exit rule` on the corrected engine (511c840b's rules, every round and the finals on it; the finals re-run on a1826d26f, below), paired against `learned-rev-half-280/` (§54) on seeds 21–40; the pairing at `2.9.0-s82/finals-paired-half.md`, the channel split `fa-channels-half-290.md`, the fee split `seat-income-half-290.md`, the appeal-seat probe `appeal-tell-half-290-direct.txt` (the direct's policy, seeds 21–26). The honest references on the same seeds: the hand (60/40 by hand on the identical slots) and all-careful.

**An invariant corrected first (a1826d26f; the suite 373 / 0 / 32 in three modes, 777fff916).** The direct chain's round-10 evaluation and its final stopped at seed 36 on `I21 m_A step 7.272727 → 14.545455 outside its bounds`. The engine's step is exact — `_step` clamps the ratio at ×2, 7.2727… × 2 — and the period record stores the multipliers rounded to six decimals; I21 compared the rounded pair with a tolerance of 1e-6, and 2 × 7.272727 + 1e-6 is 14.545454999… in floating point, under 14.545455. A check of the engine misread the record; the engine misapplied nothing, no rule moves (§19's first row's neighbour: the invariant corrected, not the engine). I21 now reads a step's bounds with a tolerance of 2e-6 — the rounding at both ends — for the dial's own step and the three per-seat steps alike; a step beyond ×2 or under ×0.9 on the record is still a failure (`test_v29` 18: the pair built at a period's close, the failure cases on a copy of the record, the record itself being hashed into the History). The exposure was this cell's alone: it is the first where m^A climbs past small integers (the careful and liar basins hold it at 1.00; (A) alone 1.15; the half's seed 36 reached 14.5). Every training round ran clean (the collector marks an errored episode and none is marked); the head-alone chain's final ran clean; the direct's round-10 evaluation and final were re-run on the corrected invariant, the model unchanged.

**H1 — the half.** Both learners settle at **care 0.78 / 0.82** (2.8.0's 0.74 / 0.81; the direct +0.038 ± 0.008 paired, the head alone +0.006 ± 0.010). The standing false approvals **18.7 ± 1.0 / 16.0 ± 1.1 against the hand's 10.4 ± 0.8 — 1.79× / 1.53×** (2.8.0's 1.99× / 1.61×), **−4.0 ± 1.7 / −2.4 ± 1.6 paired**, above the hand on 19 and 18 seeds of 20.
