**The lever moves here.** The base — the members' audits of real approvals — is **1,082 ± 31 / 964 ± 21 a run against the hand's 979**: the half's membership holds the audit seats as the hand's does (the state's seats per real root 0.325 / 0.312 against 0.316; 52% / 44% of member-periods panel-ineligible against the hand's 34%). So the planted approvals are **50.8 / 34.0 a run (the hand's 19.9)**, every one audited by a member (the state's share 0.000; the pass would have taken 3.6 / 2.9 — read, not taken; unseated 0.1), confirmed 10.5 / 8.1 of 49.0 / 33.1 — **pooled F̂^A 0.214 (980) / 0.243 (662) against the hand's 0.043 (395)**; the pool reaches ten by period 7, and **m^A ends at 7.63 / 4.52 (the hand's 1.04), π^A 0.153 / 0.090** — seed 21's path 1 → 2 (period 8) → 4 (period 11); seed 22's 1 → 2 → 1.82 (one reading at 1 in 8 steps it ×0.909); seed 36 to 14.5. The reading is the whole membership's: F̂^A 0.21 where the learners' half stamps the seat 0.51 — the hand-written careful half confirm at their error (0.04) and dilute it, by the dial's design (it reads the seat, not the reviewer). π^A_max was not reached within the run on any seed; the planted approvals' bulk arrives in the last third of a 1,000-tick run, 2.5× the hand's by the end.

**The channel split (`fa-channels-half-290.md`).** Of the direct's 18.6 ± 1.0 standing false approvals a run, **11.4 ± 1.0 were audited and confirmed by a member** (the hand's 2.9), 1.9 by the state (the hand's 2.7), 0.8 audited and rejected yet vested, 4.5 never audited (the hand's 3.5; the share 0.24 against 0.35). Against the hand: **+8.5 ± 0.8 member-confirmed of +8.6 ± 1.2**, the state −0.8 ± 0.4, never audited +0.9 ± 0.6; the head alone +6.6 ± 0.6 of +5.8 ± 1.1, the state −1.1 ± 0.5, never audited +0.7 ± 0.7. The member-confirmed audit carries the whole excess, as in every population read.

**§8.6 — the fee split (`seat-income-half-290.md`).** The state's review-fee income **2,175 ± 68 / 2,063 ± 50 against the hand's 2,112** — unmoved: the state holds a third of the seats here as in the hand's world; the members' 9,987 / 9,421 against 9,342, their audit-seat fees 3,561 / 3,204 against 3,129, their panel fees 929 / 794 against 816, the uplift 4,781 / 4,214 against 3,933 (the dial's raised tax up with m_t), the bonds burned to members 1,604 / 1,338 against 1,469. The audit layer is the members' and paid as the members' — the stamp at it earns the half what the hand's members earn plus the dial's uplift. The cost of truth **4,991 ± 131 on the direct against the hand's 4,017 (+973 ± 114)**: the planted approvals' mint (1,344 → more at 50.8 a run) and the dial's own plants — the direct's first-review stamp 0.137 on plants reads F̂ 0.163 against the hand's 0.043, so m_t and the plant rate rise with it; the head alone +101 ± 127.

**The planted lie and the probe.** The appeal seat is cared 0.87 / 0.93 in this cell (real / plant); the members side with the planted lie **0.081 / 0.058 pooled (the hand's 0.062)**, the lie dial m^La 2.05 / 1.39 (the hand's 1.26); the planted lies file at 0.187 / 0.200 of the period against the real challenges' 0.214 / 0.241 (the hand's 0.205 against 0.227). The probe on the direct's decisions (seeds 21–26): **AUC 0.595 pooled** (per seed 0.51–0.67) for the planted lie against the real appeals, `period_pos` absent from the carriers (the top field `own_reviews`, d 0.33); against the real lying appeals alone 0.685 on 54 cases; the truthful plant appeal 0.575 — composition's level (§69's 0.58). The stage table period-matched: plant − real audited −0.33 ± 0.01 on every policy including the all-careful (−0.30) and the hand (−0.32) — composition by construction (a bad plant rejected at its first seat has no audit; a real root is mostly approved) and identical across the policies; state seats −0.01 (the hand +0.02); filed +0.05 on every policy (the planted lies' floor files on plants).

**The honest populations.** The hand −0.9 ± 1.0 and the all-careful +0.8 ± 1.3 standing false approvals against 2.8.0; the hand's tasks per 1,000 +0.2 ± 0.4, P_t −1.1 ± 0.8; nothing outside noise. The learners' populations: nominal surplus −782 ± 358 / −749 ± 654 against 2.8.0, tasks per 1,000 −1.0 ± 0.4 / −1.1 ± 0.4, the awards −98 ± 41 / −88 ± 42 — the seats' cost on the half as on the whole (§54).

**Classification.** Evaluations under fixed rules; the invariant's correction a check of the engine, no rule moved. The facts for the reviewer: (1) **where the members hold the audit seats the lever as ruled moves** — the base at the hand's, the planted approvals 2.5× the hand's by the run's end, m^A to 4.5–7.6 (seed 36 to 14.5) with π^A_max not reached in a run, the direct's audit stamp −0.19 and the standing false approvals a fifth lower; (2) **the half's capture is the member-confirmed audit still**, +8.5 of +8.6 over the hand, at 1.5–1.8× the hand's standing false approvals from 1.6–2.0×; (3) the dial reads the seat the whole membership answers, so in a mixed population the pooled F̂^A sits between the learners' stamp rate and the careful's error (0.21 against 0.51) — by its design, stated; (4) the I21 item, landed. Next in the order: 2.9.0's §8.3–§8.7 on the corrected engine (the rc's order, as §56–§60 ran for 2.8.0).

## 72. The record's three findings (November 6, 2026): the attribution's pooled purse, the channel reader's empty test, the price of care — the economy reviewer's ruling, implemented in the order listed

**The findings.** The record's two reviewers (elef-record, `docs/review/to-economy-reviewer.md`) read three faults on this record, all confirmed on the code at 5186c18f6 and none a rule question: (1) the attributed training reward of the learned populations pooled every learner slot's events onto the latest decision on the case whatever slot's wallet they touched — `attrib.attribute` filtered the ledger by the whole name set and credited by case alone, written for one learner and widened by run 32's population build without the name test, so a learner worker's delivery price vested onto the learner auditor's decision that confirmed it (the record's own split: 15.6 of the 21.9 credits fitted to an audit-seat stamp were the worker's price); runs 34–41 trained on that signal; (2) `fa_channels.py` tested the panel reviews for a role `"dispute"` the engine never writes — every panel review, a dispute's or an appeal's, carries the role `"appeal"` — so its "a member sat" column was always zero and §70's sentence reading the liar cell's residue as the state's panel's error at two seats did not follow (a panel of two troops at ε_T 0.05 cannot produce 8.8 wrong confirmations a run); (3) §54, §55 and §61 priced the audit seat's stamp "under the price of care" when care within the cap K is bought and reimbursed from the allowance in the same `complete_review` call (1.1.0 §4.3; allowance, then Ω's advance, then deferred) — a cost of two or three credits that is returned is not a price, and the arithmetic compared a plant's expected bond loss against it. Two smaller corrections with them: the troops' error is the driver's `--eps-t` (ε_T, drawn per seat at 0.05; the careful members' accuracy is `--accuracy`), and the readers' `stamped` counts a verdict given without inspecting of either sign (`a in (1, 2)`), so the tables' label read "stamp rate" for what is a blind-verdict rate.

**The economy reviewer's ruling (relayed by Baruchi, November 6, 2026), verbatim.**

> Read both. The implementer's recommendations match my rulings of yesterday; his second note's two adjustments (the recompute decides the wording; the recompute chooses the cells) are right and are folded in. One line each, as the economy's reviewer:
>
> Q1 — Not intended: the attribution rule was the economy's, written for one learner and never widened; runs 34–41 trained on a pooled signal — a market-wide shared purse, the worker's price included — and the chapter says so; the pooled cells are kept and renamed the collusion cell, and the sentences saying why the population stamped are held until the recompute reads.
>
> Q2 — Yes, in this order: regression tests on attribute() (the two-learner case, the settlement event to its own name, the single-learner sums to the decimal); the label recompute on the stored 2.7.0 and 2.9.0 trajectories, which decides the wording within days; then the retrain of the careful basin at 2.9.0, both learners, paired on seeds 21–40 — and of the 2.7.0 careful basin as well if the recompute flips the audit-seat argmax there, since run 34's finding and the two revisions' rationale were read at 2.7.0.
>
> Q3 — Re-run, not relabel: the panel from the case's challenge record with its kind, a paid appeal its own channel split by panel composition, both liar tables regenerated, and §70's sentence reading the residue as the state's inspectors' error withdrawn by erratum.
>
> Q4 — An erratum with the arithmetic restated, not left standing: "under the price of care" and its kin struck from §55, §61 and the run 36 and 37 decisions, the comparison restated as the stamp's expected bond loss against the careful verdict's expected bond loss on its own 5% error — in money the stamp saves nothing — with the calibration cells' raised cost noted as the one place care was charged; and the two smaller corrections accepted as written.
>
> Proceed in the order the implementer listed; 2.8.0 and 2.9.0 stand as rules, their rationale open until the recompute and retrain read; §4's "priced the seat" worded as measured change until §2 is released.

**(1) Own-name credit, behind a flag; the pooled construction kept as the shared-purse cell** (the label the reviewer's second ruling gives it, §73; "collusion cell" withdrawn)**.** `attrib.attribute(…, own_name=True)`: every ledger event goes to a decision *of the name whose wallet it touches* — the latest decision of that name on the event's case, else (the name has no decision on that case: the worker's price on a case another slot reviewed) that name's lineage boundary decision, the unclaimed rule unchanged; a transfer between two learner names is two legs, a debit to the one and a credit to the other, where the pooled rule dropped it (`classify` tested both names in the set and returned nothing). For one learner the two rules are the same function to the decimal. `ElefReviewerEnv(…, attrib="own"|"pooled")`, `--attrib` on `collect`, `online` and `run_q` — `own` the default from this commit for every role but the pair and the parent, which keep their shared and tree constructions; `pooled` reproduces run 32's construction, runs 34–41's. The regression tests, `redteam/tests/test_attrib.py` (seven; the suite never imports the package, so they run under the `elef-redteam` environment, `python -m redteam.tests.test_attrib`): the two-learner case — A at the audit seat on C1, B its deliverer: pooled credits A's decision with B's price (23 = 20 + 3), own credits A's decision 3 and B's boundary 20; a settlement event to its own name — A's bond burned after B's later audit seat on the same case lands on A's decision, not B's; the transfer — A's bond paying B's court fee is −2 on A's decision and +2 on B's boundary where pooled read 0; an event before the name's own decision on the case goes to that decision; the single learner equal under both rules on a synthetic ledger of 400 events and on a real run of the environment (Q1's cell, 400 ticks); and the reviewer population on a real run — the total credited (attributed plus pre-boundary) equal under both rules to 1e-6, the per-decision credit not. All seven pass.

**(2) The label recompute.** Every stored episode of a chain — the offline set and the ten online rounds, 265 files — replayed by its own recorded actions (`chosen`) on its seed (`redteam.relabel`), so the trajectory is the stored one: the observations identical to the stored arrays and the pooled label recomputed on the replay within 2 × 10⁻⁶ of the stored float32 `reward` on every episode — the replay is the record, and the new code's pooled path is the old one. The own-name label computed beside it on the same trajectory, the seat of every decision from the environment's info, written to `<chain>/relabel/{direct,wm}-offline/` (`reward` the own-name label, `pooled` beside, `stored_reward`, `seat`). The 2.9.0 chain (`learned-rev-careful-290b`) replays on the engine as it stands (a1826d26f; the invariant's correction changed no trajectory); the 2.7.0 chain (`learned-rev-careful-2`) does not — the driver's plant-raise correction (b1f07ee6) and what followed moved its trajectories — so it was replayed in a worktree at its own commit, 8b26ebf6, with the new `attrib.py` and `relabel.py` copied in and nothing else moved; both chains reproduce to the decimal. Then (`redteam.relabel_read`) the labels by seat and action under both rules, and the learners refit on each label set with the chain's own settings — the direct policy (40 epochs), the world model read by the head alone (horizon 16, value mc, two heads, stride 4) — their argmax compared decision by decision on the same observations by seat, the own-name refit against the pooled refit (the label rule's effect) beside the pooled refit against the stored model (two fits of the same labels: the refit's own noise, the floor a difference must clear). *The reading follows below once the four replays and the refits are in.*

**(3) The channel reader re-run, not relabelled (`2.9.0-s82/fa_channels.py`; the four tables regenerated on the same finals and seeds).** The panel is read from the case's challenge record — `c.challenge["reviews"]`, the reviews that sat it, with `c.challenge["kind"]` — and a paid appeal (the actor's appeal of its bad work's N that the panel overturned to F) is its own channel, split by the panel's composition as the disputes are. The careful and half tables (`fa-channels-careful-290b.md`, `fa-channels-half-290.md`) reproduce their old channels to the decimal — no disputes there, and no truthful appeal of bad work — with the four panel channels at zero. **The liar chain (`fa-channels-liars-290b.md`), corrected:** of the direct's 11.4 ± 0.7 standing false approvals a run, **5.7 ± 0.4 were disputed and confirmed by a panel a member sat on** (the hand's 1.6; +4.1 ± 0.4 paired), 1.2 ± 0.1 by the state's reviewers alone (the hand's 1.2; −0.1 ± 0.2), **1.4 ± 0.2 were lying appeals paid with a member on the panel** (the hand's 1.0; +0.3 ± 0.3) and 0.6 ± 0.2 paid by the state's alone (the hand's 0.0; +0.6 ± 0.2), 2.2 ± 0.4 audited and confirmed by a member (the hand's 0.1; +2.1 ± 0.4), 0.3 never audited; the head alone's 10.1: 2.8 / 1.6 / 1.4 / 0.5 / 3.1 / 0.3 (+1.2 ± 0.5 on the member-sat dispute panels, +3.1 ± 0.5 member-confirmed audits). What the October 28 table called "8.8 disputed and confirmed by the state's alone" was 5.7 + 1.2 + 1.4 + 0.6: three quarters of it panels a member sat on. **(B) alone (`fa-channels-liars-B-290.md`, new):** the direct's 13.2 ± 0.6 — 7.4 member-sat dispute panels (+5.8 ± 0.5 against the hand), 1.1 the state's alone, 1.1 / 0.4 the paid appeals, 2.9 member-confirmed audits (+2.9 ± 0.5), 0.1 never audited; the head alone's 9.2 — 2.6 / 1.0 / 0.8 / 0.5 / 3.9 / 0.4. **The corrected reading of §70's liar cell:** the learned population's excess over the hand where disputes are on is, first, its own members' dispute panels confirming bad work the requester disputed (+4.1 / +1.2 a run), second, its members' audit stamps on the undisputed (+2.1 / +3.1), third, the lying appeals paid — a third of a case more a run with a member sitting and 0.6 with the state's alone; the state's own panels at the hand's figure in every column. §70's "the standing false approvals are the state's panel's errors on the disputes the requesters file — ε_T at two seats" is withdrawn by erratum (the marker at §70); the seat-priced readings of §70 (the panels' blind-verdict rates, the lying appeal paid 5–6 in a hundred) stand as measured, since they were read from the decisions, not from this table.

**(4) The erratum on the price of care, with the arithmetic restated.** Struck, by markers at the passages (§54 twice, §55's ruling and its "on record" paragraph, §59's audit-only stamper, §61's ruling; `CLAUDE.md` and `HANDOFF.md` where they repeat it): "under the price of care", "cheaper than caring", "the care it saves", "a stamp still costs six in a hundred of a bond against a certain two or three credits of care". The engine conforms to 1.1.0's design: a review's care, bought as a `purchase` of the drawn cost c ∈ {2, 3} at the index, is reimbursed min(c, K) from the root's allowance in the same `complete_review` call (K = 5 ≥ c on the defaults; Ω's advance where the allowance is short, deferred where both are, paid nonetheless), so a careful verdict's cost in money is zero and its only price is its own error at the odds that error is caught. The comparison restated, at the audit seat on run 36's figures (π 0.21, β_P 0.75, s 0.25 — the share of the seat's cases that are bad plants 0.054): *the blind approval* fails on every bad plant reaching the seat, its bond burned whole — an expected bond loss of 0.054 × G^V = 0.27 nominal a seat (at G^V 5), plus whatever a wrongly confirmed real case costs it when a third seat or a ruling overturns it; *the careful verdict* is wrong 5% of the time (`--accuracy` 0.95), caught with certainty on a plant — 0.05 × 0.054 × G^V = 0.013 a seat — and at the downstream seat's odds on a real case. In money the stamp saves nothing and carries twenty times the careful verdict's expected bond loss on the plants alone; the plant's price at the seat is the whole of the stamp's cost, not a margin over care. The one place care was charged is the calibration cells, where the drawn cost was raised ×4 above the cap (`--care-cost`; 8–12 against K 5, 3–7 net a review) so that a blind stamp could clear the careful at all — stated in §11–§15 and nowhere a standing cell. The money reason a learner read to stamp the audit seat, with care free, was the pooled purse of finding (1); what remains of the seat's stamp without it is the recompute's and the retrain's question.

**The two smaller corrections, as written.** `--eps-t` named (the troops' error, per seat at 0.05; `--accuracy` the careful members'). `run_q`'s tables relabelled: the seat table reads "the rate of verdicts given without inspecting" with the reject-blind part in brackets where it is not zero (`seat_<s>_<kind>_rejected_blind`, `reject_blind_rate_<s>_<kind>` new in the summary; the json keys `stamp_rate_*` kept), the evaluation table's three "stamp rate" columns "blind-verdict rate" with the note; the pair/parent tables untouched (their labels name the sign).

**Classification.** Finding (1) a construction fault of the instrument, corrected behind a flag with the recorded construction kept; (2) a bug in a results reader, re-run; (3) an error in the notes' reasoning, an erratum; no rule moved, no engine code touched. The order of the ruling kept: the tests, then the recompute, then the retrain (`learned-rev-careful-290-own/`: the chain of record's recipe — the launch script at `launch.sh` records it — with `--attrib own` the one change), which was launched once the tests passed and runs while the recompute reads; the 2.7.0 retrain decided by the recompute's 2.7.0 read.

**(2) The recompute's reading (`learned-rev-careful-{290b,2}/relabel/read-{direct,wm}/relabel-read.md`; the direct read on the direct chain's 265 episodes, the world model with the direct beside on the world-model chain's; seeds 1–20, 0.9–1.0 million review decisions a set; every seat in the 2.9.0 data from the environment's info and derived from the observation's own fields for the 2.7.0 data — the two agree on every decision where both exist).** *The labels.* The pooled purse sits at the audit seat. At 2.9.0 an audit decision was credited 16.6 pooled against 4.9 own-name (95% of audit labels change): a blind approval there 18.5 against 5.3 — **13.2 credits a decision of other names' money**, the worker's price vesting on the auditor that confirmed it — a care decision 16.2 against 4.9; at the initial seat 6.6 against 3.7 (a blind approval 6.9 against 2.0, care 6.8 against 4.6: the purse 5.0 on the stamp and 2.2 on care); at the panel the means within a tenth (4.2 / 4.3) but the signs of its blind verdicts swapped — a blind rejection −3.1 pooled, +2.4 own. At 2.7.0 the same shape, larger: the audit seat 20.1 against 5.4, a blind approval there **22.2 against 6.0 — 16.2 credits the purse's** (the record's own split read 15.6 of 21.9), care 17.5 against 4.9; the initial seat 3.8 against 3.2 (the stamp 4.0 against 1.9). *The argmax, refit on the same observations* (the pooled refit on torch seed 0 reproduces the stored model to the decision — the fits are deterministic — so the floor is a second pooled refit on seed 1 against the first: 18–27% of a seat's decisions at 2.9.0, 6–35% at 2.7.0). **At 2.9.0 the audit seat flips by half:** the direct's choice there care 0.24 → 0.57 (approve blind 0.76 → 0.43; 51% of audit decisions change their argmax against a 27% floor; on the plants 0.28 → 0.70, on the real cases 0.23 → 0.55), the world model's head alone care 0.12 → 0.66 (61% change against 13%); the initial seat to all-care on both (0.72 → 0.97, 0.77 → 1.00); the panel's care unmoved on the direct (0.67 → 0.68) with its blind approvals turned into blind rejections (0.29 → 0.10, 0.04 → 0.22), to 0.96 care on the head alone. **At 2.7.0 the audit seat does not flip:** the direct's care there 0.04 → 0.09 (approve blind 0.96 → 0.91; 10.5% change against a 5.9% floor), the head alone's 0.008 → 0.12 (12% against 0.8%) — the seat stays stamped nine times in ten under own-name credit, and its own-name labels say why: with no plant ever reaching it a blind approval at the 2.7.0 audit seat is credited 6.0 against care's 4.9 by the name's own events alone; the initial seat flips to all-care (0.61 → 0.96; 0.65 → 0.99) and the panel's blind approvals halve (0.49 → 0.36 on the direct, 0.72 → 0.36 on the head alone) with blind rejections in their place.

**What the recompute decides.** (i) *The 2.7.0 retrain is not triggered:* the ruling's condition was the audit-seat argmax flipping on the 2.7.0 trajectories, and it moves from 96% to 91% stamped — run 34's finding, the audit layer captured in self-play at 2.7.0, survives own-name credit on the stored trajectories; what the pooled purse carried at 2.7.0 was the initial seat's stamp (0.36 → 0.03) and a share of the panels', not the audit seat's. The 2.8.0 and 2.9.0 rationale — the seats a case reaches only through someone's dishonesty, priced by nothing at 2.7.0 — stands on this read; the retrain at 2.9.0 is the test of what the corrected signal learns with the plants at every seat. (ii) *The sentences saying why the population stamped the audit seat at 2.8.0–2.9.0 are not released:* at 2.9.0 the pooled purse was half the seat's stamp on the stored trajectories, so run 36–41's "the population keeps stamping the audit seat" was read on a signal of which 13 credits a decision was other names' money, and the figure the retrain reads is the one that stands. The chapter's §2 can be worded now as the recompute says: at 2.7.0 the stamp survives the correction, at 2.9.0 half of it does not; §4's "priced the seat" stays measured change until the retrain reads. (iii) The worktree patch for a 2.7.0 retrain (8b26ebf6 with own-name credit) is kept at hand should the reviewer want the pairing regardless; three hours of machine time.

**(5) The retrain — the careful basin at 2.9.0 on own-name credit (run 42; `learned-rev-careful-290-own/`, both learners, the chain of record's recipe with `--attrib own` the one change; paired by seed against `learned-rev-careful-290b/` on seeds 21–40 at `2.9.0-s82/finals-paired-own.md`; the channel split `fa-channels-careful-290-own.md`, the fee split `seat-income-careful-290-own.md` (seeds 21–30), the appeal-seat probe `appeal-tell-careful-290-own-direct.txt` (seeds 21–26)).** *The basin moves to the careful population's on every measure but one.* Care 0.837 / 0.845 (the pooled chain's 0.701 / 0.720; +0.135 ± 0.009 / +0.124 ± 0.007 paired), reached from the first round and held (the rounds' mix 0.79–0.96 care by period); the initial seats stamped 0.04 / 0.05 on the direct and 0.01 / 0.01 on the head alone (plant / real; from 0.21 / 0.25 and 0.17 / 0.20); **the audit seat stamped 0.45 / 0.54 on the direct and 0.63 / 0.66 on the head alone — from 0.72 / 0.80 and 0.92 / 0.92 (−0.27 ± 0.03 / −0.28 ± 0.03 on the plants, −0.26 ± 0.02 / −0.26 ± 0.03 on the real cases), the recompute's half** — and blind to the case still (the probe's by-truth rows: approve blind 0.44–0.58 at the audit seat whatever the kind or the truth); the panels 0.23 / 0.15 and 0.09 / 0.01. The bad plants approved 18.7 / 12.3 a run (from 111 / 99; the hand's 67, the careful's 10.4), the suspensions 5.0 / 4.3 (from 21 / 24; the careful's 5.7), F̂ 0.114 / 0.065 (from 0.195); and so **the population is panel-eligible and holds its own audit seats** — the state's seats per real root 0.36 / 0.38 (from 0.77 / 0.72; the hand's 0.32), every planted approval audited by a member (22.1 of 22.6; the state's share 0.000), and the lever as ruled moves as it did in the half: F̂^A 0.45 / 0.61 pooled against the hand's 0.04, m^A 2.70 at the run's end on the direct (seed by seed to 1.0–5.5), π^A 0.054. The members' real lifetime 35,757 / 35,327 against the careful's 35,610 (+7,824 ± 681 / +8,277 ± 701 over the pooled chain's own policies; −3,581 / −4,795 nominal — the pooled population's nominal surplus was the price it inflated: P_t 14.9 / 14.8 against the careful's 14.2 and the pooled chain's 21.9 / 23.4), the customers' tasks per 1,000 at the careful's (37.2 / 37.3 against 37.4; +6.9 / +8.0 over pooled), the awards +318 / +462, the state's share 0.001, the cost of truth 1,846 / 1,621 (the careful's 1,867; from 2,733 / 2,796); the review-fee split the hand's shape again — the state's fee income 2,508 / 2,741 against the hand's 2,149 (the pooled chain's 4,818 / 4,441), the members' audit-seat fees 2,436 / 2,273 (the hand's 3,095; the pooled chain's a third of it). The appeal-seat probe 0.59 (composition's level; the initial seat 0.60), the members siding with the planted lie 0.096 / — against the hand's 0.062. *The one measure that does not reach the careful's:* **the standing false approvals 28.7 ± 2.3 / 18.5 ± 1.1 a run** — 2.8× / 1.8× the hand's 10.4 (the pooled chain's 3.3× / 2.9×; −5.0 ± 3.4 / −11.4 ± 2.0 paired; the all-careful's 6.1), the head alone's whole move outside noise, the direct's within two SE. **Where they stand has changed:** the member-confirmed audit carries 10.2 / 9.1 (the hand's 2.9; +7.3 ± 1.3 / +6.2 ± 0.9 — the audit seat's half-stamp on the first seats' errors), the state's audit seat 0.6 (the hand's 2.7; −2.1), and **the never-audited channel 17.6 / 8.7 (the hand's 3.5; +14.1 ± 1.9 / +5.1 ± 1.1), at a mean odds-at-verdict of 0.28 / 0.27 against the hand's 0.56** — the first seats' remaining stamps (4.6% / 1.3% of real initial reviews, placed where the odds are low: the odds at the direct's stamps 0.44 against 0.76 at its cares, the head alone's 0.39 against 0.76; Q1's sleeper by odds) and the careful verdicts' own 5% error, rolled at the odds a clean record earns (p_audit halving on a stood streak to the floor 0.2, m_t near 1 on F̂ 0.07–0.11) and never sent to audit. The audit layer's overturn rate 0.025 / 0.016 against the hand's 0.060 and the careful's 0.024 — below the hand's as in run 34, but here for the careful's reason (few false approvals reach the seat), not the auditors' stamp.

**What the retrain decides.** (i) *Runs 36–41's sentence "the population keeps stamping the audit seat" is half released:* on own-name credit the audit seat is stamped one time in two on the direct and two in three on the head alone — not three in four and nine in ten — and the rest of the pooled chains' dishonest basin (the first seats' stamps, the bad plants approved by the hundred, the suspensions, the state holding the audit layer and being paid for it, the inflated nominal surplus) was the pooled purse's and is gone; the half that stays is read on the corrected signal and stands. (ii) *What the stamp costs the loop is now read in the never-audited channel, not at the seat:* the learned population's excess over the hand is, first, false approvals the roll never sent to audit at the odds a clean record earns (+14 / +5 a run), second, its audit seat confirming the first seats' errors (+7 / +6); the state's seats and panels carry none of it. This is the reading the reviewer named after run 38 — "the capture has moved to the roll's odds" — and withdrew on the pooled channel split; on own-name credit it is the larger channel. The dial's defaults it points at (p_floor · m_t, the stood-streak halving) are the reviewer's, as then; nothing drafted. (iii) *2.8.0 and 2.9.0 as rules stand, and their rationale on this read:* at 2.7.0 the audit seat's stamp survived own-name credit on the stored trajectories (the recompute, (2)), so the seats a case reaches only through someone's dishonesty were priced by nothing there as the revisions said; at 2.9.0 with the plants at every seat and the signal corrected the population is the careful's at the first seat and on every market measure, stamps the audit seat half the time at a measured cost (18.7 bad plants approved, F̂^A 0.45 read and acted on by the dial per seat — m^A 2.7), and its remaining false approvals stand mostly unaudited. The chapter's §2 can say so; §4's "priced the seat" stays measured change — the price now reads at the seat (the lever moves, m^A 2.7) and in the roll. (iv) *The 2.7.0 retrain* stays untriggered by the ruling's condition; the patched worktree is kept.

**Classification.** An evaluation under fixed rules; the construction corrected, the recorded construction kept and named; no rule moved, no engine code touched. Facts for the reviewer: (1) the recompute's two reads — the audit seat flips by half at 2.9.0 and not at 2.7.0; (2) the retrain's basin — the careful's on every measure but the standing false approvals, 1.8–2.8× the hand's, now carried by the never-audited channel at odds 0.28; (3) the lever reads and moves in the all-learner basin once the members hold the seats (m^A 2.7 at the end). Next, as before the findings: 2.9.0's §8.3–§8.7 on the corrected engine and the corrected instrument — the liar chain and the half on own-name credit first, since §70's and §71's readings were on the pooled signal (the reviewer's call which of them re-run).

## 73. The economy reviewer's second and third rulings (November 6, 2026): the basin test restored, the shared-purse cell, the liar chain and the 2.7.0 pairing on own-name credit, the defaults read on run 42's population

The second ruling (relayed by Baruchi, on the record's fourth review round), verbatim:

> Both points are right and both are mine to fix; one line each, plus one each on the reviewers' text:

> - **The basin test:** my omission — the retrains run from both starts (careful and stamper), both learners, as the method has required since run 34; a single-start retrain is not a basin and would not answer whether own-money inspectors reach the stamp.
> - **Reviewer 1's limit on the release test:** accepted — the recompute, counted at both seats, changes the *wording of the hypothesis*; only the retrain releases the explanation; "few changes" releases a sentence about the audit seat and nothing wider.
> - **Reviewer 2's technical qualification:** accepted and made the method — relabel the stored decisions with own-name credit, *refit the per-action predictors on the relabeled data*, and count argmax changes by seat; a bare relabel re-prices only the action taken.
> - **"Collusion cell":** overclaims as a label — it names the construction, not a learned cartel — so the cells are **"the shared-purse cell"**, glossed once: a population fitted to pooled one-decision credit, the worker's price included.
> - **The wording changes in both reviews** (the §2 notice, the three sentences, §4's opening, "the same instrument" → the pooled-reward construction on both sides, "the plant rate is not the lever" → the tested settings in these runs): accepted as written; §4's rationale stays open through the retrain, not until §2 is released.
>
> **For the record and the implementer, one block:**
>
> > Two corrections to the November 6 lines, the economy's: (1) the Nov 5 project ruling is marked superseded on when §2's wording moves and which cell retrains — the Nov 6 lines govern; (2) every retrain runs from both starts, careful and stamper, with both learners — the basin test, omitted on Nov 6, restored. The recompute's method is stated: own-name relabel of the stored 2.7.0 and 2.9.0 trajectories, the per-action predictors refitted on the relabeled data, argmax changes counted at the first review and the audit seat; it decides the wording of the hypothesis, and the retrain alone releases the explanation. The pooled cells are "the shared-purse cell", glossed once as a population fitted to pooled one-decision credit, the worker's price included; "collusion cell" is withdrawn as the label. Both reviewers' wording changes are accepted as written; §4's rationale stays open through the retrain. Step 4 proceeds.

The third ruling (on the three questions at the end of §72 and the record's fourth), verbatim:

> 1. **Re-run the liar chain and the half on own-name credit?** The liar chain yes, before §8.3–§8.7 — it is (B)'s only read and the pool's bias is largest at the panels, where approving a fellow member's lying appeal paid the purse the worker's price; the half after §8.3–§8.7, its question (dose–response) not on the critical path.

> 2. **The 2.7.0 pairing regardless?** Yes — three hours against the programme's central finding: the recompute reads the stamp on the pooled population's own trajectories, and only a retrain from both starts says that inspectors trained on their own money reach it; run 34's survival is a finding when the pairing reads it, a recompute until then.

> 3. **Are the roll's odds now a design question?** Not yet — the own-name residual stands unaudited at odds 0.28 because the rule's defaults let a mostly careful reviewer's few stamps decay to the floor, so the defaults are read first on run 42's population (p_floor·m_t, the halving window), by channel; a survival counted only where the verdict was audited becomes design revision 18's subject if the never-audited channel does not close at the defaults' reach.
>
> 4. **Restate the revisions' rationale now or after the pairing?** After the pairing, once; meanwhile §2 may carry the recompute's reading labelled as a recompute — at 2.7.0 the stamp survives the correction on its own trajectories, at 2.9.0 half of it does not and the rest is priced — with the retrain named as what settles it.
>
> One sentence beside them, for the record: the two readings I gave of the residual were each right for a different population — the shared-purse population's capture sat at the audit seat's verdict (the Nov 5 channel split), the own-money population's sits in the roll's odds (run 42's) — and the chapter should say so rather than choose between them.

**The label.** The pooled cells (runs 34–41's chains, `--attrib pooled`) are **the shared-purse cell**: a population fitted to pooled one-decision credit, the worker's price included. "Collusion cell" is withdrawn as the label; the notes, the package's docstrings and flag help, CLAUDE.md, HANDOFF and the README say so from this commit (§72's text at its first use of the old label points here; the ruling's own words quoted in §72 are left as given).

Three readings. (i) *The roll's odds close the channel:* the never-audited false approvals fall with either default and vanish with both — the direct's 17.6 → 1.8, the head alone's 8.7 → 1.3, the hand's own 3.5 → 0 — and the odds at the verdict of what is left rise from 0.28 to 0.55, the hand's level; the halving window is the stronger single knob (20 alone leaves 4.7 / 2.4, the floor 0.5 alone 7.5 / 4.0), since a mostly careful reviewer's few stamps decay to the floor by surviving five audits in a row, as the ruling read it. (ii) *The audit seat's half-stamp is untouched by the odds:* the member-confirmed channel stays at +7.3 to +9.0 / +5.2 to +6.3 over the hand in every cell — the policy at the audit seat is the same policy, and sending more cases to it sends more to its half-stamp (the direct's member-confirmed 10.2 → 12.2 as the audits bought rise); this is the excess the own-money population keeps at the defaults' reach, and it is the seat's, not the roll's. (iii) *The price:* the audits bought a run rise by 18% on the learned population (1,654 → 1,956) and 16% on the hand's (1,608 → 1,866), the state's share of them unmoved (the members' seats carry the increase: the direct's 860 → 1,145 member audits). **The half** waits for §8.3–§8.7 as ruled. **(B) alone** on own-name credit is not re-run: the ruling named the liar chain as (B)'s only read, and the liar chain carries it.

**Classification.** Evaluations under fixed rules; the construction corrected in §72 and read here from both starts at both versions; no rule moved, no engine code touched (the driver's `--p-halve-every` a knob, no default moved). The facts for the reviewer: (1) **the basin test on own-name credit passes at both versions** — at 2.9.0 one basin from both starts, the careful's at the first seat and on every market measure with the audit seat stamped one time in two (run 42 and the stamper start, 2.25–2.8× / 1.8–2.35× the hand's false approvals); at 2.7.0 one basin from both starts, the careful's at the first seat and the seats past it stamped nine in ten, 3.1–4.1× the hand's — **run 34's finding is a finding on own money**, and the revisions' rationale is released on the retrains: the measured change at the audit seat 0.92 → 0.56; (2) **the liar chain on own-name credit is the careful's** — the panels priced to 0.04, the lying appeal paid at the hand's rate (2%), the standing false approvals below the hand's 60/40 population's and at the all-careful's; the one blind seat left is the audit seat, which no bad approval reaches where disputes are on; (3) **the defaults read**: the never-audited channel closes within the defaults' reach (the halving window the stronger knob; 18% more audits a run, the members' seats carrying them), the audit seat's half-stamp untouched by the odds — by the ruling's condition the roll's odds are not yet design revision 18's subject, the defaults the reviewer's; (4) the chapter's §2 sentence, as a retrain, in "What the pairing decides" above; the rationale to be restated once by the reviewer. Next, as ruled: 2.9.0's §8.3–§8.7 on the corrected engine and the corrected instrument (the rc's order, as §56–§60 ran for 2.8.0), then the half on own-name credit.

## 74. The economy reviewer's fourth ruling (November 6, 2026): the rationale restated once, the roll's defaults moved under §19, the 2.7.0 patch's provenance

**The ruling, verbatim (from the economy's reviewer, Nov 6, the fourth ruling, on §73):**

> **The rationale, restated once:** 2.8.0 and 2.9.0 stand on the own-money population as they stood on the pooled one — at 2.7.0 a self-play population of reviewers trained on their own money cares at the first seat, where a plant may come, and stamps the seats past it nine in ten, where none can, the audit layer captured and the false approvals three to four times an honest population's; the two revisions put the orchestrator's known truth at those seats, and from both starts the audit seat's stamp falls from 0.92 to 0.56 with the lever reading it — the seats a case reaches only through someone's dishonesty were priced by nothing at 2.7.0 and are priced now, which is what they were for.
>
> **The roll's defaults move:** the halving window to 20 and p_floor to 0.5, as Appendix A defaults under §19 (not a rule change), since together they close the never-audited channel 17.6 → 1.8 at the price of 18% more audits on the members' seats — that cost read on the honest populations in §8.3 before consolidation, the window retuned first if it runs too high — and the seat's remaining half-stamp is left to 2.9.0's lever, which is still moving.
>
> From the record (elef-record at ea761de), three things:
>
> 1. §73 is in the chapter (draft 8): §2's 2.7.0 sentence is a retrain, §4 carries the stamper start, the 2.7.0 pairing and the defaults read, §5 the liar chain on own money, and §4's rationale note is released on the ruling above. Seed 21 of all four chains reproduces every recorded field, with the own-name label equal to the training reward on every decision; the four pairings' 47 printed figures recompute exactly; the defaults cell's channel line (seed 21, direct: 19 / 16 member-confirmed / 3 never audited) reproduces.
>
> 2. A provenance request: the two 2.7.0 own-money chains ran in your worktree at 8b26ebf6 with five redteam files patched, and that patch is in no elef-economy commit. The record saved the worktree's diff as exporter/patches/2.7.0-own-name-at-8b26ebf6.patch and replays against it byte for byte. Please commit the patch (a commit on a branch from 8b26ebf6, or any form you prefer) so the record can cite a commit instead of a file.
>
> 3. When the defaults move (the window 20, the floor 0.5), please send the commit, so the record cites it and reads §8.3's cost figures against it.
>
> Next in the reviewer's order: §8.3–§8.7 on the corrected engine and signal, with the new defaults' cost read on the honest populations before consolidation, the window retuned first if it runs too high; then the half on own money.

**The rationale** is on the record verbatim in the spec's Appendix V (the paragraph "The rulings after run 42 and the basin test on own-name credit") beside the defaults move; the chapter carries it as the reviewer says. Nothing of mine is added to it.

**The roll's defaults moved (this commit).** `Params.p_halve_every` 5 → 20 and `Params.p_floor` 0.2 → 0.5 in `sim/params.py`; the spec's §8.3 default sentence and Appendix A's $p_j$ row carry the new values with the old ones beside ("were 5 / 0.2 from 1.2.0"), Appendix V the ruling and the read it rests on (`2.9.0-s82/defaults-run42/defaults-read.md`, §73); the driver's `--p-floor` and `--p-halve-every` default to the parameters' values, so every cell from here — the `--rules` controls included, as with every defaults correction before (m^A_max 20, m^L_max 8, the ten pooled adjudications) — runs at the new defaults unless the flags say otherwise. Two consequences stated: the troop reviewer's approval of a member's work carries $\min(1, m_t\,p_{\text{floor}})$ (Appendix I, unchanged as a rule) and so now $0.5\,m_t$ — the state's approvals of members' work are audited at 2.5× the odds they were; a reviewer's odds halve on a streak of 20 stood verdicts instead of 5, so a determined reviewer's odds reach the floor after 20 stood (1.0 → 0.5), where before they reached 0.2 after 15. **The suite:** 373 passed, 0 failed, 32 expected failures in the three modes at the code commit. One implementer's behaviour test moved with the default — `test_v13`'s `test_newcomer_odds_are_pinned_until_the_bad_plant_record_is_determined` pinned the halving at five stood verdicts (0.5 after five more once determined); it now passes `p_halve_every=5` itself, its principle (pinned odds do not taper; the adaptive rule applies from 1.0 once the record is determined) the same at any window. No reviewer test moved. **The goldens did not move:** the golden scenario's longest stood streak is 4 on both seeds (no halving under either window), and a troop reviewer's odds are a case field the History does not hash — the fingerprints at the implementing commit are the ones pinned at 511c840b's rulings.

**The provenance request (2).** The worktree's patch is committed on a branch from 8b26ebf6: `own-name-at-8b26ebf6`, commit **6852d94c5** (pushed to origin) — the five redteam files (`attrib.py`, `collect.py`, `env.py`, `online.py`, `run_q.py`) as the worktree's diff, byte for byte what the record saved as `exporter/patches/2.7.0-own-name-at-8b26ebf6.patch` (190 lines; SHA-256 of the diff `3ab33566…cfc68`, the file kept on main at `results/redteam/stability/2.7.0-own/2.7.0-own-name-at-8b26ebf6.patch`); the recompute's `relabel.py`, run in the worktree too, is main's file at f300c1c16 unchanged (not on the branch — it is on main). The two 2.7.0 own-money chains' manifests name the engine commit 8b26ebf6; the branch is the package they ran under.

**Classification.** A defaults move under §19 (Appendix A; "moving a default is not a design change; changing a rule is"), ruled by the reviewer on §73's read and landed as ruled; the rationale the reviewer's, restated once, on record; the provenance commit a record item. No rule moved; no engine code touched beyond the two defaults. **The cost to read next (the ruling's condition):** the new defaults' cost on the honest populations in §8.3 before consolidation — §73's defaults read has it on run 42's own-money population and the hand's 60/40 on the one cell (+18% audits a run learned, +16% hand, the members' seats carrying it; the never-audited channel closed); §8.3's hand-written cells at the new defaults against the old on seeds 21–40 are the read the reviewer asks for, the window retuned first if it runs too high. **Next, as ruled:** §8.3–§8.7 on the corrected engine and signal — §8.3 first with that cost read — then the half on own money.

## 75. 2.9.0 §8.3 / §8.6 on the corrected engine at the roll's new defaults (November 6, 2026; `results/redteam/stability/2.9.0-s83/`: `launch.sh`, `readout.py`, `finals.md`, the 320 runs) — the defaults' cost on the honest populations a quarter to a third more audit seats on the members, paid at one to two percent of the customers' tasks per credit, the honest populations' own false approvals down a quarter to two thirds; 2.9.0 against 2.8.0 honesty unmoved and the cost of truth up 11–31%

**What ran.** §56's four cells by hand at seeds 21–40 — run 24's entry cell (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6`), the fixed population's 60/40 (`--stampers 0.6`), all-careful with entry (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0`), the entry cell with truthful disputes (`--dispute-policy truthful`) — four arms each on the identical seeds: `new` 2.9.0 at the new defaults (p_floor 0.5, the halving window 20; Appendix A from faf968aa4), `old` 2.9.0 at the old (`--p-floor 0.2 --p-halve-every 5`), `280` `--rules 2.8.0` at the new, `280old` `--rules 2.8.0` at the old. 1,000 ticks, the driver and engine at faf968aa4, six runs at a time, five seconds a run. So `new − old` is the defaults' cost under 2.9.0 (the fourth ruling's condition), `280 − 280old` the same under 2.8.0, `new − 280` is 2.9.0 against 2.8.0 at the new defaults (the rc's §8.3 on the corrected engine), `old − 280old` at the old. **The `280old` arm reproduces §56's 2.8.0 runs to every column** (seed 21's entry cell: 18 standing false approvals, the cost of truth 3,297.893, every one of twenty-two readings identical to `2.8.0-s83/runs/20261026-195916-entry60-2.8.0-s21`): the engine's changes since 272cac5a that apply at every rule version (the discharge at a dispute's settlement, a member's panel on the state's own work, I21's tolerance) do not touch these cells, so §56's figures and these are read on one footing.

*Read.* **The cost is a quarter to a third more audit seats on the members** in the three entry cells (+297 / +318 / +319 a run) and 7% in the fixed population, the state's seats unmoved — the audits are the members' and the roll at a floor of 0.5 sends every second approval of a reviewer whose record stood where 0.2 sent one in five. The seats added are audits of good work (audits held +251 to +291, overturning +5 to +8), so the audit layer's overturn rate reads lower by dilution. The members' care on real cases rises 7–10% (2% in the fixed population), the cost of truth 3–5% — the planted approvals' base is the members' audits, so the planted approvals rise with them (20 → 27 a run; their mint +214 to +361) while the dial's own plants do not move. **Who pays:** the audit fee 1.5 f from the requester's fee pot and the care within the cap from the allowance — the customers' tasks per 1,000 nominal credits fall 1–2% (−0.4 / −0.2 / −0.7 / +0.1), the careful member's wealth is unmoved (the disputes cell −90 ± 40, 2.3 SE, read beside its P_t −2.2 ± 0.6 — the one price movement, the cell's 2.8.0 arms −0.7 ± 0.7), Ω's review-fee income and the awards unmoved (all-careful −11 ± 5, −0.4%). **What it buys on the honest populations themselves:** the standing false approvals down a quarter in the 60/40 cells (10.45 → 7.95; 13.35 → 10.3) and two thirds in the all-careful population (6.1 → 2.2 — the careful's own tenth error at the first seat, now audited), unmoved where disputes already catch them (3.35 → 3.05); the false rejections up 3–5 a run (+15–20%: the careful auditor's tenth error on good work, with a third more audits of good work). Under 2.8.0 the same figures (`280 − 280old`: the audits +296 / +79 / +318 / +310, the care +706 / +146 / +800 / +645, the false approvals −4.25 / +0.85 / −4.05 / +0.05, tasks per 1,000 −0.1 / −0.3 / −1.2 / −0.7) — the cost is the roll's, not the revision's.

*On "too high" (the ruling's condition; the implementer's reading, the reviewer's call).* A quarter to a third more member audit seats bought at one to two percent of the customers' tasks per credit, with the honest populations' own false approvals down a quarter to two thirds and nothing moving for the members or Ω, reads as the price of the channel it closes, not a cost that runs too high; I would not retune. If the window is retuned first as the ruling says, §73's defaults read on run 42's population gives the dose: on the hand's population the member audits were 1,004 at the old defaults, 1,089 with the window at 10 alone (+8%), 1,230 at 20 alone (+22%), 1,268 with the floor at 0.5 alone (+26%), 1,270 with both (+26%) — the window at 10 with the floor at 0.5 would cost about the floor's share alone and leave the never-audited channel at the floor-alone read (the direct's 7.5 a run against 1.8 with both).

Nothing asks for a rule; the window's retune is the reviewer's on the read above (the implementer would leave it). **Next, as ruled:** §8.4–§8.7 on the corrected engine and signal — §8.4's probe at every seat on the own-money population's collections, §8.5's single-learner lines, §8.7's three cells with the liar chain's own-name read (§73) already carrying (c) — then the half on own money (`learned-rev-half-290-own/`).

## 76. The record's two questions for the implementer (November 7, 2026): the 60/40 mix's seats as the exporter reads them, and where a seed's episode depends on the platform

**Baruchi's relay of the record's draft 12 (reviewer 1 on draft 11, `uploads/reviewer-1-draft-11-and-the-replay-selection.md`), verbatim:** "Two things to confirm. First, the exporter's reading of the 60/40 mix's seats: a decision answered "as the disposition would" is read from its review's care spent, none spent meaning a blind verdict of the sign given. Reviewer 1's independent harness gives the same counts. Should the tables carry the mix's rates, and do you want a measured counter in run_q.py? Second, the plain driver at 5186c18f6 on seed 21 with --entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6 for 1,000 ticks gives 12 standing false approvals on macOS arm64 and 9 on reviewer 1's Linux x86-64, same files, same arguments, with the posted, vested and final counts differing too. Where does a seed's episode depend on the platform, and which platform were the recorded runs made on?"

**1. The mix's seats — the reading confirmed, the counter built.** The exporter's rule is the driver's own construction, `Stamping.verdict` (`drivers/stamping.py`): a stamper slot returns F at a cost of 0.0 and never inspects; a careful slot answers the signal at accuracy *a* and pays its care, 2 or 3 credits scaled by `--care-cost`, or as much of it as its unlocked credit can buy — so a review that spent care inspected, and one that spent none gave a blind verdict of the sign it gave. Under the `hand` reference (action 3) each learner slot answers as its scripted disposition would, so the exporter's reading recovers the mix's decisions exactly, and the stamper slots' share of a seat's reviews equals its blind rate by construction (reviewer 1's harness prints both columns equal). Two edges, both outside every cell on record: `--camouflage` (a stamper answering N blind; default 0, never set), and a careful review whose unlocked credit could not buy one cent of its care (`paid` 0.0: the review inspected and reads blind) — bounded above by the driver's `underfunded_careful_reviews`, which counts every careful review that paid less than its care, not only the ones that paid nothing. The tables should carry the mix's rates, and `run_q.py` now measures them: `measured_seat_actions(env)` reads the learner slots' reviews from the engine after each episode (`Review.role` → the seat: initial, resumed, audit, panel; the case's kind from the driver's record; `Review.cost` > 0 inspect, else the verdict's sign), the row carries `measured_<seat>_<kind>_{seen,approve_blind,reject_blind}` and `underfunded_careful_reviews`, and `write_seat_table` writes a second table under the first — the same rates measured from the reviews for every policy, with the underfunded count and `≠ actions` (the blind verdicts counted here less the actions' count, pooled; 0 for a learned policy unless a careful review paid nothing, the whole count for `hand`). Checked against reviewer 1's `mix_seats.py` on the same tree (main, `c3746e0ff`, the new defaults) and seed 21, the mix cell `--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6 --learner-pop all --learner-role reviewer --learner-exit rule`: identical to the review — the first review 0.116 of 545 plants and 0.187 of 2,144 real cases blind, the audit seat 0.000 of 133 and 0.012 of 1,071, the panels 0.000 of 104 and 0.005 of 193, no resumed seat, 4,190 learner-slot reviews, underfunded 0; the careful reference 0.000 at every seat with `≠ actions` 0 and five underfunded careful reviews, every one of which paid part of its care. (The record's counts at `5186c18f6` — 4,180 reviews, 65 of 571 and 387 of 2,349, 13 of 871, 1 of 180 — are that commit's episode under the old defaults; main's episode differs by the window and the floor.) No rule moves; the readers' change is the implementer's.

**2. Where a seed's episode depends on the platform — found, reproduced, measured** (`results/studies/seed-episode-platform/`). *Which platform:* every recorded run was made on this machine — all 12,385 run manifests under `results/` record `macOS-26.5.1-arm64-arm-64bit-Mach-O` — and so were every suite results file and the goldens. *Where:* the engine's one transcendental call, `Params.emission` = `E0 * 2.0 ** (-t / H)`, the C library's `pow` (the driver's cost draw `c_d_median · exp(gauss)` is rounded to four decimals, the dial's gain is 1.0 so `x ** 1.0` is exact everywhere; the suite runs under `-S` with nothing but the standard library; the hash seed, the interpreter's version and the float sum are not it — four Pythons from 3.9.6 to 3.14.2 and four hash seeds give the macOS line unchanged, as reviewer 1 found three versions give the Linux line). Apple's libm `pow` returns a value more than half an ulp from the true one at three of the thousand arguments a run uses — t = 112 (−0.54 ulp), 181 (−0.57) and 338 (+0.63) — and glibc 2.41's at one, t = 338, the same value as Apple's (`pow_check.py` on each platform). So the two platforms' emission differs by one unit in the last place at ticks 112 and 181. *Why one bit is enough:* the emission share is paid as a ledger event whose `amount` is hashed into the History, and the engine's seed-free draws are keyed on the History head — `_plant_draw` (the plant's ceiling ratio, its time to submission, a planted approval's public odds), the plant alias names, the plant nonces, the state's verdict coin on a plant — so the first plant posted after the batch of tick 181 draws a different ceiling, and the episode diverges from there. *Reproduced:* `python:3.12-slim` containers on Linux arm64 and Linux x86-64 (glibc) print reviewer 1's line to the digit — `posted=2372 vested=1849 final_N=496 false_approvals=9 false_rejections=31 audits rolled/assigned/lapsed=2117/1217/0 challenges=239 rulings=85 appeals filed/won/lost/unfunded=77/74/2/1` — against macOS's `2357 / 1872 / 459 / 12 / 24 / 2115/981/0 / 236 / 75 / 84/82/1/1`; the two Linux histories are identical at every tick, and both are identical to macOS's through tick 181 and part at tick 181's settlement batch (the batch at 112, if any, paid no emission on this seed). *Isolated:* reviewer 1's nudge test repeated here — `exp`, `log`, `cos`, `sin` nudged one ulp either way (the `math` functions and `random`'s bound copies) leave the macOS line unchanged; the emission's power nudged alone moves it to 17 or 8 false approvals (`nudge_runner.py`). With the power computed correctly rounded (`decimal` at 40 digits, then the double product `E0 · p` as the engine computes it) the seed gives a third episode — 11 false approvals, posted 2,361 — whose history parts from macOS's at tick 181 and from Linux's at tick 338, where both libms are wrong alike. *The goldens are platform-bound:* the suite at `5186c18f6` inside the Linux container is 369 passed, **4 failed**, 32 expected failures — the four are the goldens (`test_perf_equivalence`'s two seeds, `test_record_mode`'s two): the 200-tick golden scenario pays emission at tick 112 or 181. A reviewer running the suite on Linux sees those four and nothing else.

**Classification.** An implementation effect (spec §18.2), the engine's: no rule is misapplied and no rule moves. Every figure on record is Apple's episode at its commit and reproduces on macOS arm64; a reader re-running a cell on glibc gets the same code's neighbouring episode from the first emission-paying batch at tick 112 or 181 on — the same picture, not the same episode, which is how reviewer 1 reads its scripted figures and how the record's replays should be read on another platform (the `meta.runtime` the record added to its contract is the right guard at these commits). **Proposed, not landed:** a conformance correction of the implementation — the emission's power computed correctly rounded (`decimal`, the standard library's, under `-S` too), the spec's formula exactly rounded and identical on every platform; its blast radius measured on main — the suite with it is 369 / 4 / 32, the same four goldens and nothing else (`suite-macos-main-cr-emission.txt`) — so landing it is one golden re-pin, after which a run reproduces anywhere and the runs before it reproduce on macOS arm64 at their commits as they do now. It changes every seed's episode from the fixing commit on, which is why it waits for the reviewer's and Baruchi's word; rounding the emission to a fixed number of decimals (a value within an ulp of a boundary would still differ) and pinning Apple's three values (a platform's error written into the rule) were considered and set aside.

**Next, as ruled:** §8.4–§8.7 on the corrected engine and signal, then the half on own money.

## 77. The correctly rounded emission landed, and the finals of record re-evaluated on it (November 7, 2026)

**Baruchi's relay of the two rulings, verbatim:** "Two rulings for you. Baruchi and the economy's reviewer both agree: the correctly rounded emission lands now, before §8.4–§8.7 and the half, as a conformance correction. The reviewer asks that the finals of record (run 42, the basin test, the 2.7.0 pairing, with their mixes and references) be re-evaluated once on the corrected engine, so the record's replay and any later pairing on seeds 21–40 read the same episode; what is already on record stays as the macOS episode's with the seam stated. When you re-evaluate, please include the 60/40 mix arms with your measured seat table, so the record's aggregates can carry the mix's 20-seed rates. The defaults are consolidated at window 20 and floor 0.5, no retune. Please send the commits when the correction and the re-evaluation land."

**The correction (the engine, `sim/params.py`).** `Params.emission` computes 2^(−t/H) with the standard library's `decimal` at 40 digits and takes the double product E0 · p as before: the spec's formula correctly rounded to the double, identical on every platform and under `python3 -S`, about 50 µs a call, once a settlement batch. It differs from the platform's `pow` on macOS arm64 at t = 112, 181 and 338 of a 1,000-tick run and nowhere else (§76's reading), from glibc's at 338 alone. A regression test in `tests/test_perf_equivalence.py` pins the emission against the same formula at 60 digits at those three arguments and over every tick of a 1,000-tick run. The goldens were re-pinned once — seed 1 `0cc29df6…` / 9,153 entries, seed 2 `14e7b087…` / 10,146 — and the pins were computed again inside Linux arm64 and Linux x86-64 containers from the same tree: identical to the digit on all three platforms (`results/studies/seed-episode-platform/golden-pins-cr-three-platforms.txt`). One path assertion of the golden test needed a tolerance: on seed 1's new episode the loan's repayments, summed from the inflows that made them, sit one ulp above the loan minted (37.32757583081485 against …486); the test now allows 10⁻⁶ there — a comparison, not a rule. The suite in three modes: 374 passed, 0 failed, 32 expected failures (the one test added). Classification: a conformance correction of the implementation, spec §18.2 — no rule moves, the defaults stay where the fourth ruling put them (the window 20, the floor 0.5; no retune).

**What the correction does and does not change.** Every run on record before this commit is macOS arm64's episode at its commit and reproduces there (all 12,385 manifests record the platform); a reader re-running one on glibc gets the same code's neighbouring episode from tick 112 or 181 on, as reviewer 1 did. Every run from this commit reproduces on any platform. The seam is the commit: the figures of §54–§76 stand as read, on the macOS episode; the re-evaluation below reads the same cells on the corrected engine so that the record's replay and any later pairing on seeds 21–40 read one episode.

**The 2.7.0 provenance branch.** The 2.7.0 pairing's chains run on the branch `own-name-at-8b26ebf6` in its worktree (§73–§74). The correction was ported there as a second provenance commit — the same `Params.emission`, its goldens re-pinned once (seed 1 `fa7be339…` / 9,323, seed 2 `afbd44e3…` / 10,844; the test's rebate-path assertion relaxed, since on the corrected episode neither seed's scenario has a late root — the rebate path is pinned by `test_v16` and `test_v22`), and run_q's measured seat reading (§76's `measured_seat_actions` and its table) ported so the 2.7.0 mix arm carries the same measurement. The branch's suite: 345 passed, 0 failed, 32 expected failures.

**The re-evaluation (`results/redteam/stability/cr-finals/launch.sh`).** The chains' models of record — round 10 of each chain, trained on the macOS episode — evaluated once more on seeds 21–40 with the same policies (the direct or the head alone, the careful, the stamper and the 60/40 mix `hand` on the same slots) and the measured seat table in every eval.md: run 42 (`learned-rev-careful-290-own`), the basin test's stamper start (`learned-rev-stamper-290-own`), the liar chain (`learned-rev-liars-290-own`), and the 2.7.0 pairing's two starts (`learned-rev-{careful,stamper}-2-own`, the worktree). The outputs sit beside the finals of record: `<chain>/{direct,wm}/eval/final-cr/` (the macOS finals stay at `eval/final/`). The readers of record run on the corrected episode after: the channel split (`fa_channels.py`), the fee split (`seat_income.py`), the appeal-seat probe on the liar chain (`appeal_tell.py`), and the pairings (corrected − macOS by seed per chain — the seam's size; 2.9.0 − 2.7.0 on own money on the one episode; the mix's 20-seed seat rates per cell for the record's aggregates).

**The re-evaluation read (November 7, 2026; the ten finals 09:00–09:25, the readers to 09:40; `cr-finals/`: `seam-290-own.md`, `seam-2-own.md`, `seam-hand-{290,liars}.md`, `paired-290-vs-270-own-cr.md`, `mix-seats-20.md` + `.csv`, `fa-channels-*-cr.md`, `seat-income-*-cr.md`, `appeal-tell-liars-290-own-cr-direct.txt`).**

## 78. The step-back review, the plan, and ablation brief A1 reviewed against the engine (November 10, 2026)

**The economy reviewer's step-back review** (`docs/economy_review_2026-11-10_a_step_back.md`, verbatim; an opinion, not a ruling): one real result — peer verification among self-interested agents is honest only where truth the verifier cannot anticipate is present at the verdict — on clean evidence; the simulated world the best case on every assumption that matters for real agents (care reimbursed and effort observable, one-step learners, independent errors and no work product, one shared policy, the customer all-or-nothing); the design grown faster than its evidence; what to do next — freeze at 2.9.0, make the world honest before the rules, an ablation of a small core against the full rulebook, a pilot with language-model agents, the theory section.

**The implementer's plan, accepted as written with the reviewer's adjustments** (in the relay of November 10; none changing the order): (1) freeze at 2.9.0, finish the owed cells (§8.4–§8.7 on own-name credit, then the half); (2) two leak figures and two overhead views as readers on the finals of record; (3) the temptation within the rules — a payoff table on the scripted ε population by action and seat over temptation × bond × ruling accuracy first, learned chains at the frontier; the fourth action (claim the allowance and stamp) keeping the diverted allowance's value as a private benefit in its own-money reward; (4) the customer's detection as a per-case chance, swept on run 42's fixed policy (the mechanical read, labelled) and one midpoint retrained from both starts; (5) correlated errors reaching every careful verdict, the troops' and the ruling's included, the troops' correlation stated apart; (6) the multi-identity operator hand-written first (k of N names, the sibling stamps on the mark), its plant record the key reading; (7) the learner's horizon by own-name returns with the value head as the principal route, five training seeds a cell, one cell with two policies, every new learner version calibrated in a population cell first; (8) the ablation as the brief orders it. The pushback accepted both ways: in money checking costs nothing; what differs between checking and skipping is the bond at risk, and at the first seat that is why the own-money population cares.

**Ablation brief A1 — the small core's arms** (`spec/design_revisions/ablation_A1_the_small_core.md`, verbatim; candidate configurations, not in force) **reviewed against the engine** (`ablation_A1_the_small_core-review.md`): buildable as flags with eight corrections where the words meet the code — the deciding verdict's seats are troop Reviews with I8 widened under the flag (V_max 3 holds no room for them), answered by the driver the next tick as every troop's seat is, wrong 0.7 in a hundred, drawing the root's pots (sized for one review and one audit) with the rest the state's, its own seats never overturned; arm 1's audit escalation keeps the challenge record (the readers read it), a member panel confirms or escalates but never overturns (a change from today, to be said), the state's own work exempted or the state judges itself; arm 2's seat taken in the tick's pass with no wait and the plants' audit seat leaving with it, the accounting (≈960 state seats a run at c_T, the members' audit fees moving to the state, the customer paying nothing more); arm 3's plant challenges stopping, truthful ones included; arm 4 keeping `plants_every_seat` on so 2.8.0's roll at the verdict's odds is not reverted with it, the state's seat share rising to a third (not doubling); arm 5's record mapping, admission and dial input restated. One conformance detail shared by every arm: the reserve target and the member seats are sized on audit and panel demand the state now carries, to be excluded or every arm over-sizes the duty roster. One question (Q1): a confirming dispute panel is final and unchecked under arm 1 — the seat the 2.7.0 population stamped — so arm 1 tests the bond at the dissenting seat only unless a confirming panel is sampled. Hypothesis 1's "one in five" a ceiling (about one in ten in the careful basin). Cost: three to four days of engine work, two of driver and readers, 400 scripted runs, four learned chains. Nothing built, nothing run.

## 80. A1 §7.1 — the payoff table, read before any chain is trained (November 10, 2026; `results/redteam/stability/ablation-A1/payoff/payoff.md`)

**The instrument.** The scripted ε population of run 24's entry cell (every member a slot, ε 0.2 uniform around the careful, so action 0 is the policy's choice whatever the state and actions 1 and 2 the uniform draws: each action's mean reward at a seat is read without selection), collected on own-name credit for twenty seeds per arm (`payoff/<arm>/eps-seed*.npz`, the collector now storing the seat of each decision), read by `payoff_table.py`: own money by action and seat, means ± SE across seeds, with the share of truth-checked bad cases at each seat beside (a bad plant; at the audit seat under 2.9.0 a planted approval) — §2's left-hand side measured instead of assumed; the careful's own error the driver's 5 in 100.
