| arm 0, π^A pinned at 0.20 | 0.170 ± 0.001 | +4.52 | +3.19 | **−1.33 ± 0.09** | −1.96 ± 0.12 | −4.18 ± 0.17 |
| arm 1, the trusted ruling | 0.041 ± 0.002 | +4.29 | +4.48 | **+0.19 ± 0.11** | −2.13 ± 0.26 | −2.21 ± 0.28 |
| arm 1b, the sampled confirmation (0.25) | 0.039 ± 0.003 | +4.45 | +4.43 | **−0.02 ± 0.07** | −2.20 ± 0.16 | −2.76 ± 0.25 |
| arm 1c, the cheaper false alarm | 0.042 ± 0.003 | +5.00 | +4.87 | **−0.13 ± 0.08** | −2.16 ± 0.09 | −2.92 ± 0.29 |

- **The first review and the panel pay for checking in every arm** (a blind approval 2–4 credits under an inspection), where the truth-checked bad cases are 10–12 and 22–40 in 100.
- **The audit seat under 2.9.0 as in force is a coin toss in money**: 3.8 truth-checked bad in 100 against the careful's 5, the blind approval worth +0.02 ± 0.06 against inspecting — §2's line, where the learned populations' half-stamp sits (run 42's 0.45–0.66).
- **The lever from above prices it as §2 says**: at π^A 0.10 the share is 10 in 100 and the blind approval costs 0.62 ± 0.09; at 0.20, 17 in 100 and 1.33 ± 0.09. Hypothesis 2 (the seat closes on the learned population once the lever is held above the line) goes to the chain.
- **The trusted ruling does not price it** (+0.19 ± 0.11, toward the stamp if anything), as hypothesis 1 restated says; a correct rejection at the audit seat now loses −3.98 against −1.93 under arm 0, the punishment surer.
- **The sampled confirmation at 0.25 does nothing to the edge in this population** (−0.02 ± 0.07; ε / r needs the bad share at the seat above 0.2 — hypothesis 4's condition), and **the cheaper false alarm moves it a little** (−0.13 ± 0.08; the blind rejection's cost falls from −1.93 to +0.36, still 4.6 under inspecting) — whether that is enough for the learned population is hypothesis 3's question, on the chain.
- One reading of the instrument: under arm 1 the SEs at the initial seat are three to nine times the other arms' (±0.46 against ±0.05) — the deciding verdict's one-tick latency and the all-or-nothing of its outcome on a case's events spread the attributed reward of the first seat's decisions; the means are the other arms'.

**In flight (the queue, `ablation-A1/queue.sh`):** the scripted sweep (five cells × nine configurations × seeds 21–40, `scripted/launch.sh`; its readout `readout.py` → `finals.md`), the ruling's accuracy sweep after it (0.95 / 1.0 on arm 0); the owed chains first, then the three learned chains (`learned/{pa10,arm1c,arm4}/`) and §8.5's lone learners re-read on the corrected engine (Q3's and Q4's policies of record under 2.9.0 and under `--rules 2.7.0`, seeds 21–40). §8.4's probe at every seat on run 42's collection and Q1's re-arrival chain afresh follow when the machine frees. Not yet built: the per-seat bond multiplier for the bond sweep (×2, ×4 at the audit and panel seats), queued after the sweeps.

**Addendum (November 10, the reviewer's question):** the table's three action columns are each action's own money in absolute credits, so the blind rejection's −1.93 → +0.36 at the audit seat is absolute; against inspecting it reads −6.51 ± 0.10 under arm 0 and −4.64 ± 0.09 under arm 1c — a blind rejection does not pay under 1c, hypothesis 3 stands and 1c's chain runs as written. The table now prints both blind verdicts' edges against inspecting for every arm (`payoff.md`): at the audit seat the rejection's −6.51 (arm 0), −5.90 / −5.38 (π^A 0.10 / 0.20), −8.27 (arm 1), −8.36 (1b), −4.64 (1c) — the column that guards each arm against opening the other blind verdict. The three readings go into the brief as readings; the counts they ask for (arm 2's own-error verdicts with the false overturns the second seat prevented; the disputes refused after a sampled confirmation that stood) are added as counters before the ablation chains start — not while the scripted sweep runs on the committed tree.

## 81. A1 §7.2 — the scripted populations under the arms, and the ruling's accuracy swept (November 10, 2026; `results/redteam/stability/ablation-A1/scripted/finals.md`, `readout.py`, the 1,100 runs)

**The instrument.** Five scripted cells (all-careful with arrivals; run 24's 60/40 entry cell; the fixed 60/40; the entry cell with truthful disputes; the entry cell with liars at 0.1) × seeds 21–40 × eleven configurations: arm 0 (2.9.0 as in force), arm 0 with π^A pinned at 0.10 and 0.20, arm 0 with the ruling's accuracy 0.95 and 1.0 (the step-3 default, swept beside the arms), arms 1, 1b (r 0.25), 1c, 2, 3, 4 — 1,000 ticks, paired by seed against arm 0. Three runs stopped on I45 as first written (a troop's initial seat on a member's delivery overturned by the deciding verdict — today's rule for a troop seat settled against the state, the brief protecting only the verdict's own seats); I45 scoped at 5233e468d, the engine unchanged, the three re-run on it (`logs/*.failed-I45.log` kept). The 60/40 entry cell is the headline; the others agree in direction.

**The leak** (standing false approvals, arm − arm 0, the entry cell; the liar cell beside):

| arm | entry cell | liars | false penalties, initial / panel (entry) | deciding verdicts | the orchestrator's c_T | system's overhead / task | customer's overhead / task |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| arm 0 (level) | 7.2 ± 0.7 | 3.7 ± 0.5 | 20.5 / 11.9 | 0 | 2,398 | 15.8 | 14.4 |
| π^A 0.10 | +0.5 ± 0.7 | +0.3 ± 0.5 | 0 / 0 | 0 | +145 | +2.4 | +0.4 |
| π^A 0.20 | +0.9 ± 1.0 | +0.6 ± 0.6 | 0 / 0 | 0 | +102 | +4.5 | +0.3 |
| ruling 0.95 | −0.2 ± 0.9 | −1.7 ± 0.4 | −11.2 / −8.1 | 0 | −86 | −0.2 | −0.2 |
| ruling 1.0 | −0.5 ± 0.9 | −2.2 ± 0.4 | −16.2 / −11.9 | 0 | −17 | −0.2 | −0.3 |
| arm 1 | −0.3 ± 0.7 | −1.9 ± 0.4 | −17.9 / −11.2 | 239 | +938 | +0.2 | −0.2 |
| arm 1b | −2.0 ± 0.7 | −1.5 ± 0.6 | −15.3 / −11.2, audit +1.7 | 492 | +2,320 | +1.0 | +0.4 |
| arm 1c | −0.2 ± 0.7 | −1.8 ± 0.4 | −17.5 / −11.1 | 216 | +819 | +0.2 | −0.1 |
| arm 2 | −3.5 ± 0.6 | −2.3 ± 0.4 | −10.4 / −11.2 | 255 | +4,449 | −0.7 | −0.1 |
| arm 3 | −0.1 ± 0.7 | −2.7 ± 0.5 | −17.2 / −11.9 | 239 | +815 | −0.1 | −0.3 |
| arm 4 | −2.7 ± 0.9 | −3.0 ± 0.4 | −11.3 / −11.9 | 255 | +4,405 | −1.4 | −0.3 |

- **The false penalties under 2.9.0 are the ruling's, not the peers'.** Arm 0's 20 initial and 12 panel false penalties a run (a bond burned on a verdict that matched the truth) fall to 4–5 and 0 at a ruling of 1.0 and to 8–9 and 3–4 at 0.95, in every cell — the driver's ruling at 0.8 (its default since 1.0.1, a disposition, not a rule) burns a correct reviewer's bond at every split it gets wrong. Arm 1 removes the same penalties (−18 / −11) by the same route: its deciding verdict is a 0.993 judge in the ruling's place. What arm 1 adds over a ruling at 1.0 is the two-wrong-peers overturn it forbids — about 4 a run at the first seat — at the price of 240 deciding verdicts and 940 credits of c_T a run. **Hypothesis 1 holds** (the penalties cut, the leak not up), and the ruling's accuracy is the larger part of it.
- **Arms 2 and 4 take the hand's leak down by a third to a half in every cell** (−3.5 ± 0.6 and −2.7 ± 0.9 in the entry cell; −2.3 / −3.0 in the liar cell; −1.8 / −2.7 with truthful disputes; −2.3 / −1.5 in the fixed 60/40; nothing to take in the all-careful cell) — the member-confirmed channel zero by construction, the suspensions down by three a run, the system's overhead per task down 0.7–1.4 credits (the cost of truth −820 / −1,350 with the plants' later seats gone), the customers' tasks per 1,000 up 1.1 and the customer's overhead unchanged or lower. **Hypotheses 5 and 7 read as predicted on the scripted populations.** The orchestrator's cost is the c_T column: +4,400 credits a run (≈ 1,990 audit seats at 2.2 each after the allowance), of which 145–490 unreimbursed; its share of all review seats from a tenth to a third (X_t 6 → 4, the member seats 3 → 0 under arm 4: C1 as ruled).
- **The pins cost what §2 says**: +1,070 / +2,300 to the cost of truth and +2.4 / +4.5 to the system's overhead per task, the hand's leak unmoved (+0.5 ± 0.7 / +0.9 ± 1.0). Their value is on the learned population only (hypothesis 2, on the chain).
- **Arm 1b prices the confirmation and pays for it where the population is honest**: 1.7–2.5 false penalties at the audit seat a run in every cell (hypothesis 4's condition), twice arm 1's deciding verdicts, +2,320 c_T; the leak −2.0 ± 0.7 in the entry cell. **Arm 3** reads as arm 1 with the panels the state's: the lying appeal paid 0.85 → 0.25 and the lying dispute 3.3 → 0.8 a run (arm 1's 0 / 0.55: the state's bench errs where a member panel adjudicated by the deciding verdict did not — the 0.7% on 420 verdicts). **Hypothesis 6** holds on the liar cell: a lying challenge paid under one in a hundred filed (0.25 of 37 appeals, 0.8 of 92 disputes), the truthful won 86–94 of 100.
- The time from submission to vesting is unmoved (±0.08 of 12.2 ticks); P_t within noise in every arm; Ω's fee income −230 to −450 a run under arms 1, 1b, 1c and 3 (the audits held fall 150–350 a run — the member audit seats the dispute's or the verdict's settlement discharged); every invariant held on the 1,100 runs.

**What this decides.** On the hand's populations the orchestrator's seat holds the leak at or below 2.9.0's at a lower system overhead per task, so hypothesis 7's scripted half stands and the brief's refutation condition is not met here; the learned populations (arm 0 pinned at 0.10, arm 1c, arm 4 from the careful start; the chains queued behind the owed cells) decide the rest. The ruling's accuracy is a driver default (`--ruling-accuracy` 0.8 since 1.0.1); moving it is not a design change — the reviewer's call whether the cells of record should carry 0.95 or 1.0 from here, since every false-penalty reading on record carries the 0.8.

**Addendum 2 (November 10, the reviewer's panel check before 1c's chain; `payoff.md`, the table "the panel by its question"):** the appeal panel and the dispute panel split from the observation's question one-hot as the probe splits them, the two edges against inspecting. At the **appeal panel** (10,800 decisions under arm 0, 7,450 under arm 1, 8,000 under 1c; the truth-checked bad cases 0.23–0.34 — the planted lying appeals) both blind verdicts are clearly under inspecting in every arm: arm 0 −2.59 ± 0.16 / −2.86 ± 0.24, arm 1 −2.24 ± 0.31 / −5.03 ± 0.41, arm 1c −3.02 ± 0.29 / **−2.50 ± 0.17** — 1c's lenience raises the blind confirmation (the rejection that matches a standing N) by 2.5 credits and leaves it 2.5 under inspecting. At the **dispute panel** (170–220 decisions a cell: in run 24's world every dispute is a planted lying dispute of a good plant, the real requesters filing none) the blind reject is −9.57 ± 0.33 under arm 0, −10.13 ± 0.36 under arm 1 and **−5.09 ± 0.22 under 1c** (the lenience halving what the lie costs it, as the reviewer reasoned, and leaving it 5 under inspecting); the blind uphold is at inspecting in every arm but arm 1 — +0.13 ± 0.54 (arm 0), +0.46 ± 0.25 (1c), +1.07 ± 0.34 (1b), −2.06 ± 2.26 (arm 1, one seed's lump) — by composition: with no real dispute in the cell the standing F at a dispute panel is always right, so a blind uphold is a free correct answer there (§57's reading of the panel's gap), under arm 0 as under 1c, and not a blind verdict 1c opens. By the reviewer's condition (a blind verdict at or above inspecting under 1c where under arm 1 it is clearly below) nothing is met: **1c runs as built**, the lenience at the audit and panel seats; the hold on its chain released. The pinned arms' panels beside: the appeal panel's blind approval −3.44 / −4.27 at π^A 0.10 / 0.20 (the planted lies there 0.33 / 0.41 of the cases), the blind rejection −2.31 / −2.16. The brief's addendum 1 on record (`spec/design_revisions/ablation_A1_r2_the_small_core.md`, §1–§9 unchanged); the owed chains' note will carry each pinned cell with its pin and the prediction it is read against (§8.7's m^A at 1: the capture returns; the brief's 0.10 / 0.20: below 0.15), and every learned chain's note the first review's blind-verdict rate beside the audit seat's.

**On record:** the brief's addendum 2 (`spec/design_revisions/ablation_A1_r2_the_small_core.md`, §1–§9 unchanged) and the reviewer's first reads (`ablation_A1_first_reads.md`). **Rulings taken on:** the ruling's accuracy stays at 0.8 for every cell of record (the world every run on record was read in; swept at 1.5.0 and left; an accurate ruling at no cost is free truth — what one costs is what arm 1 pays); hypothesis 1 recorded in two parts, the judge's accuracy carrying most of it and the structure (a panel confirms or escalates) removing the two-wrong-peers overturn; the false penalties by source in every table from here — **the driver's harvest now splits them by the challenge's settler** (`false_penalty_by_ruling` / `_by_peers` / `_by_deciding`; the scripted readout and `run_q`'s chain record carry them, with the leak's two figures, the two overhead views and the time to vesting, so every learned chain's note reads the system's overhead a task beside the leak); **one count, no build — the rulings sought a run under arm 0, by cell** (`finals.md`): all-careful 101 ± 3 (20 wrong), the entry cell 99 ± 3 (19), the fixed 60/40 80 ± 2 (15), truthful disputes 105 ± 3 (21), liars 113 ± 2 (21) — so a ruling seated only where it is sought today is about a hundred seats a run (one troop's at 0.95, ≈ 300 c_T; or a deciding verdict's two or three, ≈ 600–700) beside arm 1's 240 verdicts and 940 c_T, which also escalates the audit's disagreements and the panels' non-confirmations the ruling never saw. **Arm 2's chain added** after 1c's (`learned/arm2/`, `queue2.sh`): the order the owed chains, arm 0 pinned at 0.10, arm 4, 1c, arm 2.

In run 24's world, where no requester disputes, every dispute is a planted lie on a good plant and the learned population **confirms the standing approval blind 79 times in 100 at the dispute panel** (the ε floor 7; the appeal panel of the same population 11) — the panel taught by planted lies alone; where the requesters dispute truthfully and the liars at 0.1 (the liar chain, the dispute panel a third plants) it inspects 91 in 100 and confirms blind 5. The reviewer's suspicion for the detection sweep stands on this number: where customers dispute rarely, a panel taught by planted lies confirms blind, two confirmations stand unchecked, and the truthful dispute is lost — to be read at a detection of 0.25 under arms 0, 1 and 2 against 3 and 4, refuted if the learned dispute panel inspects there and truthful disputes are won at the scripted rate. Nothing built on it. The run-42 figure is read on the round-10 collection (the final policy's own collection, `online11`, comes with §8.4's probe job and will be read beside).

## 83. The reviewer's three reads on the dispute panel (November 10, 2026): the liar chain's panel by the dispute's truth, the engine's reading confirmed, the frozen policy at a detection of 0.1 and 0.25 launched

**The reviewer's note taken on** (8929a6304 read; the costing recorded as derived: a ruling seated only where it is sought today ≈ 100 deciding verdicts a run, ≈ 390 of c_T at arm 1's own rate — 940 / 240 ≈ 3.9 a verdict — for about 19 of the 20 wrong rulings; arm 1's further 140 verdicts ≈ 550 of c_T buy the two-wrong-peers overturn, ≈ 4 a run, and a panel that cannot pay a lie on its own; for the design revision after the runs, no arm). The first reads on record updated (`spec/design_revisions/ablation_A1_first_reads.md`).

**The engine's reading, confirmed.** 2.8.0's truthful planted dispute is the plant requester's dispute of a *bad plant's pending F* at the trailing real requesters' rate on unfulfilled work approved (the driver's `dispute_window`; zero where the population does not dispute) — so it arrives only where a member has wrongly approved a bad plant, at a rate the population controls, and in run 24's world, where no requester disputes, at none; the planted lie — the plant requester's dispute of a *good* plant's F — arrives at the lie quota (max(2, π^Ld × the trailing period's real disputes), the floor of two a period whatever the population does). In the careful basin that panel therefore sees lies only, as the reviewer reads it; nothing in the engine has it otherwise. (Under `--dispute-policy truthful` the real requesters' rate is read from the window and the truthful planted dispute follows it.)

**Read (2): the liar chain's dispute panel by the dispute's truth** (the round-10 collection; a truthful dispute contests unfulfilled work, a lie contests fulfilled):

| set | truthful share of the panel's cases | truthful: decisions, blind confirmation, inspects (plant share) | lies: the same |
|---|---|---|---|
| the liar chain's population, round 10 | 0.282 ± 0.009 | 2,159 — **0.055 ± 0.006**, 0.903 (0.52) | 5,382 — 0.048 ± 0.004, 0.907 (0.26) |
| the same cell's ε set | 0.326 ± 0.010 | 2,609 — 0.063 ± 0.006, 0.882 (0.53) | 5,309 — 0.068 ± 0.003, 0.866 (0.33) |

A second point against run 42's 0.79 at none: with 28 in 100 of the panel's disputes truthful (half of those on bad plants — the truthful planted dispute at the real rate), the learned population inspects 90 in 100 on both kinds and confirms blind 5 — the panel priced where truthful disputes come, and not told apart from the lies (0.055 against 0.048).

**Read (3), launched** (`ablation-A1/detection/launch.sh`; the switch exists — the driver's `--dispute-policy truthful --dispute-rate r`, the per-case probability a requester disputes a false approval, 1.0 in the truthful-dispute cells of record): run 42's final policy (the direct's round-10 model) frozen in a world at a detection of 0.1 and of 0.25, seeds 21–40, the scripted careful and the 60/40 hand on the same slots in the same worlds (`frozen-d0.1/`, `frozen-d0.25/`); the readings the disputes filed and won by the dispute's truth, the standing false approvals and the leak, the court fees. The reviewer's expectation, written before: truthful disputes won under a half — about three in ten (both seats confirm 0.64 of the time; a split to the 0.8 ruling) — against more than nine in ten for the scripted careful; refuted if the frozen population wins more than eight in ten. Read (1) comes with §8.4's collection.

## 84. Read (3): run 42's policy, frozen, where customers notice bad work 10 and 25 times in 100 (November 10, 2026; `results/redteam/stability/ablation-A1/detection/frozen-d0.1/`, `frozen-d0.25/`)

**The instrument.** The direct's round-10 model of run 42 (`learned-rev-careful-290-own`) frozen — not retrained — in run 24's entry cell with `--dispute-policy truthful --dispute-rate 0.1` and `0.25` (the driver's per-case probability that a requester disputes a false approval; 1.0 in the truthful-dispute cells of record), seeds 21–40, 1,000 ticks; the scripted careful and the 60/40 hand on the same slots in the same worlds. An evaluation under fixed rules, run ahead of the chains as the reviewer asked.

| detection | policy | truthful disputes filed a run | won | **won share** | standing false approvals | − the hand's (paired) | lost disputes a run (the court fee each) | panel blind-verdict rate, plant / real (all panel kinds) | audit seat blind, real |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1 | run 42's policy, frozen | 4.1 ± 0.4 | 3.0 ± 0.3 | **0.72** | 14.7 ± 1.5 | +7.6 ± 1.5 | 1.15 ± 0.20 | 0.28 / 0.17 | 0.52 |
| 0.1 | the scripted careful | 3.6 ± 0.6 | 3.5 ± 0.6 | 0.97 | 2.6 ± 0.4 | −4.6 ± 0.7 | 0.10 ± 0.07 | 0 / 0 | 0 |
| 0.1 | the 60/40 hand | 8.7 ± 0.6 | 8.5 ± 0.6 | 0.98 | 7.1 ± 0.5 | — | 0.20 | — | — |
| 0.25 | run 42's policy, frozen | 11.0 ± 0.5 | 7.7 ± 0.4 | **0.70** | 15.4 ± 1.2 | +8.8 ± 1.3 | 3.30 ± 0.41 | 0.40 / 0.21 | 0.54 |
| 0.25 | the scripted careful | 7.1 ± 0.5 | 7.0 ± 0.5 | 0.99 | 2.0 ± 0.4 | −4.7 ± 0.7 | 0.10 ± 0.07 | 0 / 0 | 0 |
| 0.25 | the 60/40 hand | 23.5 ± 1.1 | 22.6 ± 1.0 | 0.96 | 6.7 ± 0.6 | — | 0.90 | — | — |

**Read.** The loss is real and it is a quarter to three in ten of the truthful disputes, not seven in ten: the frozen population wins **72 in 100 at a detection of 0.1 and 70 at 0.25** against 97–99 for the scripted careful and 96–98 for the hand — between the reviewer's expectation (about three in ten won) and the refuter (more than eight in ten). Each lost dispute leaves the bad work paid and costs the customer the court fee: 1.2 and 3.3 lost disputes a run. The disputes do their work on the rest — the standing false approvals 14.7 / 15.4 against run 42's 16.3 at a detection of none — but the population's leak stays twice the hand's (+7.6 ± 1.5 / +8.8 ± 1.3), and the hand's requesters, with more bad approvals before them to dispute, file twice as many and win nearly all. The panels pooled (appeal, dispute and the third seat) are answered blind 0.17–0.21 on real cases and 0.28–0.40 on plants — the dispute panel's own rate on the final policy comes with read (1); the audit seat 0.52–0.54, run 42's. The watch item's second half, stated as it is: a panel taught by planted lies alone confirms the standing approval too often when a truthful dispute arrives, and in these worlds loses three of ten; the retrained cells from both starts (step 4 proper) decide whether the population learns to look once truthful disputes exist — the liar chain, where 28 in 100 of the panel's disputes were truthful, says it does (§83). Nothing built; the rulebook frozen; the reviewer's candidates for a later draft (the planted challenge in both truths at the dial's rate, the sampled confirmation, the state's bench) stand as named.

## 85. The dispute panel's actions by the dispute's truth and owner, and its seats by holder, in the two detection cells (November 10, 2026; `ablation-A1/detection/collect-d{0.1,0.25}/`, `panel-by-truth-d*.txt`, `frozen2-d*/`)

**The instruments.** (i) One round of run 42's final policy — the direct's round-10 model started from (`--start`; the online driver fits afresh without it, the fault caught and the first collections discarded before any read, 73231d019) with ε 0.1 uniform as the rounds were — in each detection cell, seeds 1–20 (`online11`), the dispute-panel decisions split by the case's truth and owner from the observation's question one-hot, the appeal question beside (`panel_by_truth.py`); (ii) the two detection evaluations re-run with `run_q`'s record now carrying the seats by their holder and the false penalties by source (`frozen2-d*`, seeds 21–40; the figures of §84 reproduced to the decimal).

**(a) The seat's holder.** In the learned population the state holds **39 in 100 of the panel seats** at both detections (the state's appeal seats 155 / 163 a run beside the members' 241 / 215; the careful's and the hand's 27 in 100, the members holding 301–384) — the learned population's members are the less eligible for panels (the duty cap, the record) and its panels wait to the state more often; the state's audit seats 811 / 896 a run beside the members' 1,200 / 1,119. A state seat confirms a truthful dispute only at ε_T.

**(b) The tell: none.** On the dispute panel the members confirm the customers' truthful disputes blind at the planted lies' rate:

| detection | kind | decisions | inspect | blind confirmation (F stands) | blind rejection |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1 | truthful, the customer's | 93 | 0.34 ± 0.06 | **0.62 ± 0.07** | 0.04 |
| 0.1 | truthful, the plant's (a bad plant) | 119 | 0.24 ± 0.07 | 0.75 ± 0.07 | 0.01 |
| 0.1 | a lie, the plant's (a good plant) | 212 | 0.38 ± 0.06 | **0.61 ± 0.06** | 0.01 |
| 0.25 | truthful, the customer's | 206 | 0.27 ± 0.06 | **0.69 ± 0.06** | 0.04 |
| 0.25 | truthful, the plant's | 241 | 0.22 ± 0.03 | 0.72 ± 0.05 | 0.06 |
| 0.25 | a lie, the plant's | 229 | 0.28 ± 0.04 | **0.69 ± 0.04** | 0.02 |

The customers' and the orchestrator's lies are not told apart (0.62 against 0.61; 0.69 against 0.69); the population is priced by the lies as far as it is priced at all. **(c)** The final policy's own blind confirmation at the dispute panel, 0.65 / 0.70 over every kind, is under the round-10 collection's 0.79 at a detection of none — the collection's round-9 policy a little blinder than the final, and the panel's cases now partly truthful — but not by much.

**The arithmetic closes.** A per-seat blind confirmation of 0.61 × 0.62 + 0.39 × 0.05 ≈ 0.40 (the members' rate on the customers' disputes, the state's share at its error) gives a lost dispute at 0.40² + 2 × 0.40 × 0.60 × 0.2 ≈ 0.26 — §84's 0.28 / 0.30. So seven in ten won is (a) and (b) together: the members confirm blind about two times in three as the collection said, and the state's seats, two in five of the panel, carry most of the customers' wins. The appeal question beside (appeals and the third seat, by the case's truth and owner): inspected 0.81 on every kind, the real unfulfilled cases (the third seat on bad work) rejected blind 0.25–0.28 and approved blind 0.04–0.05, the plants' 0.10 / 0.06.

**What it decides.** Nothing built, the rulebook frozen. The watch item's second half stands with its cause named: the dispute panel of a population taught where nobody disputes confirms blind two times in three whatever the dispute's truth, and the loss of three in ten is the members' two-in-three times the state's absence from three seats in five. The retrained cells (after the ablation chains; run 42's policy the start, at 0.25 then 0.1) read whether the truthful disputes' arrival teaches the seat; the reviewer's amended expectation, on record before: at 0.25 the panel inspects and truthful disputes are won near the scripted rate.

## 86. The customers' truthful disputes by the panel's make-up, and the state's panel seats by cause (November 10, 2026; `ablation-A1/detection/disputes-by-panel-d{0.1,0.25}.md`, `disputes_by_panel.py`; the driver's `panel_short_tier_ticks` / `panel_short_free_ticks` at 77226f959)

**The instrument.** Run 42's final policy and the scripted careful on seeds 21–40 at a detection of 0.1 and 0.25, read from the engine's cases at the end: every customer's truthful dispute (a real root, unfulfilled, the requester the challenger) by its panel's make-up — two members, one member and one of the state's, two of the state's — and whether it was won; beside it, at every panel seat the driver could not fill, whether a free on-duty member existed but was kept off the panel tier (by its record, or undetermined — fewer than n_min bad plants judged) or no member was free at all.

**Read 1.** The customers' truthful disputes won, by the panel's make-up (pooled over the seeds; the count behind):

| detection | make-up | run 42's policy: disputes a run | won share | the careful: won share |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1 | two members | 1.7 | **0.44** (34) | 0.97 (38) |
| 0.1 | one member, one of the state's | 1.4 | **0.89** (27) | 1.00 (13) |
| 0.1 | two of the state's | 1.0 | **1.00** (20) | 1.00 (20) |
| 0.25 | two members | 3.2 | **0.23** (64) | 0.99 (67) |
| 0.25 | one member, one of the state's | 3.8 | **0.82** (76) | 0.97 (37) |
| 0.25 | two of the state's | 4.0 | **0.98** (79) | 1.00 (38) |

The reviewer's arithmetic from §85's rates (about a half, about 84 in 100, and 98) is the direct figure within its counts: a panel of two learned members wins the customer's dispute 44 in 100 at a detection of 0.1 and 23 at 0.25, a mixed panel 89 / 82, the state's panels 100 / 98 — the careful's members 97–99 at every make-up. The customers' seven in ten (§84) is the three make-ups weighted: at 0.25 the learned population's disputes fall 29 / 35 / 36 in 100 to the three, the two-member panel winning one in four. Recorded as it is: **where two learned members sit the customer's dispute it is lost three times in four; the customers' wins are the state's seats.**

**Read 2.** The state's 39 in 100 of the panel seats, by cause: **the tier, not the supply** — of the 263 / 276 ticks a run on which a panel seat waited unfilled at the driver's seating under run 42's policy, 262.5 / 275.4 had a free on-duty member kept off the panel tier and 0.5 / 0.1 had no member free (the careful: 192 / 210 ticks, likewise all tier). The member-periods panel-ineligible: 92.6 / 92.1 a run in the learned population against the careful's 63.3 / 63.5 — the careful's 63 are the undetermined (an arrival is panel-ineligible until n_min bad plants are judged, 1.6.0's `panel_determined`), the learned population's 29 more are its members' records (â above θ_panel = 0.2, the audit seat's half-stamp on bad plants: run 42's 19 / 12 bad plants approved a run). So the reviewer's reading holds: **2.9.0's price on the audit seat is what put the state on the panel** — the learned members lose the panel tier by stamping planted approvals at the audit seat, and the state takes the seats they cannot sit; the undetermined share is the careful's too. Nothing built; the rulebook frozen. The retrained cells (after the ablation chains; run 42's policy the start, 0.25 then 0.1) read the state's share of the panel seats beside the members' own rate, as ordered: a population that inspects at the panel and still stamps at the audit seat keeps losing its tier.

## 87. The joint of the two verdicts on the customers' truthful disputes (November 10, 2026; `ablation-A1/detection/disputes-by-panel-d{0.1,0.25}.md`, the reader extended; the brief with its addenda through C6 on record)

**The instrument.** On the same cases as §86 (run 42's final policy and the careful, seeds 21–40, a detection of 0.1 and 0.25), each panel's two verdicts on a customer's truthful dispute — both confirm the standing F, split, both reject — against the product of the single-seat F rate p on the same panels (p², 2p(1−p), (1−p)²); the two-member panels the question, since there both seats are the one shared policy (on a mixed panel the product of one rate is not the right reference: the state's seat rejects at 1 − ε_T and the member's confirms, so a split is the typical outcome — 0.82 observed).

| detection | make-up | panels | single-seat F rate p | both confirm, observed / p² | split, observed / 2p(1−p) | both reject, observed / (1−p)² |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1 | two learned members | 34 | 0.706 | **0.500 / 0.498** | 0.412 / 0.415 | 0.088 / 0.087 |
| 0.25 | two learned members | 64 | 0.828 | **0.750 / 0.686** | 0.156 / 0.285 | 0.094 / 0.030 |
| 0.1 | two careful members | 38 | 0.039 | 0.000 / 0.002 | 0.079 / 0.076 | 0.921 / 0.923 |
| 0.25 | two careful members | 67 | 0.067 | 0.000 / 0.005 | 0.134 / 0.125 | 0.866 / 0.870 |

**Read.** At a detection of 0.1 the two seats are independent to the third decimal (both confirm 0.500 against 0.498 on 34 panels); at 0.25 they agree more than independence gives — both confirm 0.75 against 0.69, both reject 0.094 against 0.030, the split 0.16 against 0.29 on 64 panels — **a mild correlation of the two verdicts, the one shared policy making the same choice on the same case, recorded as correlated verdicts already in the population ahead of step 5**. But the loss is the single seat's rate before it is the correlation's: on the two-member panels the members' verdict is F **0.71 / 0.83** of the time on a customer's truthful dispute (seeds 21–40; the collection's blind confirmation of §85, 0.62 / 0.69, was seeds 1–20 under ε 0.1, a third of whose draws inspect — the policy's own rate is above it), and independent seats at 0.83 win 0.03 + 0.29 × 0.8 ≈ 0.26 against the 0.23 observed; at 0.69 they would win 0.43, as the reviewer computed, but 0.69 is not the rate on these panels. So the reviewer's conditional holds in part: the two members do confirm together more than chance at 0.25, and most of the three-in-ten they win is the seat's own 0.83. The careful's two-member panels: both reject 0.87–0.92, independent.

**Recorded from the reviewer's note:** 2.9.0's price on the audit seat does not deter this learner, which cannot perceive eligibility — it filters: the members whose records fail lose their panel seats, the state takes them, and that holds the customers' disputes at seven in ten; three in ten is what two members win. Two readings for the design revision, nothing built: one seat of the state's on a panel is worth most of two (0.89 / 0.82 won against 0.44 / 0.23); the careful's 27 in 100 is the tier's own cost — the undetermined kept off the panel in a population that fails no plant. From here every collection on record is made by a script kept in the repo, its note naming the script and the commit (§8.4's first). The retrained cells as ordered, read by make-up with the state's share of the panel seats beside the members' own rate. Nothing else from the reviewer until the joint read and the owed chains are in.

**Addendum to §87 (the reviewer's reading, November 10):** recorded and the reviewer's reading corrected — the two members' seats are independent at 0.1 and mildly correlated at 0.25; what loses the dispute is the seat's own rate, 0.71 / 0.83 on these panels, not a shared choice ("two members are not two looks" withdrawn as written: two members are two seats that mostly do not look). Two consequences: §85's (c) is read with its ε — without exploration the final policy's seat confirms as often as the round-10 collection's 0.79, so the customers' seven in ten is the state's seats alone; the correlation at 0.25 is recorded as mild and unexplained (64 panels over 20 seeds cannot separate a shared choice on the same case from seeds with different rates), step 5's to measure, nothing asked now. The first reads on record updated.

## 88. The owed cells on own-name credit (November 10, 2026; `results/redteam/stability/learned-rev-{pifloor,picap,half}-290-own/`, the pairings at `2.9.0-s87-own/finals-paired-{pins,half,half-vs-run42}.md`, the channel splits `2.9.0-s87-own/fa-channels-*-290-own.md`): at the floor the pin changes nothing for the learned population, whose dial already sat there; at the cap the rate is a lever from above on own money — the audit seat 0.21 / 0.18 blind — paid at two and a half times the system's overhead a task; the half's audit seat 0.34 / 0.24, the dose-response holding

**The cells and their pins.** Three chains on run 42's recipe (`learned-rev-careful-290-own/launch.sh`: the careful-weighted offline set on seeds 1–20, ten rounds of ε 0.1 uniform exploration, the direct the carrier and the world model under the head alone beside, stride 4, own-name credit, the finals on seeds 21–40 with the careful, the stamper and the 60/40 `hand` on the same slots), every round and every final on the corrected engine (ce29d2b14's emission, the roll's defaults 0.5 / 20; the flags of 24490af3b–401a34a9e, A1's all off); `launch.sh all` each, the finals at `{direct,wm}/eval/final/`. Paired against run 42 (`learned-rev-careful-290-own/`, `eval/final-cr/`, §77's re-evaluation on the corrected engine), the same seeds.
- **(b′) `learned-rev-picap-290-own/` — π_t pinned at π_max 0.40 (`--pi0 0.40`), the dial live on the rest.** The same prediction's other end — the mirror, a diagnostic as in run 37. Against run 42.
- **The half, `learned-rev-half-290-own/` (`--learner-pop half`).** Against run 41 (`learned-rev-half-290/`, §71: the shared purse at 2.9.0 on the engine of October 28 — the roll's old defaults 0.2 / 5 and the macOS episode, so that pairing carries the defaults' move and the seam of §75 and §77: the all-careful's own standing false approvals 9.7 → 3.6 are the defaults') and against run 42 (the dose: half the members learning against all of them, the same engine and defaults).
- **Not among the three: 2.9.0 §8.7's own pinned cell, m^A at 1** (the rc's item 7: "the self-play population with m^A pinned at 1 — the planted approvals at the floor — expected: the seat unpriced, the capture of run 36 returns; the lever shown from below"). It is a different pin — π^A, not π_t — and was not launched with the owed cells (§79 launched 2.8.0's (a′) and (b′) on own money). Its launcher is `learned-rev-pafloor-290-own/launch.sh` (`--pi-a-max 0.02` = π^A_0, so the per-seat multiplier cannot step; the rest of the dial live), queued behind arm 2's chain (`ablation-A1/queue3.sh`), read against the rc's prediction when it lands. The readers: `2.9.0-s82/finals-paired.py` with a named base cell (`BASECELL`) and the two leak figures, the two overhead views, the time to vesting, the rulings sought and the false penalties by seat as rows (run 42's `final-cr` was written before those keys existed in `run_q`, so its columns read `nan` for them — the cell's own hand and careful carry them); `fa_channels.py` as in §72, on each chain's final models.

**The seats — the first review's blind-verdict rate beside the audit seat's** (measured from the reviews, real cases; direct / head alone; the last column the bad plants met at the audit seat over the members' audit decisions a run, against the brief's §2 line — the inspector's own ε, 5 in 100):

| cell | care | first review blind | audit seat blind | panel blind | planted approvals confirmed (pooled F̂^A) | truth-checked bad at the audit seat, in 100 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| run 42 (the dial live; `final-cr`) | 0.825 / 0.878 | 0.024 / 0.010 | **0.539 / 0.409** | 0.135 / 0.010 | 0.45 / 0.61 (§72) | 3.8 / 3.6 |
| (a′) π_t at the floor 0.10 | 0.807 / 0.853 | 0.027 / 0.001 | **0.603 / 0.565** | 0.208 / 0.073 | 0.49 / 0.55 | 3.3 / 3.1 |
| (b′) π_t at the cap 0.40 | 0.923 / 0.942 | 0.008 / 0.003 | **0.214 / 0.178** | 0.038 / 0.007 | 0.15 / 0.14 | 7.8 / 5.4 |

The hand's truth-checked bad share at the audit seat 3.5 in 100 at the floor and in run 42, 3.9 at the cap, 7.6 in the half's world; the careful's 2.4 / 3.5 / 5.3. The seat's blind rate follows that share across the cells — 3.1–3.8 in 100 → 0.41–0.60, about 5 → 0.24–0.34, 5.4–7.8 → 0.18–0.21 — §2's arithmetic reading through on the learned populations as it did on the scripted table (§80).

**(a′) — the pin at the floor changes nothing for the learned population, because that is where its dial already sat.** Paired against run 42 by seed: the standing false approvals +0.8 ± 1.4 / −0.8 ± 0.9 (17.1 ± 0.9 / 9.2 ± 0.9 against 16.3 / 9.9), care −0.02 / −0.03, the audit seat +0.06 / +0.16 blind, the market unmoved (P_t +0.6 ± 0.5 / −0.5 ± 0.5, tasks per 1,000 −0.5 ± 0.4 / +0.2 ± 0.5, the real lifetime −893 ± 946 / +55 ± 634), the cost of truth −242 ± 64 / −323 ± 93 (1,656 / 1,615 against 1,897 / 1,937 — the live dial's cost in run 42 is within 15% of the pinned floor's). Run 42's population is careful enough at the first seat (F̂ 0.05–0.08 under F* 0.10) that its dial held m_t near 1 and π_t near its floor through the run: the pin names the state the live dial was in. The per-seat lever moves as in run 42: 32 / 28 planted approvals a run, confirmed at the audit seat 15.4 / 14.7 (pooled F̂^A 0.49 / 0.55), m^A 3.8 / 3.4 by the end, π^A 0.076 / 0.068 — 2.7 / 2.6 planted approvals in 100 of the members' audit decisions, 3.3 / 3.1 truth-checked bad cases in all, under the line, and the seat stays half-stamped there as §2 says it may. What the pin does move is the hand: with a quarter of run 42's plants (16 planted approvals a run against 26; the dial's plants' mint 10,115) its stampers live longer — the 60/40 mix's measured blind share at the first review 0.28 / 0.28 against run 42's 0.10 / 0.15, the suspensions 19.4 from 15.8 (the stampers cycle through probation more often), the standing false approvals 10.1 from 7.2 (+2.9 ± 0.6), the cost of truth 1,262 from 4,034 (−2,772 ± 72: the hand's live dial runs m_t up on its stampers' record; the pin stops it). So the learned population reads 1.7× / 0.9× the hand's here against 2.3× / 1.4× in run 42 — a ratio moved by the hand, not by the learner. The prediction is not met on own money, and could not be: the capture that was to return was the shared purse's (§72), and on own money the floor is where the population already was. The leak at the floor: 0.0084 / 0.0045 false approvals per vested task (0.033 / 0.018 per bad delivery) against the hand's 0.0059 (0.026) and the careful's 0.0014 (0.005); the system's overhead a vested task 9.1 / 9.3 against the hand's 10.2 and the careful's 10.1, the customers' 11.8 / 11.7 against the hand's 15.7 and the careful's 12.4; the rulings sought 64 / 76 a run (the hand's 93, the careful's 100), 14.5 / 13.6 of them wrong — the 0.8 ruling's; the false penalties 29.1 / 14.7 a run at the initial seat, 0.1 at the audit seat, 5.6 / 6.6 at a panel (the careful's own 21.8 / 0.05 / 12.6). The state's seats 0.40 / 0.43 per real root (+0.036 ± 0.018 / +0.062 ± 0.023 over run 42; the careful's 0.31): with few plants the records determine slowly — 76–77% of the member-periods panel-ineligible against run 42's 70% and the careful's own 54% in the same cell.

**(b′) — at the cap the rate is a lever from above on own money, and it is paid in the system's overhead and the plants' price.** The audit seat's blind rate 0.21 / 0.18 on real cases (0.18 / 0.16 on the plants), from run 42's 0.54 / 0.41; care 0.92 / 0.94 (+0.10 / +0.06); the panel 0.04 / 0.01; the first review 0.008 / 0.003. The standing false approvals 9.1 ± 0.6 / 8.9 ± 0.8 — −7.2 ± 1.3 / −1.0 ± 1.0 paired against run 42 — against the cell's own hand 4.0 ± 0.4 (−3.2 ± 0.9: four times the plants catch its stampers sooner, 74.8 bad plants approved of 676 met) and the careful's 4.5 ± 0.6 (+1.8 ± 0.6: the careful's own ε on four times the bad plants — 37.4 of 716 met at the first seat, 5.2 in 100): **2.3× / 2.2× the hand's, 2.0× the careful's**, from 2.3× / 1.4× and 6.3× / 3.8× in run 42 — the direct's count falls by nearly half, the head alone's gain is against its references. The lever's mechanics: 724 bad plants a run met at the first seat (194 at the floor), 147 / 98 at the audit seat — 7.8 / 5.4 in 100 of the members' audit decisions, above the line — of which the planted approvals 115 / 68 a run, the dial's own (m^A 9.0 / 4.0, π^A 0.18 / 0.08 by the end; F̂^A pooled 0.15 / 0.14, the last period's 0.09 / 0.04), confirmed 17 / 9. Where run 37 on the shared purse read the audit seat stamped 0.84–0.87 at this pin (§60), own money reads it at a fifth: the shared purse's blind approval was paid in other names' money (§72: 13.2 credits a stamp), which no plant rate could reach; the own-money seat is priced by the truth-checked bad share and answers it. The cost: the cost of truth 7,525 / 7,110 a run (+5,628 ± 158 / +5,172 ± 129 over run 42; the hand's 6,430, the careful's 6,872); the system's overhead a vested task **23.4 / 22.7 against 9.1 at the floor** and the hand's 22.1 here — two and a half times, for a leak of 0.0046 / 0.0045 per vested task (0.018 / 0.018 per bad delivery; the hand's 0.0022, the careful's 0.0023) against 0.0084 / 0.0045 at the floor; and the plants as roots crowd the market — P_t 25.6 / 24.7 (+10.7 ± 0.8 / +8.5 ± 0.8 over run 42; the hand's +3.7, the careful's +8.9), the members' price 21.5 / 21.7, the real lifetime −6,983 ± 868 / −4,299 ± 1,003 (the careful's own −5,549 ± 1,031), tasks per 1,000 nominal −3.6 ± 0.4 / −3.0 ± 0.6 (the careful's −2.7 ± 0.7), the slots alive 19.9 / 19.6 (−3.5 / −4.0; the careful's −3.9), the awards −98 / −99 (the careful's −65); the customers' overhead a task 11.7 / 11.7 (the hand's 14.7). The market's movement is the plants' at 0.40, not the learner's: the careful population pays the same. The state's seats fall to 0.07 per real root (−0.29): four times the plants determine every record within the first period, the panel-ineligible member-periods 28% / 25% (the careful's 24%), and the members hold the seats. The brief's "below 0.15" is hypothesis 2's threshold for the π^A pins; here, with π^A at 0.18 reached by the dial under π_t 0.40, the seat reads 0.21 / 0.18 — near the line, not under it; the pa10 chain decides it. The rulings sought 75 / 77 (14.7 / 15.6 wrong); the false penalties 18.7 / 16.8 at the initial seat, 0.15 / 0.10 at the audit seat, 13.2 / 14.1 at a panel (the careful's 19.4 / 0.35 / 16.7).

**The half on own money — the dose-response holds.** Against run 41: care 0.87 / 0.92 from 0.78 / 0.82; the first review 0.016 / 0.000 blind from 0.15 / 0.07; the audit seat 0.34 / 0.24 from 0.54 / 0.61 (−0.20 / −0.37); the panel 0.10 / 0.00 from 0.13 / 0.02; the standing false approvals 10.9 ± 0.9 / 7.9 ± 0.8 against the hand's 7.2 ± 0.7 — **1.5× / 1.1×** (run 41's 1.8× / 1.5×) — −7.8 ± 1.5 / −8.1 ± 1.5 paired, of which the hand's own −3.2 ± 0.7 and the careful's −6.0 ± 0.9 are the roll's defaults and the seam (§75, §77), so the ratio to the hand is the clean figure. Against run 42 (all learning; the same engine and defaults; the half cell's `hand` arm is run 42's hand to the decimal, the same world): the audit seat 0.34 / 0.24 against 0.54 / 0.41, the standing false approvals −5.3 ± 1.7 / −2.0 ± 0.8 (10.9 / 7.9 against 16.3 / 9.9) — half the members learning leak less than all of them, as §52's half and run 41 read. The half's per-seat lever: 75 / 66 planted approvals a run (the hand's 27, the careful's 31), confirmed 13.8 / 10.4, pooled F̂^A 0.19 / 0.16 against the careful's 0.056, m^A 8.3 / 5.6 and π^A 0.17 / 0.11 by the end (run 41's m^A 7.6 / 4.5), about 5 bad plants in 100 at the audit seat; the leak 0.0055 / 0.0040 per vested task (0.022 / 0.016 per bad delivery; the hand's 0.0038, the careful's 0.0018); the system's overhead a task 15.5 / 15.1 (the hand's 15.8, the careful's 14.9), the customers' 13.7 / 13.5 (the hand's 14.4); the market at the careful's — P_t 20.2 / 18.9 (the hand's 19.4), tasks per 1,000 33.1 / 33.6 (the hand's 31.7, the careful's 33.5), the real lifetime 19,264 / 20,146 (the hand's 15,768, the careful's 20,380), the nominal surplus 17,824 / 17,463 (the careful's 17,672); the bad plants approved 42 / 41 a run at the careful's 39 (the hand's 62 — the non-learning half is the hand's); the suspensions 9.5 / 9.4 (the careful's 9.4); the state's seats 0.28 per real root (the careful's 0.27; 38–41% of the member-periods panel-ineligible against the careful's 35%); the rulings sought 90 / 97; the false penalties 24.8 / 23.0 at the initial seat, 0.35 / 0.30 at the audit seat, 12.6 / 14.5 at a panel.

**What the three decide, against the predictions.** (1) The π_t pin at the floor reads the state run 42's live dial was in, and nothing returns: on own money the capture of run 36 is not there to return — it was the shared purse's (§72). (2) The pin at the cap closes the audit seat to a fifth — the rate is a lever from above on own money where on the shared purse it was none — at a system overhead a task two and a half times the floor's and a market that pays the plants' price in P_t and tasks per credit; across the four cells the seat's blind rate follows the truth-checked bad share at the seat, §2's arithmetic on the learned populations. (3) The half's dose-response holds on own money, the audit seat 0.34 / 0.24 against all's 0.54 / 0.41. Evaluations under fixed rules; nothing here asks for a rule. The channel splits follow in an addendum when the readers finish; 2.9.0 §8.7's m^A-at-1 cell is queued and will be read the same way.

**Addendum to §88 (the channel splits; November 10, 2026; `2.9.0-s87-own/fa-channels-{pifloor,picap,half}-290-own.md` by `2.9.0-s82/fa_channels.py` at 763a7d5be, the chains' round-10 models on seeds 21–40, the cell's hand beside):** the reader's standing false approval is a real root vested unfulfilled with the state's own deliveries excluded, so its totals sit a few tenths under the finals' (the half's hand 6.9 against 7.2). **At the floor** the split is run 42's to the decimal: of the direct's 17.1 / the head alone's 9.2 a run, 14.4 / 7.2 were confirmed at the audit seat by a member (the cell's hand 4.2), 0.5 / 0.7 by the state (the hand 4.3), 2.0 / 1.0 never audited at a mean odds-at-verdict of 0.59 / 0.54 (the hand none); paired, the member-confirmed audit +10.2 ± 1.0 / +3.0 ± 0.9, the state's seats −3.9 ± 0.5 / −3.7 ± 0.5, the never-audited +2.0 ± 0.4 / +1.0 ± 0.3 (run 42 at the consolidated defaults: member-confirmed +9.9 / +5.1 over the hand, the never-audited 2.0 / 0.9). The audits bought a run: 1,089 / 983 with a member sitting and 876 / 936 with the state (the hand 730 / 924) — the state holds nearly half the audit layer here, as the 76–77% panel-ineligible member-periods say. **At the cap** the excess over the hand splits between the seat and the roll: of 9.1 / 8.9, 6.0 / 4.8 member-confirmed (the hand 1.9), 0.1 / 0.2 the state's (the hand 0.8), 2.9 / 3.8 never audited (the hand 0.5) at odds 0.54 / 0.52 — paired, +4.0 ± 0.8 / +2.9 ± 0.9 member-confirmed, +2.4 ± 0.4 / +3.3 ± 0.6 never audited, −0.8 ± 0.2 / −0.6 ± 0.2 the state. The never-audited share of the learned population's standing false approvals is 0.32 / 0.43 at the cap against 0.12 / 0.11 at the floor (the hand's 0.13 / 0.00): with the seat closed to a fifth, what remains is as much the roll's as the seat's — a bad approval by a population whose first review is blind 0.008 / 0.003 is mostly a caring member's own error, and a caring member's odds sit at the roll's floor 0.5 m_t on a long stood streak, so the roll misses half of them where the hand's stampers are pinned at 1 and its careful members carry shorter streaks (the hand's never-audited at odds 0.74). The audits bought: 1,553 / 1,520 with a member and 152 / 150 with the state (the hand 1,550 / 220) — the state is out of the audit layer at the cap (panel-ineligible 25–28%). **The half**: of 10.8 / 7.9 (the hand 6.9), 8.1 / 5.5 member-confirmed (the hand 3.3), 1.9 / 1.9 the state's (the hand 2.9), 0.8 / 0.4 audited, rejected and vested (the hand 0.8), 0.1 / 0.1 never audited; paired, the member-confirmed audit +4.8 ± 0.8 / +2.2 ± 0.7, the state −0.9 ± 0.4 / −0.9 ± 0.5, the never-audited +0.1 ± 0.1 — the whole excess the member-confirmed audit, as run 41 read it at the old defaults (+8.5 ± 0.8 of +8.6 ± 1.2), the never-audited channel closed by the defaults as §73's read said it would (the audits bought 1,381 / 1,384 members, 579 / 596 the state; the hand 1,277 / 616). **Across the three cells** the state's seats carry none of the excess anywhere (−0.6 to −3.9 against the hand in every cell), the member-confirmed audit carries it at the floor and the half, and at the cap what is left is half the seat's and half the roll's; no dispute or appeal channel exists in these cells (disputes off, the paid appeals 0.0 in every row). Nothing here moves §88's reading.

**Addendum 2 to §88 (the dial's own path by period in run 42 and the liar chain — the reviewer's question of November 10; `2.9.0-s82/dial_path.py` → `dial-path-{run42,liars}.md`, the policies of record re-run on seeds 21–40 with the engine's period records read from the public view, the hand beside):** the question was whether "run 42 to the decimal" at the floor pin means m^A never left 1 in run 42. It does not: the floor pin pins π_t, the dial's plant rate, and m^A — the per-seat multiplier on the planted approvals — is live in both cells and climbs in both. (i) **A run starts its dial at the floor** by construction: m^A 1 and π^A = π^A_0 0.02 at genesis, nothing carried between runs. (ii) **Run 42's dial is fed and climbs.** The planted approvals adjudicated at a member's audit seat in a closed period: 0.2, 2.5, 5.1, 6.8, 13.6 under the direct (0.2, 2.4, 4.7, 6.5, 12.7 under the head alone) — rising with the base (the members' audits of real approvals 34 → 299 a period as the records determine and the members take the seats) and with π^A itself — so the pool of ten is reached at the third close in 3 seeds and at the fourth in all 20, reads F̂^A 0.37–0.59 against F* 0.10 and steps the full ×2 at every close from there: m^A 1 → 1 → 1.15 → 2.30 → 4.37 (the direct, the mean over 20 seeds; 20 of 20 left 1, the first step at the third or fourth close, the median the fourth), 1 → 1 → 1.15 → 2.10 → 3.74 (the head alone; 19 of 20), 4.77 / 3.81 at the run's end (3.3–8.0), π^A 0.095 / 0.076; readings taken 2.2 / 1.9 a run of five closes. **A run of five periods feeds two steps, not none**: the cells of record read 2.9.0 with its dial a quarter of the way to its cap (π^A_max 0.40 is m^A 20 — four to five doublings past the first reading, six to seven closes a 1,000-tick run does not have), and the audit seat's half-stamp in run 42 is read with π^A at 0.02 for the first three periods and 0.05–0.10 for the last two: the floor's reading for most of the run, the dial's own climb only begun. (iii) **The hand's dial stays at 1** (m^A 1.13 at the end; 3 of 20 seeds stepping once, late): its periods close on the initial-review count, twelve a run, with 2.2 adjudications each — the pool reaches ten at the sixth close and reads F̂^A 0.03–0.09, under F* — the dial reading a careful seat and leaving its multiplier where it is. (iv) **The liar chain's dial is never fed**: zero planted approvals adjudicated at a member's audit seat in every period, under both learners and the hand — every planted approval disputed first (§68's fact (iii)) — so m^A is 1.00 on every seed from genesis to the end: where disputes are on, π^A has been read at the floor only, and could only be. The step rule as it stands, in the dishonest basin's first half: ten pooled adjudications at π^A_0 0.02 on a base of 130–300 member audits a period is 2.5–6 a period, so the first reading lands at the fourth close. **The cheapest direct read of the dial's climb, named:** the frozen policies of record in a 2,000-tick run (`run_q --ticks 2000`: ten closes, no code — the step is per close and the policy's fields are within-period), the dial reader beside it; a start-value knob (m^A at genesis) would be a `Params` field with the suite behind it and would read the seat at a multiplier the dial had not earned, where the longer run reads it earning one. The dial reader at 2,000 ticks on run 42's two policies and the hand is running (`dial-path-run42-2000.md`); the full evaluation at 2,000 ticks on the reviewer's word.

## 89. §8.5 on the corrected engine, the first half (November 10, 2026; `results/redteam/Q3/line-v4/eval-{290cr,270cr}/`, `Q4/line-v4b/eval-{290cr,270cr}/` made by `ablation-A1/queue.sh` at 763a7d5be, the pairing `2.9.0-s85-own/pair_evals.py` → `s85-paired.md`; the rc's 2.8.0 control and Q1's line queued by `2.9.0-s85-own/launch.sh`, to follow in an addendum): nothing moves for the deliverer; the pair's stamping reviewer keeps its seats and its lifetime under 2.9.0 against 2.7.0 where under 2.8.0 on the old engine it lost a fifth — the CEM's reviewer beside loses three quarters of its audit seats

**The construction.** The rc's §8.5 asks for Q4's pair and Q1's line under 2.9.0 against 2.8.0, expecting the audit-stamping reviewer to lose its panels and be poorer and the careful unchanged. What the queue ran first, beside the ablation chains, is §58's construction on the corrected engine: Q3's and Q4's trained policies of record (`Q3/line-v4/models/wm-online-10.pt`, `Q4/line-v4b/models/wm-online-10.pt`; the head alone the line's planner in both, the CEM beside) evaluated on seeds 21–40 under 2.9.0 and under `--rules 2.7.0`, every other flag the line's, on the engine at 763a7d5be with the consolidated roll defaults (the floor 0.5, the window 20) and the corrected episode — paired by seed. The rc's own control (`--rules 2.8.0`) and Q1's line of record (`Q1/rearrive-280/models/wm-online-10.pt`, §60's chain, under 2.9.0 and under 2.8.0) are queued one process at a time behind this read and land in an addendum. These are lone-learner evaluations: the first review's blind rate stands beside the audit seat's in every row below, and the population's standing false approvals a run beside the slot's; the leak and the system's overhead a task are the driver's population columns, which `run_q`'s lone-learner evaluation does not carry.

A deliverer's cases are not plants, and the planted approval and the planted lie are review-side instruments; the one way 2.9.0 reaches a deliverer is through the reviewers who judge it, and run 24's population judges a bad delivery as before.

### Q4 — the pair (the deliverer D and its reviewer R, blind assignment, no re-arrival)

*Read.* The line's planner does not reproduce §58's loss. Under 2.8.0 on the engine of October 26 at the old roll defaults the same policy lost −1,012 ± 332, its audit seats 282 → 150 and its suspensions 0.15 → 0.40; here under 2.9.0 against 2.7.0 on the corrected engine it reads −65 ± 323, its audit seats 275 → 202 with 22 of them plants and 9 of those stamped (the plant and real rates alike at 0.41 / 0.40, as in §58 — it cannot tell them apart), its panels unchanged in number with 18 plants among them, and its suspensions 0.30 → 0.15; R's own real wealth 3,009 → 2,900, D's 1,402 → 1,446. The policy is the same file and the world is not: on the corrected engine at the consolidated defaults this reviewer stamps the first seat 0.09 / 0.08 under 2.7.0 and 0.07 / 0.08 under 2.9.0 (plant / real) where §58 read 0.22 / 0.16, so fewer of its first-review stamps fall on plants, its duty record holds, and the plants at its later seats cost it nine stamps a life and a few seats. The rc's expected reading — the audit-stamping reviewer loses its panels and is poorer — holds instead for the CEM beside: −695 ± 282 (2.5 SE), its reviews 630 → 447, its audit seats 211 → 57 and its panels 38 → 15, its suspensions 0.35 → 0.90 — §58's mechanism (a plant failed at a later seat moves the plant record that governs duty eligibility, 1.2.0 §11.2) at a reviewer whose first seat is blind 0.30 on plants and rejects blind 0.09. The careful pair +92 ± 167 (its reviewer's real audit seats displaced by 38 plants' and its panels by 34, as §56 read, and no suspension); the colluding pair destroyed under both rules; the pair's cases never meet (the blind assignment); no learner colludes. The population's standing false approvals around the head alone's reviewer 14.1 → 10.2: a lone later-seat stamper's confirmations are fewer where the seats carry plants.

**For the ruling.** Evaluations under fixed rules; nothing proposed. Two readings for the record: (i) a stale policy is a reading of the world it meets, and this one's first-review stamp rate on the corrected engine is a third of what it was on October 26 — the pairing across the rule is clean (the same engine, the same defaults on both sides), the pairing against §58's figures is not; (ii) the rc's prediction is read against 2.7.0 here and will be read against 2.8.0 in the addendum, with Q1's line; the deliverer's and the pair's reads do not depend on which control, since the planted approval and the planted lie reach a lone reviewer only at seats §58 already priced.

**Addendum to §89 (the supersession's cause named; the reviewer's ask of November 10; `results/redteam/Q4/line-v4b/eval-{280cr,270cr}-olddefaults/` by `2.9.0-s85-own/olddefaults.sh`, the head alone on seeds 21–40):** two corrections of this section first. (a) §58's first-seat figure for the line's planner, 0.22 / 0.16, is the *per-seed mean* of the ratio stamped / seen (a reviewer that saw few plants in a seed and stamped some weighs as much as one that saw many); the pooled rate the seat table prints, and the one §89 quoted for the corrected engine (0.07 / 0.08), was 0.100 / 0.098 on October 26 — so §89's "a third of what it was" compared two definitions. On one definition each: pooled, the first seat 0.100 / 0.098 under 2.8.0 on the old engine → 0.065 / 0.077 under 2.9.0 on the corrected one; per seed, 0.221 / 0.163 → 0.083 / 0.083. Both read a fall, of a third and of three fifths; the pooled is the rate of record. (b) The cause. The policy is the same file in every cell; between October 26 and this read the engine took the eighth conformance item and the rulings after runs 38–39 (2.9.0's own, not the 2.7.0 arm's), the public view's masking (nothing the 45 fields read), the own-name attribution (training only), the emission's correctly rounded power (the episode's draws, noise — §77) and **the roll's defaults moved under §19 (the economy reviewer's fourth ruling, November 6, 2026; notes §74; `Params.p_floor` 0.5 and `p_halve_every` 20 from 0.2 and 5)**. The last is the one that reaches a lone reviewer's observation: it reads its own applied odds, and under the old defaults they halved every five stood reviews down to 0.2 m_t, under the new they halve every twenty down to 0.5 m_t. The odds bands say where it sat: on the old engine 7,650 of its 13,315 decisions a cell (all seats) were at applied odds under 0.7 and it gave a blind verdict at 18–20% of them against 15% at odds 1; on the corrected engine at the consolidated defaults 15,975 of 16,142 are at odds 1 (the halving seldom reached within a stood streak of twenty, every overturn resetting it) and it gives a blind verdict at 15.3% of them — the same rate it always had at odds 1. The direct check: the same policy under 2.8.0 and 2.7.0 on the corrected engine at the *old* defaults (`--p-floor 0.2 --p-halve-every 5`), seeds 21–40 paired — the pair −673 ± 318 real (§58's −1,012 ± 331; the 2.8.0 arm +198 ± 309 against the old engine's, noise), the first seat 0.209 / 0.140 per seed and 0.116 / 0.116 pooled (§58's 0.221 / 0.163 and 0.100 / 0.098), the odds bands populated as then (0.24–0.29 blind under odds 0.7), R suspended 0.30 (0.40), its audit seats 313 plant + 2,954 real over the twenty seeds (§58's 288 + 2,722). §58's reading returns with the old defaults and goes with the new: **the correction that took the stamp away is the roll's defaults, not a correction of the plant's path** — a reviewer that learned to stamp where its odds had decayed has nowhere to do it when the odds no longer decay within its reach. The supersession stands as stated: §58 is the old engine's read at the old defaults; under 2.9.0 at the consolidated defaults the plants at a lone stamper's later seats cost it nothing outside noise because its first seat no longer feeds its duty record. The control of record is 2.8.0 when `eval-280cr` lands (in flight behind Q3's and Q1's); the 2.7.0 comparison stands as the first read.

**Addendum 2 to §89 (the rc's control of record, 2.8.0, and Q1's line; November 10, 2026; `Q4/line-v4b/eval-280cr/`, `Q3/line-v4/eval-280cr/`, `Q1/rearrive-280/eval-{290cr,280cr}/` by `2.9.0-s85-own/launch.sh`, the pairing `s85-paired-280.md`):** the same policy files, the corrected engine at the consolidated defaults, 2.9.0 against `--rules 2.8.0` on seeds 21–40 paired. **Q4, the pair:** the line's planner (the head alone) +254 ± 275 real (its audit seats 188 → 202 a life with 17 → 22 plants among them, 7 → 9 stamped; its panels 36 → 50 with 6 → 18 plants; R suspended 0.05 → 0.15; R's real wealth 2,640 → 2,900, D's 1,452 → 1,446); the CEM beside −422 ± 320 (its audit seats 164 → 57, its panels 32 → 15, its suspensions 0.65 → 0.90, R's real 2,254 → 1,523 — §58's mechanism, at 1.3 SE against this control and 2.5 SE against 2.7.0's); the careful pair +140 ± 217 (its audit seats 351 → 368, its panels 69 → 94 with 13 → 34 plants, no suspension); the colluding pair destroyed under both (−76 ± 57). The rc's expected reading — "the audit-stamping reviewer loses its panels and is poorer; the careful unchanged" — holds for the CEM's reviewer and not for the line's planner against 2.8.0 as against 2.7.0; the careful is unchanged against both. **Q3, the deliverer:** the head alone +189 ± 87 (2.2 SE; the careful +76 ± 125, the CEM +28 ± 121, the zero-effort deliverer 50 → 9 real, −41 ± 40) — the one movement against 2.8.0 is the line's own, with its bad deliveries 0 → 0.05 suspensions and nothing at its seats, and reads as the plants' price in the run's market (P_t) rather than anything at a deliverer's seat; against 2.7.0 it was +68 ± 104. **Q1, the line (re-arrival):** no win on either rule, as §60 read on the old engine — the CEM −99 ± 61 and the head alone −55 ± 101 against 2.8.0, both under the careful's 1,720 by 180–225 under 2.9.0 (1,495 / 1,539); the first review blind 0.06 / 0.12 and 0.09 / 0.10 (plant / real), the audit seat 0.32 / 0.32 and 0.38 / 0.37, the panel 0.26 / 0.16 and 0.36 / 0.14; the returns 0.05 / 0.30 a run (1.05 / 1.30 identities, 2 / 15 real of floats minted), the harvester's ceiling 425 real (510 of floats a life over 11.2 identities, 21 suspensions; §60's 505) against the careful's 1,720, the leaver 1,584 — 2.3.0's finding stays closed by 2.7.0 under 2.9.0 as under 2.8.0. The 2.8.0 control is the one of record for the rc's item 5; the 2.7.0 read (§89) stands as the first. Evaluations under fixed rules; nothing proposed.

(The `all` rows, +0.058 / +0.136 on the policies of record, are composition across the seats — the seat's own fields name the seat and the plant share differs by seat — and are not read; §57 read them the same way.)

**Read.** (i) **The audit seat is blind to the probe in both chains and both sets** — −0.0024 ± 0.0038 / +0.0005 ± 0.0020 on the policies of record, −0.0014 / −0.0009 on the ε sets, the case's fields alone at chance (0.51; 0.38 on 140 plants), no carrier above three ten-thousandths: §57's reading (−0.002 / +0.003 on the shared purse at the old defaults) on own money at the consolidated ones. In the liar chain the seat's plant share is 0.013 / 0.022 — §68's fact (iii) in the probe's denominator: where disputes are on every planted approval is disputed first and the audit seat audits approvals of good work. (ii) **The initial seat sits at its floor**: +0.0036 ± 0.0016 / +0.0021 ± 0.0013 against the ε floors +0.0012 ± 0.0010 / +0.0020 ± 0.0007 — the residual 0.000–0.002 (§57's 0.001–0.003, §53's 0.010 on the 2.7.0 population before the raise item), carried by `seen_beta_rel` +0.0006 and `rv_gap` +0.0004 — the trailing window's lag on the plant's ceiling and the seating gap at their §53 figures, under a thousandth each. (iii) **The panel's gap is the question's base rate, whole**: +0.0378 ± 0.0028 / +0.0490 ± 0.0096, of which the question one-hot carries +0.0387 / +0.0494 and the next field +0.001 / +0.006; within a question nothing — the dispute seat −0.0046 ± 0.0033 / −0.0045 ± 0.0013 (no field adds a ten-thousandth), the appeal seat −0.0010 ± 0.0014 in the careful cell. The base rate itself has moved with the cell: in the careful cell (disputes off) a dispute question is a planted lying dispute of a good plant every time (65 of 65 — the real requesters file none), so the question names the plant; in the liar cell the dispute seat is a plant 0.34 / 0.39 of the time against §57's 0.56 on 2.8.0's disputes-on population, because 2.9.0's real requesters dispute truthfully and lie at 0.1 beside the dial's planted disputes — the panel's gap +0.185 → +0.049 is that share's movement, not the seat's. (iv) **One reading above noise, on the ε set alone**: the appeal question in the liar chain reads +0.0330 ± 0.0064 on the ε set, carried by `rv_gap` (+0.0328) — the interval from the case's submission to this seat's assignment, which at a panel sums the first seat's wait, the verdict's, the filing delay and the panel wait — and +0.0084 ± 0.0047 under the policy of record (`rv_gap` +0.0122), 1.8 SE; the chain's own stage table (the direct's final) reads the filing delay 0.00 / 0.00 and the panel wait 0.30 / 0.28 real / plant alike, so what the ε set's interval carries sits before the filing, in the sum of the stages a planted lie's case takes to its N against a real appealed case's. The raw AUC at the seat is 0.58 / 0.62, the composition level §70 closed the eighth item at (the hand's 0.58); the members of record side with a planted lie 2% (§73). A reading of the ε set's population, not a tell the population of record uses; the stage-wise interval by case kind at the appeal seat is the diagnostic if the reviewer wants it run — the stages are on record by kind already, their sum to the panel seat is not.

**Against §57.** The audit seat blind then and now; the initial seat's residual above its floor 0.001–0.003 then, 0.000–0.002 now; the panel's gap +0.046 → +0.038 with disputes off and +0.185 → +0.049 with disputes on, the question's base rate in every cell. Evaluations under fixed rules; nothing proposed.
